<div style="clear: left;"></div>
<h2><span class="mw-headline" id="Descriptions">Descriptions</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/wiki/Horde?action=edit&amp;section=1" title="Edit section: Descriptions"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h2>
<div style="clear:left;"></div><p><span style="display:inline-block; min-width: 32px; text-align:center"><img alt="Stub.png" src="/images/thumb/Stub.png/25px-Stub.png?fdeef6" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="25" height="30" style="vertical-align: middle" data-file-width="44" data-file-height="52" /></span> <span class="greybar" style="font-size: 0.85em; padding: 0 0.2em;"><i>Please add any available information to this section.</i></span></p><div style="clear:left;"></div>
<ul><li><i>The indomitable Horde is driven by unity. They are fervent keepers of freedom and hope, relentlessly opposed to any who threaten these ideals, including the stringent Alliance.</i><sup id="cite&#95;ref-9" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-9"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>9<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup> <i>The Horde fights for honor and freedom, surviving in a world that questions their unity and strength.</i><sup id="cite&#95;ref-10" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-10"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>10<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup></li>
<li><i>The Horde is made up of orcs, forsaken, tauren, trolls, blood elves, and most recently, goblins. Misunderstood and cast aside, these diverse and powerful races strive to overcome their differences and unite as one in order to win freedom for their people and prosper in a land that has come to hate them. In the Horde, action and strength are valued above diplomacy, and its leaders earn respect by the blade, wasting no time with politics. The brutality of the Horde's champions is focused, giving a voice to those who fight for survival.</i><sup id="cite&#95;ref-11" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-11"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>11<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup></li></ul>
<dl><dt>Classic</dt></dl>
<div style="clear:left;"></div><p><span style="display:inline-block; min-width: 32px; text-align:center"><a href="/wiki/World_of_Warcraft" title="World of Warcraft"><img alt="World of Warcraft" src="/images/WoW_Icon_update.png?d4be1e" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="32" height="16" style="vertical-align: middle" data-file-width="32" data-file-height="16" /></a></span> <span class="greybar" style="font-size: 0.85em; padding: 0 0.2em;"><b>This section concerns content related to the original <i><a href="/wiki/World_of_Warcraft" title="World of Warcraft">World of Warcraft</a></i>.</b></span></p><div style="clear:left;"></div>
<ul><li><i>Four races comprise the Horde: the brutal orcs, the shadowy undead, the spiritual tauren, and the quick-witted trolls. Beset by enemies on all sides, these outcasts have forged a union they hope will ensure their mutual survival.</i> (in-game)</li></ul>
<ul><li><i>A coalition of outcasts who have forged a union in the flames of survival, the Horde values action and strength, committed to enduring in an unforgiving land that would see it extinguished.</i><sup id="cite&#95;ref-12" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-12"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>12<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup></li></ul>
<h2><span class="mw-headline" id="History">History</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/wiki/Horde?action=edit&amp;section=2" title="Edit section: History"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h2>
<div class="thumb tright"><div class="thumbinner" style="width:322px;"><a href="/wiki/File:HordeLogo.png" class="image"><img src="/images/thumb/HordeLogo.png/320px-HordeLogo.png?5db34f" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="320" height="405" class="thumbimage" data-file-width="1992" data-file-height="2523" /></a>  <div class="thumbcaption"><div class="magnify"><a href="/wiki/File:HordeLogo.png" class="internal" title="Enlarge"></a></div>The modern Horde symbol.</div></div></div>
<dl><dd><i>See also: <a href="/wiki/Old_Horde" title="Old Horde">Old Horde</a>, <a href="/wiki/Dark_Horde" title="Dark Horde">Dark Horde</a>, <a href="/wiki/Fel_Horde" title="Fel Horde">Fel Horde</a>, <a href="/wiki/Iron_Horde" title="Iron Horde">Iron Horde</a> and <a href="/wiki/True_Horde" title="True Horde">True Horde</a></i></dd></dl>
<p>In the past, the Horde referred to the orcs and their battle thralls from both <a href="/wiki/Draenor" title="Draenor">Draenor</a> and Azeroth, such as trolls and ogres. Following its utter defeat at the end of the <a href="/wiki/Second_War" title="Second War">Second War</a>, the Horde's dark power was broken, allowing Thrall to awaken the dormant spirituality of his people and free them from the <a href="/wiki/Burning_Legion" title="Burning Legion">Burning Legion</a>'s control. 
</p><p>During the events of the <a href="/wiki/Third_War" title="Third War">Third War</a>, Thrall has made lasting bonds with the tauren chief <a href="/wiki/Cairne_Bloodhoof" title="Cairne Bloodhoof">Cairne Bloodhoof</a> and the troll shadow hunter <a href="/wiki/Vol%27jin" title="Vol&#39;jin">Vol'jin</a> of the Darkspear tribe. The ties between the three races are very close, as both share many similar cultural views. With the help of the tauren, both the orcs and the tribe of trolls have established a place for themselves in <a href="/wiki/Kalimdor" title="Kalimdor">Kalimdor</a>. During the <a href="/wiki/Invasion_of_Durotar" title="Invasion of Durotar">invasion of Durotar</a>, <a href="/wiki/Vol%27jin" title="Vol&#39;jin">Vol'jin</a> and <a href="/wiki/Rexxar" title="Rexxar">Rexxar</a> unexpectedly had been presented with an opportunity to bring the <a href="/wiki/Stonemaul_clan" title="Stonemaul clan">Stonemaul clan</a> into the Horde and seized on it.<sup id="cite&#95;ref-13" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-13"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>13<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup>
</p><p>The present Horde is mostly about surviving in a land that has come to hate them. The orcs are hated because, much like the Forsaken, they were manipulated by corrupt forces that channeled their aggression on unsuspecting foes, thus leaving behind a legacy of brutality and domination. In fact, many races of the Horde have a history of being unscrupulous. Though redeemed and trying to make amends or simply trying to survive, they are not forgiven nor trusted by many members of the <a href="/wiki/Alliance" title="Alliance">Alliance</a> who believe them to be their old selves still.<sup id="cite&#95;ref-14" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-14"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>14<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup> The <a href="/wiki/Troll" title="Troll">trolls</a>, <a href="/wiki/Tauren" title="Tauren">tauren</a> and their other allies are the ones who understood them, and so they are hated for that.
</p>
<h3><span class="mw-headline" id="World_of_Warcraft">World of Warcraft</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/wiki/Horde?action=edit&amp;section=3" title="Edit section: World of Warcraft"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h3>
<div style="clear:left;"></div><p><span style="display:inline-block; min-width: 32px; text-align:center"><a href="/wiki/World_of_Warcraft" title="World of Warcraft"><img alt="World of Warcraft" src="/images/WoW_Icon_update.png?d4be1e" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="32" height="16" style="vertical-align: middle" data-file-width="32" data-file-height="16" /></a></span> <span class="greybar" style="font-size: 0.85em; padding: 0 0.2em;"><b>This section concerns content related to the original <i><a href="/wiki/World_of_Warcraft" title="World of Warcraft">World of Warcraft</a></i>.</b></span></p><div style="clear:left;"></div>
<p>After carving out a functional empire of their own and with growing numbers by the day, the <a href="/wiki/Forsaken" title="Forsaken">Forsaken</a> knew that the next step to realizing their vengeance upon <a href="/wiki/Arthas_Menethil" title="Arthas Menethil">Arthas Menethil</a> was to secure their place in the world. In time, their leader <a href="/wiki/Sylvanas_Windrunner" title="Sylvanas Windrunner">Sylvanas Windrunner</a> looked for allies, and to that end she sent ambassadors to both the <a href="/wiki/Alliance" title="Alliance">Alliance</a> and the Horde. While emissaries to <a href="/wiki/Stormwind_City" title="Stormwind City">Stormwind City</a> never returned, <a href="/wiki/Hamuul_Runetotem" title="Hamuul Runetotem">Archdruid Hamuul Runetotem</a> believed that Forsaken could be redeemed and spiritually revived. Thus he brought the Forsaken ambassadors to meet with <a href="/wiki/Cairne_Bloodhoof" title="Cairne Bloodhoof">Cairne Bloodhoof</a>, high chieftain of the <a href="/wiki/Tauren" title="Tauren">tauren</a> tribes, and Cairne agreed that the undead should be given a chance to thrive.<sup id="cite&#95;ref-15" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-15"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>15<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup> Thus, the tauren convinced Warchief Thrall, despite his misgivings, to forge an alliance of convenience between the Forsaken and the Horde. This alliance of convenience has granted the Horde a better footing in the mostly <a href="/wiki/Alliance" title="Alliance">Alliance</a> controlled <a href="/wiki/Eastern_Kingdoms" title="Eastern Kingdoms">Eastern Kingdoms</a>, and in turn, with the Horde's protection, and even the assistance of the <a href="/wiki/Argent_Dawn" title="Argent Dawn">Argent Dawn</a>,<sup id="cite&#95;ref-16" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-16"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>16<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup> the Forsaken were able to keep their holdings in Lordaeron.
</p><p>Meanwhile, word of the new Horde and its noble goals soon reached the <a href="/wiki/Revantusk_tribe" title="Revantusk tribe">Revantusk tribe</a>, who immediately dispatched its speediest runners to make contact with the orcs. Though initially wary, Thrall came to see that the Revantusk trolls were unique among forest trolls in a fundamental way. The Revantusk tribe had never had much in the way of the fighters or resources, and so it had instead decided to specialize in the trickier art of negotiation. By the time they spoke with Thrall, the Revantusks had acquired a reputation for trustworthiness that was well deserved. The trolls were impressed by Thrall and his dream. Even so, they remembered the recklessness and corruption of the first Horde. Thus, the Revantusks declined to rejoin the Horde, but they did agree to a pact of friendship and mutual assistance.<sup id="cite&#95;ref-17" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-17"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>17<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup>
</p><p>In time, old animosity between the Horde and Alliance arose once more which resulted in open conflict on several <a href="/wiki/Battleground" title="Battleground">battlefields</a>, keeping relations between the Horde and Alliance in a state of cold war.
</p><p>Despite the political quarreling between the Horde and Alliance, Thrall does wish to establish true peace between the two factions. However, the Alliance still remembers the <a href="/wiki/Old_Horde" title="Old Horde">Old Horde</a>'s association with the <a href="/wiki/Burning_Legion" title="Burning Legion">Burning Legion</a>. To differentiate his New Horde from its predecessor's dark legacy, Thrall sanctioned <a href="/wiki/Keldran" title="Keldran">Keldran</a>'s campaign against the <a href="/wiki/Burning_Blade_clan" title="Burning Blade clan">Burning Blade</a><sup id="cite&#95;ref-18" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-18"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>18<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup><sup id="cite&#95;ref-19" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-19"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>19<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup><sup id="cite&#95;ref-20" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-20"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>20<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup> and the demon infestation in <a href="/wiki/Desolace" title="Desolace">Desolace</a>.<sup id="cite&#95;ref-21" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-21"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>21<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup> With such a grand display of hostility against the Burning Legion's agents, Keldran and Thrall hoped the Alliance would see the new Horde's sincerity and prevent a war caused by misunderstandings.<sup id="cite&#95;ref-22" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-22"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>22<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup> Thrall also sought to send a strike team to liberate <a href="/wiki/Moira_Thaurissan" title="Moira Thaurissan">Moira Thaurissan</a> from the Dark Irons to strengthen relations in the <a href="/wiki/Eastern_Kingdoms" title="Eastern Kingdoms">Eastern Kingdoms</a>,<sup id="cite&#95;ref-23" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-23"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>23<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup> though it was ultimately Alliance forces who entered the Blackrock Depths and killed <a href="/wiki/Dagran_Thaurissan" title="Dagran Thaurissan">Dagran Thaurissan</a>, the Emperor of the Dark Irons.<sup id="cite&#95;ref-24" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-24"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>24<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup>
</p><p>When Moira covertly sent her spies with word about <a href="/wiki/Dark_Horde" title="Dark Horde">Dark Horde</a>'s alliance with the <a href="/wiki/Black_dragonflight" title="Black dragonflight">black dragonflight</a>, it reached Thrall who assembled the mightiest of his Horde warriors to claim the heads of <a href="/wiki/Dal%27Rend" class="mw-redirect" title="Dal&#39;Rend">Dal'Rend</a> and of Deathwing's son, <a href="/wiki/Nefarian" title="Nefarian">Nefarian</a>.<sup id="cite&#95;ref-25" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-25"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>25<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup><sup id="cite&#95;ref-26" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-26"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>26<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup> The Horde later received word from the <a href="/wiki/Zandalari" class="mw-redirect" title="Zandalari">Zandalari</a> that the <a href="/wiki/Atal%27ai" class="mw-redirect" title="Atal&#39;ai">Atal'ai</a> and their leader, <a href="/wiki/Jin%27do_the_Hexxer" title="Jin&#39;do the Hexxer">Jin'do the Hexxer</a>, sought to summon <a href="/wiki/Hakkar_the_Soulflayer" title="Hakkar the Soulflayer">Hakkar the Soulflayer</a>. Thus a large Horde strike force invaded <a href="/wiki/Zul%27Gurub" title="Zul&#39;Gurub">Zul'Gurub</a>, and though they killed the priests and Jin'do they were too late to stop Hakkar's summoning. Manifesting as a force of death and insanity, Hakkar poisoned the blood of the Horde's champions and tried to drag their thoughts into absolute madness. Yet it was Hakkar's own power that proved to be his undoing. In desperation, the Horde forces allowed themselves to be poisoned, and then let Hakkar feed on their corrupted blood. In his frenzied state, with every thought filled with the urge to consume blood, Hakkar paid little heed to the tactics of his seemingly harmless enemies. He was unaware of the poison entering his veins until it was too late, and in the end Hakkar was consumed by his own dark magic.<sup id="cite&#95;ref-27" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-27"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>27<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup>
</p><p>At the time of the <a href="/wiki/Ahn%27Qiraj_War" title="Ahn&#39;Qiraj War">Ahn'Qiraj War</a>, Thrall quickly came to an unprecedented agreement with <a href="/wiki/Bolvar_Fordragon" title="Bolvar Fordragon">Regent Lord Bolvar Fordragon</a>. Both the Horde and the Alliance would combine the might of their armies, as the <a href="/wiki/Might_of_Kalimdor" title="Might of Kalimdor">Might of Kalimdor</a>, in order to strike back against the threat of the <a href="/wiki/Old_God" title="Old God">Old God</a> <a href="/wiki/C%27Thun" title="C&#39;Thun">C'Thun</a> and its <a href="/wiki/Qiraji" title="Qiraji">qiraji</a> at <a href="/wiki/Ahn%27Qiraj" title="Ahn&#39;Qiraj">Ahn'Qiraj</a>.<sup id="cite&#95;ref-28" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-28"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>28<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup><sup id="cite&#95;ref-29" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-29"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>29<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup>
</p>
<h3><span class="mw-headline" id="The_Burning_Crusade">The Burning Crusade</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/wiki/Horde?action=edit&amp;section=4" title="Edit section: The Burning Crusade"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h3>
<div style="clear:left;"></div><p><span style="display:inline-block; min-width: 32px; text-align:center"><a href="/wiki/World_of_Warcraft:_The_Burning_Crusade" title="Burning Crusade"><img alt="Burning Crusade" src="/images/Bc_icon.gif?6fe702" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="30" height="14" style="vertical-align: middle" data-file-width="30" data-file-height="14" /></a></span> <span class="greybar" style="font-size: 0.85em; padding: 0 0.2em;"><b>This section concerns content related to <i><a href="/wiki/World_of_Warcraft:_The_Burning_Crusade" title="World of Warcraft: The Burning Crusade">The Burning Crusade</a></i>.</b></span></p><div style="clear:left;"></div>
<p>The <a href="/wiki/Blood_elves" class="mw-redirect" title="Blood elves">blood elves</a> needed allies, and they would no longer find them among the humans, dwarves, gnomes, and night elves. Kael'thas' alliance with <a href="/wiki/Lady_Vashj" class="mw-redirect" title="Lady Vashj">Lady Vashj</a> and <a href="/wiki/Illidan_Stormrage" title="Illidan Stormrage">Illidan Stormrage</a> had soured relations between Quel'Thalas and the Alliance, and the kingdom's secession during Anasterian's rule still left bitter memories; the Alliance had no interest in fighting for Quel'Thalas,<sup id="cite&#95;ref-WCE&#95;BE&#95;30-0" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-WCE_BE-30"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>30<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup> to the point that their <a href="/wiki/Prospector_Anvilward" title="Prospector Anvilward">ambassador</a> was actively working with several <a href="/wiki/Sentinel_spies" title="Sentinel spies">night elven spies</a> to sabotage the blood elves' arcane sanctums.
</p><p>The blood elves were offered support from an unlikely source: the <a href="/wiki/Forsaken" title="Forsaken">Forsaken</a>, under the former ranger-general <a href="/wiki/Sylvanas_Windrunner" title="Sylvanas Windrunner">Sylvanas Windrunner</a>. Though initially skeptical of a trick, the blood elves allowed the Forsaken to assist them in terms of manpower in the <a href="/wiki/Ghostlands" title="Ghostlands">Ghostlands</a>. Through the Forsaken, Lor'themar was introduced to the wider Horde. Rumors circulated that some lingering part of her still sympathized with Quel'Thalas and its hardships. Other stories hinted that Sylvanas had ulterior motives. Whatever the truth, she arranged for Warchief <a href="/wiki/Thrall" title="Thrall">Thrall</a> and High Chieftain <a href="/wiki/Cairne_Bloodhoof" title="Cairne Bloodhoof">Cairne Bloodhoof</a> to convene with Lor'themar and discuss such a possibility. Despite the elves' history of bitter warfare with the orcs, Lor'themar was receptive to the idea. He knew this Horde was different than the one that had ravaged Quel'Thalas in years past. He was also painfully aware that time was running out for his kingdom and his prince. The Horde's leaders also saw great promise in Lor'themar's people and considered it an act of honor to offer another race facing extinction their support. Thrall and Cairne extended the hand of peace, and Lor'themar accepted.<sup id="cite&#95;ref-31" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-31"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>31<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup>
</p><p>During the <a href="/wiki/Invasion_of_Outland" title="Invasion of Outland">Invasion of Outland</a> the orcs came in contact with <a href="/wiki/Mag%27har_(faction)" title="Mag&#39;har (faction)">uncorrupted orcs</a>. While the Mag'har joined the Horde, the Horde warred with the <a href="/wiki/Fel_orc" title="Fel orc">fel orc</a> clans of the <a href="/wiki/Fel_Horde" title="Fel Horde">Fel Horde</a> and ultimately killed <a href="/wiki/Warchief" title="Warchief">Warchief</a> <a href="/wiki/Kargath_Bladefist" title="Kargath Bladefist">Kargath Bladefist</a>.<sup id="cite&#95;ref-32" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-32"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>32<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup>
</p><p>Many blood elves didn't want to believe the rumors that Kael'thas had pledged himself to the Burning Legion. But when they arrived at the Netherstorm, they saw the truth with their own eyes. These blood elves sent word back to Lor'themar Theron and other high-ranking elves in Quel'Thalas, who with a heavy heart came to the consensus that Kael'thas was lost and had to be vanquished. And so, the Horde officially declared war on Kael'thas. Horde champions ultimately stormed <a href="/wiki/Tempest_Keep" title="Tempest Keep">Tempest Keep</a> and defeated the maddened prince.<sup id="cite&#95;ref-33" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-33"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>33<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup>
</p>
<h3><span class="mw-headline" id="Wrath_of_the_Lich_King">Wrath of the Lich King</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/wiki/Horde?action=edit&amp;section=5" title="Edit section: Wrath of the Lich King"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h3>
<div style="clear:left;"></div><p><span style="display:inline-block; min-width: 32px; text-align:center"><a href="/wiki/World_of_Warcraft:_Wrath_of_the_Lich_King" title="Wrath of the Lich King"><img alt="Wrath of the Lich King" src="/images/Wrath-Logo-Small.png?6705fd" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="36" height="19" style="vertical-align: middle" data-file-width="36" data-file-height="19" /></a></span> <span class="greybar" style="font-size: 0.85em; padding: 0 0.2em;"><b>This section concerns content related to <i><a href="/wiki/World_of_Warcraft:_Wrath_of_the_Lich_King" title="World of Warcraft: Wrath of the Lich King">Wrath of the Lich King</a></i>.</b></span></p><div style="clear:left;"></div>
<p>At the beginning of the <a href="/wiki/War_against_the_Lich_King" title="War against the Lich King">war against the Lich King</a>, the <a href="/wiki/Scourge_Invasion" title="Scourge Invasion">Scourge Invasion</a> sent the Alliance and the Horde into a righteous fury, and both factions mobilized their armies to bring war to the <a href="/wiki/Lich_King" title="Lich King">Lich King</a>'s doorstep in <a href="/wiki/Northrend" title="Northrend">Northrend</a>. <a href="/wiki/Warchief" title="Warchief">Warchief</a> <a href="/wiki/Thrall" title="Thrall">Thrall</a> commanded <a href="/wiki/Garrosh_Hellscream" title="Garrosh Hellscream">Garrosh Hellscream</a> and <a href="/wiki/Varok_Saurfang" title="Varok Saurfang">Varok Saurfang</a> to led the <a href="/wiki/Warsong_Offensive" title="Warsong Offensive">Warsong Offensive</a> against the <a href="/wiki/Scourge" title="Scourge">Scourge</a>. Upon arriving to <a href="/wiki/Northrend" title="Northrend">Northrend</a>, the Horde gained the allegiance of the <a href="/wiki/Taunka" title="Taunka">Taunka</a>, kin to the <a href="/wiki/Tauren" title="Tauren">Tauren</a>. As the war progressed, Varok was wary of the resurfacing bloodlust of the orcs, due to its employment in Hellscream's savage but successful tactics.
</p><p>Following the <a href="/wiki/Battle_of_Angrathar_the_Wrathgate" title="Battle of Angrathar the Wrathgate">Battle of Angrathar the Wrathgate</a>, Horde forces mobilized to <a href="/wiki/Battle_for_the_Undercity" title="Battle for the Undercity">reclaim</a> the <a href="/wiki/Undercity" title="Undercity">Undercity</a> from <a href="/wiki/Varimathras" title="Varimathras">Varimathras</a> and his forces. In the aftermath, King <a href="/wiki/Varian_Wrynn" title="Varian Wrynn">Varian Wrynn</a>, who decided to use the opportunity to reclaim Lordaeron for the Alliance, <a href="/wiki/Alliance-Horde_war" title="Alliance-Horde war">declared war</a> on the Horde. Furthermore, the credibility of the Forsaken in the eyes of the wider Horde had also suffered and Thrall dispatched a legion of a legion of <a href="/wiki/Kor%27kron_Guard" class="mw-redirect" title="Kor&#39;kron Guard">Kor'kron Guard</a> led by <span class="raceicon"></span><a href="/wiki/Bragor_Bloodfist" title="Bragor Bloodfist"><span style="color:">Bragor Bloodfist</span></a> to occupy the Undercity, guarding it from threats outside, and from any that may remain within following The Battle for the Undercity.
</p>
<h3><span class="mw-headline" id="Cataclysm">Cataclysm</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/wiki/Horde?action=edit&amp;section=6" title="Edit section: Cataclysm"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h3>
<div style="clear:left;"></div><p><span style="display:inline-block; min-width: 32px; text-align:center"><a href="/wiki/World_of_Warcraft:_Cataclysm" title="Cataclysm"><img alt="Cataclysm" src="/images/Cata-Logo-Small.png?cd16d8" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="39" height="15" style="vertical-align: middle" data-file-width="39" data-file-height="15" /></a></span> <span class="greybar" style="font-size: 0.85em; padding: 0 0.2em;"><b>This section concerns content related to <i><a href="/wiki/World_of_Warcraft:_Cataclysm" title="World of Warcraft: Cataclysm">Cataclysm</a></i>.</b></span></p><div style="clear:left;"></div>
<p>As of the <a href="/wiki/Cataclysm_(event)" title="Cataclysm (event)">Cataclysm</a>, Thrall was forced to step down from Warchief to help deal with the elemental turmoil. In his placed he named <a href="/wiki/Garrosh_Hellscream" title="Garrosh Hellscream">Garrosh Hellscream</a> acting <a href="/wiki/Warchief" title="Warchief">Warchief</a><sup id="cite&#95;ref-34" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-34"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>34<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup> of the Horde. This caused signs of internal strife among the Horde leaders, which saw <a href="/wiki/Cairne_Bloodhoof" title="Cairne Bloodhoof">Cairne Bloodhoof</a> challenge Garrosh to an honorable duel, <a href="/wiki/Vol%27jin" title="Vol&#39;jin">Vol'jin</a> making a veiled death threat against him, while <a href="/wiki/Sylvanas_Windrunner" title="Sylvanas Windrunner">Sylvanas Windrunner</a> came into conflict with him on both an ethical and a tactical level - the use of <a href="/wiki/Val%27kyr" title="Val&#39;kyr">Val'kyr</a> to raise new soldiers, and the <a href="/wiki/Forsaken_Blight" class="mw-redirect" title="Forsaken Blight">Forsaken Blight</a> as a weapon.
</p><p>While the senior leadership clashed, the Horde bolstered its ranks with the <a href="/wiki/Bilgewater_Cartel" title="Bilgewater Cartel">Bilgewater Cartel</a> led by <a href="/wiki/Trade_Prince_Gallywix" class="mw-redirect" title="Trade Prince Gallywix">Trade Prince Gallywix</a> joining after the events of the <a href="/wiki/Lost_Isles" title="Lost Isles">Lost Isles</a>, the Revantusk tribe becoming official members with them openly supporting the Horde, and the <a href="/wiki/Dragonmaw_clan" title="Dragonmaw clan">Dragonmaw clan</a> being brought back into the Horde by Hellscream personally.
</p>
<h3><span class="mw-headline" id="Mists_of_Pandaria">Mists of Pandaria</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/wiki/Horde?action=edit&amp;section=7" title="Edit section: Mists of Pandaria"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h3>
<div style="clear:left;"></div><p><span style="display:inline-block; min-width: 32px; text-align:center"><a href="/wiki/World_of_Warcraft:_Mists_of_Pandaria" title="Mists of Pandaria"><img alt="Mists of Pandaria" src="/images/Mists-Logo-Small.png?f96b23" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="46" height="14" style="vertical-align: middle" data-file-width="46" data-file-height="14" /></a></span> <span class="greybar" style="font-size: 0.85em; padding: 0 0.2em;"><b>This section concerns content related to <i><a href="/wiki/World_of_Warcraft:_Mists_of_Pandaria" title="World of Warcraft: Mists of Pandaria">Mists of Pandaria</a></i>.</b></span></p><div style="clear:left;"></div>
<p>By the time the <a href="/wiki/Mists_of_Pandaria" class="mw-redirect" title="Mists of Pandaria">mists concealing Pandaria dissipated</a>, cracks within the Horde started to emerge. The war had intensified with the surprise mana-bombing of <a href="/wiki/Theramore_Isle" title="Theramore Isle">Theramore Isle</a> on Garrosh's orders, the actions of which Baine Bloodhoof and Vol'jin openly disapproved of.<sup id="cite&#95;ref-ToW&#95;35-0" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-ToW-35"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>35<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup> In response, Garrosh ordered the <a href="/wiki/Kor%27kron" title="Kor&#39;kron">Kor'kron</a> to suppress any potential dissent, going as far as subjugating the Darkspear trolls on their own territory.<sup id="cite&#95;ref-36" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-36"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>36<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup><sup id="cite&#95;ref-37" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-37"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>37<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup> When welcoming <a href="/wiki/Ji_Firepaw" title="Ji Firepaw">Ji Firepaw</a> and the <a href="/wiki/Huojin_Pandaren" title="Huojin Pandaren">Huojin Pandaren</a> into the Horde, Warchief Hellscream makes it clear that the <a href="/wiki/Tushui_Pandaren" title="Tushui Pandaren">Tushui Pandaren</a> that joined the <a href="/wiki/Alliance" title="Alliance">Alliance</a> are now their enemies and that he not tolerate any lingering ties across enemy lines.<sup id="cite&#95;ref-38" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-38"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>38<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup>
</p><p>Horde leaders were weary of Garrosh's attempts to harness the <a href="/wiki/Sha" title="Sha">sha</a> for his own ends,<sup id="cite&#95;ref-39" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-39"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>39<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup> and the blood elves became increasingly discontented with Garrosh treating them as expendable in his attempts to do so.<sup id="cite&#95;ref-40" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-40"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>40<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup><sup id="cite&#95;ref-41" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-41"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>41<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup> The exposure of Sunreaver agents taking orders from Garrosh eventually led to the Sunreavers' expulsion from Dalaran, stoking further recriminations from the blood elf leadership towards Garrosh.<sup id="cite&#95;ref-42" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-42"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>42<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup>
</p><p>After the <a href="/wiki/Lei_Shen" title="Lei Shen">Thunder King</a>'s death the <a href="/wiki/Darkspear_Rebellion" title="Darkspear Rebellion">Darkspear Rebellion</a> began in earnest and Horde leader after Horde leader joined it. When at last even Gallywix and the Bilgewater Cartel joined the Rebellion due to Garrosh refusing to pay them for excavating the <a href="/wiki/Heart_of_Y%27Shaarj" title="Heart of Y&#39;Shaarj">Heart of Y'Shaarj</a>,<sup id="cite&#95;ref-43" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-43"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>43<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup> Garrosh re-organized his orc forces into the <a href="/wiki/True_Horde" title="True Horde">True Horde</a> and, with the power of Y'Shaarj, began a genocidal campaign to conquer Azeroth, subjugate all non-orcish life, and brutally crush all who oppose him. Garrosh and the True Horde were defeated in the ensuing conflict by the Horde rebels and Alliance forces, and ultimately <a href="/wiki/Vol%27jin" title="Vol&#39;jin">Vol'jin</a> was declared Warchief of the Horde.<sup id="cite&#95;ref-44" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-44"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>44<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup>
</p>
<h3><span class="mw-headline" id="Warlords_of_Draenor">Warlords of Draenor</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/wiki/Horde?action=edit&amp;section=8" title="Edit section: Warlords of Draenor"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h3>
<div style="clear:left;"></div><p><span style="display:inline-block; min-width: 32px; text-align:center"><a href="/wiki/World_of_Warcraft:_Warlords_of_Draenor" title="Warlords of Draenor"><img alt="Warlords of Draenor" src="/images/WoD-Logo-Small.png?be5b60" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="46" height="14" style="vertical-align: middle" data-file-width="46" data-file-height="14" /></a></span> <span class="greybar" style="font-size: 0.85em; padding: 0 0.2em;"><b>This section concerns content related to <i><a href="/wiki/World_of_Warcraft:_Warlords_of_Draenor" title="World of Warcraft: Warlords of Draenor">Warlords of Draenor</a></i>.</b></span></p><div style="clear:left;"></div>
<p>Following the events of <a href="/wiki/Siege_of_Orgrimmar" title="Siege of Orgrimmar">Siege of Orgrimmar</a>, <a href="/wiki/Garrosh_Hellscream" title="Garrosh Hellscream">Garrosh Hellscream</a> was imprisoned to be tried in <a href="/wiki/Pandaria" title="Pandaria">Pandaria</a>. Before the trial could conclude, aided by <a href="/wiki/Kairozdormu" title="Kairozdormu">an ally</a> who could manipulate the timelines, Garrosh escaped to <a href="/wiki/Draenor_(alternate_universe)" title="Draenor (alternate universe)">Draenor of the past</a>, and prevented the orcs from ever drinking the blood of <a href="/wiki/Mannoroth" title="Mannoroth">Mannoroth</a>, creating an untainted <a href="/wiki/Iron_Horde" title="Iron Horde">Iron Horde</a> under the leadership of <a href="/wiki/Grommash_Hellscream_(alternate_universe)" title="Grommash Hellscream (alternate universe)">Grommash Hellscream</a>. Under Vol'jin's command, the Horde would engage against the forces of the <a href="/wiki/Iron_Horde" title="Iron Horde">Iron Horde</a> and later the <a href="/wiki/Burning_Legion" title="Burning Legion">Burning Legion</a> on <a href="/wiki/Draenor_(alternate_universe)" title="Draenor (alternate universe)">Draenor</a>.
</p>
<h3><span class="mw-headline" id="Legion">Legion</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/wiki/Horde?action=edit&amp;section=9" title="Edit section: Legion"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h3>
<div style="clear:left;"></div><p><span style="display:inline-block; min-width: 32px; text-align:center"><a href="/wiki/World_of_Warcraft:_Legion" title="Legion"><img alt="Legion" src="/images/Legion-Logo-Small.png?7a5440" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="40" height="15" style="vertical-align: middle" data-file-width="40" data-file-height="15" /></a></span> <span class="greybar" style="font-size: 0.85em; padding: 0 0.2em;"><b>This section concerns content related to <i><a href="/wiki/World_of_Warcraft:_Legion" title="World of Warcraft: Legion">Legion</a></i>.</b></span></p><div style="clear:left;"></div>
<p>During the <a href="/wiki/Battle_for_Broken_Shore" title="Battle for Broken Shore">Battle for Broken Shore</a> the Horde and Alliance worked together to prevent the <a href="/wiki/Burning_Legion" title="Burning Legion">Burning Legion</a> from securing the <a href="/wiki/Broken_Shore" title="Broken Shore">Broken Shore</a> as a staging ground for their invasion.<sup id="cite&#95;ref-45" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-45"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>45<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup> Both armies would be pushed back, <a href="/wiki/Varian" class="mw-redirect" title="Varian">King Varian Wrynn</a> slain, and Vol'jin mortally wounded. In Vol'jin's dying moments he named <a href="/wiki/Sylvanas_Windrunner" title="Sylvanas Windrunner">Sylvanas Windrunner</a> the new Warchief of the Horde and faced with combating the armies of the Burning Legion and the hostility of the Alliance the Horde moved forward to combat the armies of the Burning Legion.
</p><p>The forces of Azeroth ultimately succeed in defeating the Burning Legion during the <a href="/wiki/Third_invasion_of_the_Burning_Legion" title="Third invasion of the Burning Legion">third invasion of the Burning Legion</a>, and the Horde soon after gained the allegiance of the <a href="/wiki/Highmountain_tauren" title="Highmountain tauren">Highmountain tauren tribes</a> of <a href="/wiki/Highmountain" title="Highmountain">Highmountain</a> and the <a href="/wiki/Nightborne" title="Nightborne">nightborne</a> of <a href="/wiki/Suramar" title="Suramar">Suramar</a>.
</p>
<h3><span class="mw-headline" id="Battle_for_Azeroth">Battle for Azeroth</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/wiki/Horde?action=edit&amp;section=10" title="Edit section: Battle for Azeroth"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h3>
<div style="clear:left;"></div><p><span style="display:inline-block; min-width: 32px; text-align:center"><a href="/wiki/World_of_Warcraft:_Battle_for_Azeroth" title="Battle for Azeroth"><img alt="Battle for Azeroth" src="/images/thumb/BattleForAzeroth-Logo-Small.png/40px-BattleForAzeroth-Logo-Small.png?25e36c" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="40" height="15" style="vertical-align: middle" data-file-width="80" data-file-height="29" /></a></span> <span class="greybar" style="font-size: 0.85em; padding: 0 0.2em;"><b>This section concerns content related to <i><a href="/wiki/World_of_Warcraft:_Battle_for_Azeroth" title="World of Warcraft: Battle for Azeroth">Battle for Azeroth</a></i>.</b></span></p><div style="clear:left;"></div>
<p>At <a href="/wiki/Warchief" title="Warchief">Warchief</a> <a href="/wiki/Sylvanas_Windrunner" title="Sylvanas Windrunner">Sylvanas Windrunner</a>'s command, the Horde began the <a href="/wiki/War_of_the_Thorns" title="War of the Thorns">War of the Thorns</a> with the goal of conquering <a href="/wiki/Teldrassil" title="Teldrassil">Teldrassil</a> itself. However after the Horde conquered <a href="/wiki/Darkshore" title="Darkshore">Darkshore</a>, the Banshee Queen gave the order for Teldrassil to be burned.<sup id="cite&#95;ref-46" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-46"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>46<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup> This triggered the <a href="/wiki/Fourth_War" title="Fourth War">Fourth War</a>, as the Alliance avenged Teldrassil's destruction by unleashing <a href="/wiki/Battle_for_Lordaeron" title="Battle for Lordaeron">an all-out siege on Lordaeron Keep</a>, defeating most of the Horde forces stationed there. However, Sylvanas managed to <a href="/wiki/New_Plague" title="New Plague">blight</a> the ruins, preventing the Alliance from establishing it as their stronghold in the region.
</p><p>After liberating <a href="/wiki/Princess_Talanji" class="mw-redirect" title="Princess Talanji">Princess Talanji</a> and <a href="/wiki/Prophet_Zul" class="mw-redirect" title="Prophet Zul">Prophet Zul</a> from the <a href="/wiki/Stormwind_Stockade" title="Stormwind Stockade">Stormwind Stockade</a>, <a href="/wiki/Rastakhan" title="Rastakhan">King Rastakhan</a> allowed the Horde to establish themselves on <a href="/wiki/Zandalar" title="Zandalar">Zandalar</a>.<sup id="cite&#95;ref-47" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-47"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>47<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup> While the Horde worked to aid the Zandalari and sway them to their cause, <a href="/wiki/Eitrigg" title="Eitrigg">Eitrigg</a> and the <a href="/wiki/Adventurer" title="Adventurer">Speaker of the Horde</a> were able to get the <a href="/wiki/Mag%27har_(alternate_universe)" class="mw-redirect" title="Mag&#39;har (alternate universe)">Mag'har clans of Draenor</a> to join the Horde. In the aftermath of the <a href="/wiki/Battle_of_Dazar%27alor" title="Battle of Dazar&#39;alor">Battle of Dazar'alor</a>, the Horde's efforts proved fruitful as the <a href="/wiki/Zandalari_Empire" title="Zandalari Empire">Zandalari Empire</a> joined the Horde, as its equals.
</p><p>As the war progressed, the Horde split between Sylvanas loyalists and Varok Saurfang's revolutionaries. Before the two armies could clash at Orgrimmar, Varok Saurfang, recognizing Sylvanas's loyalists as his brothers and sisters in the Horde and unwilling to spill more Horde blood, challenged Sylvanas to <a href="/wiki/Mak%27gora" title="Mak&#39;gora">Mak'gora</a>. During the duel, Saurfang was able to get Sylvanas to admit that she considered the Horde nothing, which caused the Warchief to swiftly kill him and abandoned the Horde altogether.<sup id="cite&#95;ref-48" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-48"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>48<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup> In the aftermath of Saurfang's death and Sylvanas's betrayal, the Horde rallied together once more, with her former loyalists defecting to his revolution. Following these events, the Horde and the Alliance have entered into an armistice with one another.
</p><p>Sometime later, the <a href="/wiki/Voldunai" title="Voldunai">Voldunai</a> <a href="/wiki/Vulpera" title="Vulpera">vulpera</a> of <a href="/wiki/Vol%27dun" title="Vol&#39;dun">Vol'dun</a> led by <a href="/wiki/Kiro" title="Kiro">Kiro</a> joined the Horde.
</p><p>In the aftermath of <a href="/wiki/N%27Zoth" title="N&#39;Zoth">N'Zoth</a>'s death and the end of the <a href="/wiki/Fourth_War" title="Fourth War">Fourth War</a>, the leaders of the Horde received the terms for an armistice from the <a href="/wiki/Alliance" title="Alliance">Grand Alliance</a>, putting the <a href="/wiki/Fourth_War" title="Fourth War">Fourth War</a> on hold indefinitely, if not permanently. With that matter settled, <a href="/wiki/Lor%27themar_Theron" title="Lor&#39;themar Theron">Lor'themar Theron</a> addressed the absence of a <a href="/wiki/Warchief" title="Warchief">Warchief</a>. Despite support for Thrall to take the mantle on again, he refused, citing that he had promised Varok Saurfang he would not take on the role once more. Instead, he brought up the idea of the Warchief being necessary, instead bringing in the belief that a change was needed for the Horde's leadership. Thus the <a href="/wiki/Horde_Council" title="Horde Council">Horde Council</a>, a conglomerate of leaders who would represent their people's interests, was created to lead the Horde,<sup id="cite&#95;ref-Warchief&#95;of&#95;the&#95;Horde&#95;(quest)&#95;6-1" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-Warchief_of_the_Horde_(quest)-6"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>6<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup> who rejoiced at the announcement of the council.<sup id="cite&#95;ref-49" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-49"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>49<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup>
</p>
<h3><span class="mw-headline" id="Shadowlands">Shadowlands</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/wiki/Horde?action=edit&amp;section=11" title="Edit section: Shadowlands"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h3>
<div style="clear:left;"></div><p><span style="display:inline-block; min-width: 32px; text-align:center"><span class="icon sl-inline"><a href="/wiki/World_of_Warcraft:_Shadowlands" title="Shadowlands"><img alt="Shadowlands" src="/images/thumb/Shadowlands-Icon-Inline.png/48px-Shadowlands-Icon-Inline.png?d84a63" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="48" height="17" style="vertical-align: middle" data-file-width="80" data-file-height="29" /></a></span></span> <span class="greybar" style="font-size: 0.85em; padding: 0 0.2em;"><b>This section concerns content related to <i><a href="/wiki/World_of_Warcraft:_Shadowlands" title="World of Warcraft: Shadowlands">Shadowlands</a></i>.</b></span></p><div style="clear:left;"></div>
<p>Sometime after the formation of the Horde Council, the armies of the Horde rallied together and defeated the <a href="/wiki/Widow%27s_Bite" title="Widow&#39;s Bite">Widow's Bite</a> and <a href="/wiki/Banshee_loyalists" title="Banshee loyalists">Banshee loyalists</a> that were causing unrest within <a href="/wiki/Zandalar" title="Zandalar">Zandalar</a>.<sup id="cite&#95;ref-50" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-50"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>50<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup> After Sylvanas defeated <a href="/wiki/Bolvar_Fordragon" title="Bolvar Fordragon">Bolvar Fordragon</a> and shattered the <a href="/wiki/Helm_of_Domination" title="Helm of Domination">Helm of Domination</a>, the Horde united with the <a href="/wiki/Argent_Crusade" title="Argent Crusade">Argent Crusade</a> in order to combat the <a href="/wiki/Scourge" title="Scourge">Scourge</a> forces that were attacking <a href="/wiki/Durotar" title="Durotar">Durotar</a>,the <a href="/wiki/Northern_Barrens" title="Northern Barrens">Northern Barrens</a>, and <a href="/wiki/Icecrown" title="Icecrown">Icecrown</a>.<sup id="cite&#95;ref-51" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-51"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>51<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup> Soon after the deaths of <a href="/wiki/Herald_Dalora" title="Herald Dalora">Herald Dalora</a> and <a href="/wiki/Nathanos_Blightcaller" title="Nathanos Blightcaller">Nathanos Blightcaller</a>, the leaderships of both the Horde and Alliance grouped with Bolvar and the <a href="/wiki/Knights_of_the_Ebon_Blade" title="Knights of the Ebon Blade">Knights of the Ebon Blade</a> and decided to send their <a href="/wiki/Adventurer" title="Adventurer">champions</a> to enter the <a href="/wiki/Shadowlands" title="Shadowlands">Shadowlands</a> in order rescue the recently captured <a href="/wiki/Thrall" title="Thrall">Thrall</a>, <a href="/wiki/Baine_Bloodhoof" title="Baine Bloodhoof">Baine Bloodhoof</a>, <a href="/wiki/Anduin_Wrynn" title="Anduin Wrynn">Anduin Wrynn</a>, and <a href="/wiki/Jaina_Proudmoore" title="Jaina Proudmoore">Jaina Proudmoore</a>.<sup id="cite&#95;ref-52" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-52"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>52<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup> Subsequently, the Horde joined the <a href="/wiki/War_against_the_Jailer" title="War against the Jailer">war against the Jailer</a>, during which they successfully rescued Thrall, Baine, and Jaina. Furthermore, each champion personally joined one of the four main <a href="/wiki/Covenant" title="Covenant">Covenants</a> of the <a href="/wiki/Shadowlands" title="Shadowlands">Shadowlands</a> and aided them in their conflicts. The Horde, Alliance, and the Covenant forces were ultimately victorious, with <a href="/wiki/Zovaal" title="Zovaal">Zovaal</a> being killed within the <a href="/wiki/Sepulcher_of_the_First_Ones" title="Sepulcher of the First Ones">Sepulcher of the First Ones</a>.
</p>
<h3><span class="mw-headline" id="Dragonflight">Dragonflight</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/wiki/Horde?action=edit&amp;section=12" title="Edit section: Dragonflight"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h3>
<div style="clear:left;"></div><p><span style="display:inline-block; min-width: 32px; text-align:center"><span class="icon df-inline"><a href="/wiki/World_of_Warcraft:_Dragonflight" title="Dragonflight"><img alt="Dragonflight" src="/images/thumb/Dragonflight-Icon-Inline.png/48px-Dragonflight-Icon-Inline.png?be4c0e" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="48" height="17" style="vertical-align: middle" data-file-width="80" data-file-height="29" /></a></span></span> <span class="greybar" style="font-size: 0.85em; padding: 0 0.2em;"><b>This section concerns content related to <i><a href="/wiki/World_of_Warcraft:_Dragonflight" title="World of Warcraft: Dragonflight">Dragonflight</a></i>.</b></span></p><div style="clear:left;"></div>
<p>After Zovaal's defeat, the truce between the Alliance and Horde continued to hold. When the <a href="/wiki/Dragon_Isles" title="Dragon Isles">Dragon Isles</a> were re-revealed to Azeroth, both factions sent a <a href="/wiki/Dragonscale_Expedition" title="Dragonscale Expedition">joint expedition</a> to investigate.<sup id="cite&#95;ref-53" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-53"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>53<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup> During this time, the Horde gained the allegiance of the <a href="/wiki/Dark_Talons" title="Dark Talons">Dark Talons</a>, a group of <a href="/wiki/Dracthyr" title="Dracthyr">Dracthyr</a>, following their awakening on the <a href="/wiki/Forbidden_Reach" title="Forbidden Reach">Forbidden Reach</a> and joined the Aspects in their <a href="/wiki/War_against_the_Primalists" title="War against the Primalists">war against the Primalists</a>. Following the war's conclusion with the death of <a href="/wiki/Fyrakk" title="Fyrakk">Fyrakk</a>, members of the Horde were amongst those that attended the celebratory party on <a href="/wiki/Amirdrassil" title="Amirdrassil">Amirdrassil</a>. In a historic move, <a href="/wiki/Tyrande_Whisperwind" title="Tyrande Whisperwind">Tyrande Whisperwind</a>, allowed for <a href="/wiki/Hamuul_Runetotem" title="Hamuul Runetotem">Hamuul Runetotem</a>, <a href="/wiki/Bashana_Runetotem" title="Bashana Runetotem">Bashana Runetotem</a>, and Horde <a href="/wiki/Adventurer" title="Adventurer">champions</a> entry into <a href="/wiki/Bel%27ameth" title="Bel&#39;ameth">Bel'ameth</a>, the new night elven capital, in recognition of the Horde's aid in ensuring that Amirdrassil successfully bloomed unto Azeroth.
</p><p>Horde forces later aided <a href="/wiki/Tess_Greymane" title="Tess Greymane">Tess Greymane</a> and her people in reclaiming <a href="/wiki/Gilneas" class="mw-redirect" title="Gilneas">Gilneas</a> from the <a href="/wiki/Scarlet_Crusade" title="Scarlet Crusade">Scarlet Crusade</a>, while Horde champions aided in the defense of the <a href="/wiki/Telogrus_Rift" title="Telogrus Rift">Telogrus Rift</a> against <a href="/wiki/Xal%27atath" title="Xal&#39;atath">Xal'atath</a> and her forces.
</p>
<h3><span class="mw-headline" id="The_War_Within">The War Within</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/wiki/Horde?action=edit&amp;section=13" title="Edit section: The War Within"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h3>
<div style="clear:left;"></div><p><span style="display:inline-block; min-width: 32px; text-align:center"><span class="icon tww-inline"><a href="/wiki/World_of_Warcraft:_The_War_Within" title="The War Within"><img alt="The War Within" src="/images/thumb/TheWarWithin-Petopia-Logo.png/40px-TheWarWithin-Petopia-Logo.png?c7c8d5" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="40" height="22" style="vertical-align: middle" data-file-width="100" data-file-height="54" /></a></span></span> <span class="greybar" style="font-size: 0.85em; padding: 0 0.2em;"><b>This section concerns content related to <i><a href="/wiki/World_of_Warcraft:_The_War_Within" title="World of Warcraft: The War Within">The War Within</a></i>.</b></span></p><div style="clear:left;"></div>
<div class="thumb tright"><div class="thumbinner" style="width:322px;"><a href="/wiki/File:Horde_forces_Khaz_Algar.jpg" class="image"><img src="/images/thumb/Horde_forces_Khaz_Algar.jpg/320px-Horde_forces_Khaz_Algar.jpg?11eb27" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="320" height="180" class="thumbimage" data-file-width="1920" data-file-height="1080" /></a>  <div class="thumbcaption"><div class="magnify"><a href="/wiki/File:Horde_forces_Khaz_Algar.jpg" class="internal" title="Enlarge"></a></div>The Horde forces landing on the <a href="/wiki/Isle_of_Dorn" title="Isle of Dorn">Isle of Dorn</a>.</div></div></div>
<p>When <a href="/wiki/Dalaran" title="Dalaran">Dalaran</a> came under attack by <a href="/wiki/Xal%27atath" title="Xal&#39;atath">Xal'atath</a> and the <a href="/wiki/Nerubian" title="Nerubian">nerubians</a> of <a href="/wiki/Azj-Kahet_(kingdom)" title="Azj-Kahet (kingdom)">Azj-Kahet</a>, Horde forces aided in the defense of the city and its evacuation. Following Dalaran's destruction above the <a href="/wiki/Isle_of_Dorn" title="Isle of Dorn">Isle of Dorn</a>, the survivors found themselves fighting against a nerubian ambush on the <a href="/wiki/Tranquil_Strand" title="Tranquil Strand">Tranquil Strand</a>.<sup id="cite&#95;ref-54" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-54"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>54<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup> After <a href="/wiki/Queensguard_Zirix" title="Queensguard Zirix">Queensguard Zirix</a> and his forces were driven off, they received sanctuary in <a href="/wiki/Dornogal" title="Dornogal">Dornogal</a>.<sup id="cite&#95;ref-55" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-55"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>55<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup> As Horde champions worked to unite the <a href="/wiki/Earthen" title="Earthen">earthen</a>, <a href="/wiki/Thrall" title="Thrall">Thrall</a> departed for <a href="/wiki/Orgrimmar" title="Orgrimmar">Orgrimmar</a> to gather Horde reinforcements for the conflict against the Harbinger.<sup id="cite&#95;ref-56" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-56"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>56<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup>
</p><p>Ten days after the fall of Dalaran, <a href="/wiki/Proudmoore_Keep" title="Proudmoore Keep">Proudmoore Keep</a> in <a href="/wiki/Boralus" title="Boralus">Boralus</a> hosted a joint meeting between several Alliance and Horde leaders who answered the call of Thrall and <a href="/wiki/Jaina_Proudmoore" title="Jaina Proudmoore">Jaina Proudmoore</a>. There, they helped organize the strike force that would be sent to Khaz Algar.<sup id="cite&#95;ref-57" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-57"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>57<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup>
</p><p>Following the arrival of the Horde fleet, they were permitted to establish an <a href="/wiki/Horde_Embassy_(Dornogal)" title="Horde Embassy (Dornogal)">embassy</a> within Dornogal.<sup id="cite&#95;ref-58" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-58"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>58<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup> Even as the Horde and Alliance united to train the earthen for the battle ahead, <a href="/wiki/Overlord_Geya%27rah" class="mw-redirect" title="Overlord Geya&#39;rah">tension</a> and <a href="/wiki/Turalyon" title="Turalyon">friction</a> remained between them.<sup id="cite&#95;ref-59" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-59"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>59<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup> Horde forces were among those who arrived to aid the <a href="/wiki/Arathi" title="Arathi">Arathi</a> when the <a href="/wiki/Nerubian" title="Nerubian">nerubians</a> of <a href="/wiki/Azj-Kahet" title="Azj-Kahet">Azj-Kahet</a> launched an all-out assault on <a href="/wiki/Hallowfall" title="Hallowfall">Hallowfall</a>.<sup id="cite&#95;ref-60" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-60"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>60<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup>
</p><p>In time, the earthen were invited to join both the Horde and the Alliance.<sup id="cite&#95;ref-61" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-61"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>61<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup><sup id="cite&#95;ref-62" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-62"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>62<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup> While some earthen chose the Horde, the <a href="/wiki/Council_of_Dornogal" title="Council of Dornogal">Council of Dornogal</a> appointed <a href="/wiki/Olbarig" title="Olbarig">Olbarig</a> and <a href="/wiki/Harmot" title="Harmot">Harmot</a> as ambassadors to represent their people.
</p><p>Horde champions later joined <a href="/wiki/Trade_Prince" class="mw-redirect" title="Trade Prince">Trade Prince</a> <a href="/wiki/Monte_Gazlowe" title="Monte Gazlowe">Monte Gazlowe</a> and his allies as they ventured into <a href="/wiki/Undermine" title="Undermine">Undermine</a>. Following the death of <a href="/wiki/Renzik_%22The_Shiv%22" title="Renzik &quot;The Shiv&quot;">Renzik "The Shiv"</a>,<sup id="cite&#95;ref-63" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-63"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>63<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup> they rallied the <a href="/wiki/Cartels_of_Undermine" title="Cartels of Undermine">Cartels of Undermine</a> into open revolt against <a href="/wiki/Jastor_Gallywix" title="Jastor Gallywix">Jastor Gallywix</a> and his <a href="/wiki/Darkfuse_Enforcement" class="mw-redirect" title="Darkfuse Enforcement">Darkfuse Enforcement</a>.<sup id="cite&#95;ref-64" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-64"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>64<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup> Gallywix was ultimately slain,<sup id="cite&#95;ref-65" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-65"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>65<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup> and Gazlowe and the other cartel leaders came together to build a new future for Undermine.<sup id="cite&#95;ref-66" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-66"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>66<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup>
</p><p>The <a href="/wiki/Red_Dawn" title="Red Dawn">Red Dawn</a> sought control of the <a href="/wiki/Arathi_Highlands" title="Arathi Highlands">Arathi Highlands</a> and attempted to pit the Horde and Alliance forces of <a href="/wiki/Hammerfall" title="Hammerfall">Hammerfall</a> and <a href="/wiki/Stromgarde_(kingdom)" title="Stromgarde (kingdom)">Stromgarde</a> against one another.<sup id="cite&#95;ref-67" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-67"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>67<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup> After <a href="/wiki/Danath_Trollbane" title="Danath Trollbane">King Danath Trollbane</a> was captured by the Red Dawn, Horde adventurers accompanied <a href="/wiki/Overlord_Geya%27rah" class="mw-redirect" title="Overlord Geya&#39;rah">Overlord Geya'rah</a> and <a href="/wiki/Eitrigg" title="Eitrigg">Eitrigg</a> to <a href="/wiki/Ar%27gorok" title="Ar&#39;gorok">Ar'gorok</a> to rescue him.<sup id="cite&#95;ref-68" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-68"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>68<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup> Afterwards, they made their way to Stromgarde and thwarted the Red Dawn's efforts, and they were ultimately exiled from the region.<sup id="cite&#95;ref-69" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-69"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>69<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup>
</p><p>When <a href="/wiki/Locus-Walker" title="Locus-Walker">Locus-Walker</a> learned of a plot by the <a href="/wiki/Shadowguard" title="Shadowguard">Shadowguard</a> to resurrect <a href="/wiki/Dimensius" title="Dimensius">Dimensius</a>, he recruited Horde champions to help halt the return of the All-Devouring.<sup id="cite&#95;ref-70" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-70"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>70<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup> Under Locus-Walker's direction, they formed an alliance with Xal'atath while battling the Shadowguard.<sup id="cite&#95;ref-71" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-71"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>71<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup> They were ultimately successful in defeating the All-Devouring, who became trapped within the <a href="/wiki/Dark_Heart" title="Dark Heart">Dark Heart</a>. In the aftermath, Xal'atath claimed the artifact and departed,<sup id="cite&#95;ref-72" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-72"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>72<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup> while the <a href="/wiki/Worldsoul" title="Worldsoul">Worldsoul</a> of <a href="/wiki/K%27aresh" title="K&#39;aresh">K'aresh</a> was saved.<sup id="cite&#95;ref-73" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-73"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>73<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup>
</p>
<h3><span class="mw-headline" id="Midnight">Midnight</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/wiki/Horde?action=edit&amp;section=14" title="Edit section: Midnight"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h3>
<div style="clear:left;"></div><p><span style="display:inline-block; min-width: 32px; text-align:center"><span class="icon mn-inline"><a href="/wiki/World_of_Warcraft:_Midnight" title="Midnight"><img alt="Midnight" src="/images/thumb/Midnight-inline.png/40px-Midnight-inline.png?801468" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="40" height="22" style="vertical-align: middle" data-file-width="100" data-file-height="54" /></a></span></span> <span class="greybar" style="font-size: 0.85em; padding: 0 0.2em;"><b>This section concerns content related to <i><a href="/wiki/World_of_Warcraft:_Midnight" title="World of Warcraft: Midnight">Midnight</a></i>.</b></span></p><div style="clear:left;"></div>
<p>When the <a href="/wiki/Twilight%27s_Hammer" title="Twilight&#39;s Hammer">Twilight's Hammer</a> reemerged as the <a href="/wiki/Twilight%27s_Blade" title="Twilight&#39;s Blade">Twilight's Blade</a>, they infiltrated Orgrimmar and attempted to recruit members out of the <a href="/wiki/Cleft_of_Shadow" title="Cleft of Shadow">Cleft of Shadow</a>, until they were stopped by <a href="/wiki/Grand_Magister_Rommath" title="Grand Magister Rommath">Grand Magister Rommath</a>.<sup id="cite&#95;ref-74" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-74"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>74<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup> The grand magister then entered into a temporary alliance with the <a href="/wiki/Void_elf" title="Void elf">void elves</a> to stop the cult at the <a href="/wiki/Twilight_Highlands" title="Twilight Highlands">Twilight Highlands</a>.<sup id="cite&#95;ref-75" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-75"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>75<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup>
</p><p>As <a href="/wiki/Xal%27atath" title="Xal&#39;atath">Xal'atath</a> and the <a href="/wiki/Devouring_Host" title="Devouring Host">Devouring Host</a> laid siege to the <a href="/wiki/Sunwell" title="Sunwell">Sunwell</a>, the Horde, <a href="/wiki/Alliance" title="Alliance">Alliance</a>, and the <a href="/wiki/Vanguard_of_the_Light" title="Vanguard of the Light">Vanguard of the Light</a> united to defend the Sunwell.<sup id="cite&#95;ref-76" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-76"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>76<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup> After the Devouring Host was pushed back,<sup id="cite&#95;ref-77" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-77"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>77<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup> the Alliance and Vanguard was granted access to <a href="/wiki/Silvermoon_City" title="Silvermoon City">Silvermoon City</a>.<sup id="cite&#95;ref-78" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-78"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>78<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup> Though they did not have access to the whole city, with the guards of the <a href="/wiki/Royal_Exchange" title="Royal Exchange">Royal Exchange</a> allowing only Horde forces entry.<sup id="cite&#95;ref-79" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-79"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>79<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup>
</p><p>After the <a href="/wiki/Hara%27ti" title="Hara&#39;ti">Hara'ti</a> <a href="/wiki/Haranir" title="Haranir">haranir</a> joined the war effort against <a href="/wiki/Xal%27atath" title="Xal&#39;atath">Xal'atath</a>,<sup id="cite&#95;ref-80" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-80"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>80<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup> individual haranir pledged themselves to the Horde,<sup id="cite&#95;ref-81" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-81"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>81<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup> while <a href="/wiki/Envoy_Ir%27ayi" title="Envoy Ir&#39;ayi">Envoy Ir'ayi</a> represented <a href="/wiki/Harandar" title="Harandar">Harandar</a> in <a href="/wiki/Orgrimmar" title="Orgrimmar">Orgrimmar</a>.
</p><p>While the armistice between the Horde and Alliance held, members who opposed the armistice aligned with two rival <a href="/wiki/Domanaar" title="Domanaar">Domanaar</a> to engage in conflict with one another on the sly, with the Horde siding with <a href="/wiki/Ziadan" title="Ziadan">Ziadan</a>.<sup id="cite&#95;ref-82" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-82"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>82<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup>
</p>
<h2><span class="mw-headline" id="Organization">Organization</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/wiki/Horde?action=edit&amp;section=15" title="Edit section: Organization"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h2>
<div class="thumb tright"><div class="thumbinner" style="width:322px;"><a href="/wiki/File:The_Horde_by_Veli_Nystrom.jpg" class="image"><img src="/images/thumb/The_Horde_by_Veli_Nystrom.jpg/320px-The_Horde_by_Veli_Nystrom.jpg?16c24b" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="320" height="418" class="thumbimage" data-file-width="3139" data-file-height="4096" /></a>  <div class="thumbcaption"><div class="magnify"><a href="/wiki/File:The_Horde_by_Veli_Nystrom.jpg" class="internal" title="Enlarge"></a></div>The armies of the Horde.</div></div></div>
<p>Orgrimmar is the capital of the Horde, and the <a href="/wiki/Warchief" title="Warchief">Warchief</a> of the Horde once stood as the undisputed leader. During the time of the warchief, they held dominion over the entire Horde,<sup id="cite&#95;ref-83" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-83"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>83<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup> maintained the faction and was able to declare war for the entire Horde, taking any precaution to ensure the stability and security of the Horde's member states, and had the final say in the induction of new Horde members. The Warchief often made use of <a href="/wiki/Warchief%27s_Command_Board" title="Warchief&#39;s Command Board">Command Boards</a> to order able-bodied citizens to join the war efforts of the Horde in different areas of <a href="/wiki/Azeroth" title="Azeroth">Azeroth</a>.
</p><p>All members of the Horde have to swear a <a href="/wiki/Blood_Oath_of_the_Horde" title="Blood Oath of the Horde">blood oath</a> to join the faction and previously were obligated to follow the warchief's commands and support the warchief in war if the warchief calls upon them for aid. When it was active, the position of warchief could be attained by having the previous warchief chose a successor or challenging the current warchief to a <a href="/wiki/Mak%27gora" title="Mak&#39;gora">Mak'gora</a>: a prearranged duel with deadly weapons between two people following a formal procedure in the presence of witnesses and traditionally fought until one party yields or is killed, usually to settle a quarrel involving a point of honor. Above all else, the title warchief was granted to those who display strength and decisive action. The final warchief was <a href="/wiki/Sylvanas_Windrunner" title="Sylvanas Windrunner">Sylvanas Windrunner</a>, who was challenged for the position by <a href="/wiki/Varok_Saurfang" title="Varok Saurfang">Varok Saurfang</a> and abandoned the Horde after defeating him.
</p><p>Each race within the Horde pledges a leader to represent them on the Horde's council, which has come to replace the warchief position. These representatives are typically the leaders of their people, though <a href="/wiki/Lilian_Voss" title="Lilian Voss">Lilian Voss</a> was appointed as representative for the <a href="/wiki/Forsaken" title="Forsaken">Forsaken</a><sup id="cite&#95;ref-Warchief&#95;of&#95;the&#95;Horde&#95;(quest)&#95;6-2" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-Warchief_of_the_Horde_(quest)-6"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>6<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup> as they assessed their leadership crisis. The council members have the abilities of the previous warchief position, able to induct races into the Horde, deny them, as well as address leadership gaps for other factions within the Horde; such as <a href="/wiki/Thrall" title="Thrall">Thrall</a> appointing <a href="/wiki/Monte_Gazlowe" title="Monte Gazlowe">Monte Gazlowe</a> to take over the position of Trade Prince of the Bilgewater Cartel.
</p><p>Equal in size, the Horde also has its complexities, much like the Alliance. The core seven races of the Horde are the <a href="/wiki/Orc" title="Orc">orcs</a>, <a href="/wiki/Darkspear_tribe" title="Darkspear tribe">Darkspear</a> <a href="/wiki/Jungle_troll" title="Jungle troll">trolls</a>, <a href="/wiki/Tauren" title="Tauren">tauren</a>, <a href="/wiki/Forsaken" title="Forsaken">undead</a>, <a href="/wiki/Blood_elf" title="Blood elf">blood elves</a>, <a href="/wiki/Goblin" title="Goblin">goblins</a>,  and <a href="/wiki/Huojin_Pandaren" title="Huojin Pandaren">Huojin</a> <a href="/wiki/Pandaren" title="Pandaren">pandaren</a>. This roster expanded in the periods leading up to, and during, the <a href="/wiki/Fourth_War" title="Fourth War">Fourth War</a>, which saw the <a href="/wiki/Nightborne" title="Nightborne">nightborne</a>, <a href="/wiki/Mag%27har_orc" title="Mag&#39;har orc">Mag'har orcs</a> of alternate <a href="/wiki/Draenor_(alternate_universe)" title="Draenor (alternate universe)">Draenor</a>, the <a href="/wiki/Highmountain_tauren" title="Highmountain tauren">Highmountain tauren</a>, the <a href="/wiki/Zandalari_troll" title="Zandalari troll">Zandalari trolls</a>, and <a href="/wiki/Vulpera" title="Vulpera">vulpera</a> join their ranks.
</p><p>The overall Horde military include the <a href="/wiki/Horde_Army" title="Horde Army">Horde Army</a>, the <a href="/wiki/Horde_air_force" title="Horde air force">Horde air force</a>, and the <a href="/wiki/Horde_navy" title="Horde navy">Horde navy</a> as the armed forces it can deploy worldwide.
</p><p>The Horde also has countless allies and smaller factions among the races of Azeroth that have joined them: the <a href="/wiki/Revantusk_tribe" title="Revantusk tribe">Revantusk</a> <a href="/wiki/Forest_troll" title="Forest troll">forest trolls</a> of the <a href="/wiki/Hinterlands" title="Hinterlands">Hinterlands</a>, the <a href="/wiki/Stonemaul_clan" title="Stonemaul clan">Stonemaul</a> <a href="/wiki/Ogre" title="Ogre">ogres</a> and various individuals like the <a href="/wiki/Mok%27Nathal" class="mw-redirect" title="Mok&#39;Nathal">Mok'Nathal</a> <a href="/wiki/Beastmaster" title="Beastmaster">beastmaster</a> <a href="/wiki/Rexxar" title="Rexxar">Rexxar</a>, the <a href="/wiki/Taunka" title="Taunka">taunka</a> of <a href="/wiki/Northrend" title="Northrend">Northrend</a>, and the <a href="/wiki/Hozen" title="Hozen">hozen</a> of <a href="/wiki/Pandaria" title="Pandaria">Pandaria</a> to name a few.
</p>
<h3><span class="mw-headline" id="Culture">Culture</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/wiki/Horde?action=edit&amp;section=16" title="Edit section: Culture"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h3>
<p>Before the tauren and trolls joined the Horde, the Horde was also called the Orcish Horde.<sup id="cite&#95;ref-84" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-84"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>84<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup>
Although the Horde is a coalition of diverse races with different cultures, agendas, and values, one thing remains constant within the Horde: respect is earned by the blade.
</p><p>The Horde makes no compromises when it comes to excellence,<sup id="cite&#95;ref-85" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-85"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>85<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup> and the power and ferocity of their warriors is legendary.<sup id="cite&#95;ref-86" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-86"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>86<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup> Misunderstood by many as evil, the Horde possesses a strong code of honor and strict laws for disobedience.<sup id="cite&#95;ref-87" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-87"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>87<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup>
</p><p>Despite their somewhat monstrous appearance, the majority of the Horde is not evil; much like the Alliance, it is comprised of diverse factions and individuals who possess a wide range of values and virtues. Even so, there is one expectation that must be met when joining the Horde: regardless of gender or station, all are expected to pull their own weight and give their talents for the betterment of the Horde.<sup id="cite&#95;ref-88" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-88"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>88<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup> When weakness is a liability to the future of the Horde, it is the duty of the strong to usurp control from the incompetent and redeem the Horde. <i>Lok'tar ogar! Victory or death</i> - it is these words that bind one to the Horde. For they are the most sacred and fundamental of truths to any warrior of the Horde.<sup id="cite&#95;ref-89" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-89"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>89<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup> For the Horde, failure is not an option.
</p><p>Culturally, orcs and tauren believe in redemption more than most other races on Azeroth and are willing to give almost anyone a chance, regardless of reputation. The Revantusk seem to have even accepted, (if not at least tolerate) their ancient enemy the blood elves. Largely because of these beliefs, a number of mortal races and many diverse factions can be found in service to the Horde.
</p>
<h3><span class="mw-headline" id="Leadership">Leadership</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/wiki/Horde?action=edit&amp;section=17" title="Edit section: Leadership"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h3>
<dl><dd><span class="noexcerpt" data-nosnippet=""><i>See also: <a href="/wiki/Old_Horde#Leadership" title="Old Horde">Old Horde#Leadership</a></i></span></dd></dl>
<p>The Horde has traditionally been led by a <a href="/wiki/Warchief" title="Warchief">Warchief</a>; a singular leader who possesses the ultimate say over the decisions that impact the Horde as a faction. This warchief is the top general, politician and ultimate supreme leader of the Horde. While other leaders still possess power over their own nations and have even been shown to defy the warchief in the past, the ability to rally and lead the Horde as a whole is a privilege that the warchief alone possesses and is able to distribute under their power. This position was abolished in favor of the <a href="/wiki/Horde_Council" title="Horde Council">Horde Council</a> following <a href="/wiki/Sylvanas_Windrunner" title="Sylvanas Windrunner">Sylvanas Windrunner</a>'s tenure as warchief. This was done as the Warchief held a dark legacy<sup id="cite&#95;ref-Negotiation&#95;5-1" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-Negotiation-5"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>5<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup> that needed to end after Sylvanas' abuse of the position.<sup id="cite&#95;ref-Warchief&#95;of&#95;the&#95;Horde&#95;(quest)&#95;6-3" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-Warchief_of_the_Horde_(quest)-6"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>6<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup>
</p><p>As the exact phrasing used in the position's abolition was 'in a time of peace', it is unknown if the position will be used again if war should return.
</p>

<h2><span class="mw-headline" id="Membership">Membership</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/wiki/Horde?action=edit&amp;section=18" title="Edit section: Membership"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h2>
<div class="thumb tright"><div class="thumbinner" style="width:242px;"><a href="/wiki/File:Orgrimmar_Hero%27s_War_Banner.jpg" class="image"><img src="/images/thumb/Orgrimmar_Hero%27s_War_Banner.jpg/240px-Orgrimmar_Hero%27s_War_Banner.jpg?ff3c9e" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="240" height="308" class="thumbimage" data-file-width="773" data-file-height="991" /></a>  <div class="thumbcaption"><div class="magnify"><a href="/wiki/File:Orgrimmar_Hero%27s_War_Banner.jpg" class="internal" title="Enlarge"></a></div><span class="nobreak"><span class="linkicon"><img alt="" src="/images/thumb/Inv_misc_8xp_encrypted04.png/16px-Inv_misc_8xp_encrypted04.png?cea0dc" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" />&#160;</span><a href="/wiki/Orgrimmar_Hero%27s_War_Banner" title="Orgrimmar Hero&#39;s War Banner"><span class="itemlink ajaxttlink qc-epic" title="Orgrimmar Hero&#39;s War Banner is an epic quality item."><span class="bracket">&#91;</span>Orgrimmar Hero's War Banner<span class="bracket">&#93;</span></span></a></span></div></div></div>
<div class="noexcerpt" style="margin: calc(0.5em + 1vh) auto calc(0.5em + 1vh) min(24px, calc(15px + 1.25%)); max-width:calc(70% + 15em)">
<div style="font-style:italic">
<p>“I'll tell ya what de Horde is. De Horde that me an' <a href="/wiki/Thrall" title="Thrall">Thrall</a> built. It be a family. When de whole world try ta put us down, da family come an' pick us back up.”
</p>
</div>
<dl><dd>— <a href="/wiki/Vol%27jin" title="Vol&#39;jin">Warchief Vol'jin</a><sup id="cite&#95;ref-92" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-92"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>92<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup></dd></dl></div>
<p>The alliances which comprised the Horde in earlier times were never restored following their defeat in the <a href="/wiki/Second_War" title="Second War">Second War</a>.
