Instead, the orcs forged new alliances that reflected their rediscovered <a href="/wiki/Shaman" title="Shaman">shamanistic</a> heritage, including the <a href="/wiki/Tauren" title="Tauren">tauren</a> and the <a href="/wiki/Darkspear_tribe" title="Darkspear tribe">Darkspear</a> <a href="/wiki/Jungle_troll" title="Jungle troll">trolls</a>. Even their pact with the undead <a href="/wiki/Forsaken" title="Forsaken">Forsaken</a> was based on compassion and a genuine desire to help the restless undead of <a href="/wiki/Lordaeron" title="Lordaeron">Lordaeron</a>.
</p><p>The Horde in <a href="/wiki/Kalimdor" title="Kalimdor">Kalimdor</a>, which acts as the bulk of the Horde's forces, serve under the <a href="/wiki/Warchief" title="Warchief">Warchief</a>'s leadership. They include most of the <a href="/wiki/Orc" title="Orc">orcs</a>, the tauren, the Darkspear trolls, the <a href="/wiki/Goblin" title="Goblin">goblins</a> of the <a href="/wiki/Bilgewater_Cartel" title="Bilgewater Cartel">Bilgewater Cartel</a>, the <a href="/wiki/Huojin_Pandaren" title="Huojin Pandaren">Huojin Pandaren</a>, and a number of less prominent races within the Horde - such as the <a href="/wiki/Stonemaul_clan" title="Stonemaul clan">Stonemaul</a> <a href="/wiki/Ogre" title="Ogre">ogres</a>. The Horde in the <a href="/wiki/Eastern_Kingdoms" title="Eastern Kingdoms">Eastern Kingdoms</a>, comprising of the <a href="/wiki/Forsaken" title="Forsaken">Forsaken</a> and the <a href="/wiki/Blood_elf" title="Blood elf">blood elves</a>, act with more independence from the Kalimdor Horde but ultimately answers to the warchief out of necessity for the Horde's support. Following the <a href="/wiki/Third_invasion_of_the_Burning_Legion" title="Third invasion of the Burning Legion">third invasion of the Burning Legion</a>, <a href="/wiki/Allied_race" title="Allied race">new allies</a> of the <a href="/wiki/Broken_Isles" title="Broken Isles">Broken Isles</a> join to the Horde, the <a href="/wiki/Nightborne" title="Nightborne">Nightborne</a> of <a href="/wiki/Suramar_City" title="Suramar City">Suramar</a> and the <a href="/wiki/Highmountain_Tribe" title="Highmountain Tribe">Highmountain Tribe</a>. These races all see the Horde differently. Some see it as an instrument of vengeance, hellbent to carve out a future for its people. Others see it as a refuge for those who need shelter, a commonwealth of the downtrodden, built for mutual support. While some view it as an alliance of convenience, a confederacy of unlikely partners free to pursue their own agendas.<sup id="cite&#95;ref-93" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-93"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>93<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup> Whatever their motivations, they are united to claim Kalimdor and the Eastern Kingdoms for themselves in order to secure key provisions and supplies needed to support their people, and forcibly remove Alliance incursions from their own lands. This Horde is the result of the actions in <i><a href="/wiki/Warcraft_III:_Reign_of_Chaos" title="Warcraft III: Reign of Chaos">Warcraft III: Reign of Chaos</a></i>.
</p>
<h4><span class="mw-headline" id="Nation_of_Durotar"><a href="/wiki/File:Orgrimmar.png" class="image" title="Orgrimmar"><img alt="Orgrimmar" src="/images/thumb/Orgrimmar.png/30px-Orgrimmar.png?9f42e3" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="30" height="30" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a> <a href="/wiki/Durotar_(nation)" title="Durotar (nation)">Nation of Durotar</a></span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/wiki/Horde?action=edit&amp;section=19" title="Edit section: Nation of Durotar"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h4>
<div class="thumb tright"><div class="thumbinner" style="width:242px;"><a href="/wiki/File:Orc_Crest.png" class="image"><img src="/images/thumb/Orc_Crest.png/240px-Orc_Crest.png?6c2f52" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="240" height="225" class="thumbimage" data-file-width="280" data-file-height="263" /></a>  <div class="thumbcaption"><div class="magnify"><a href="/wiki/File:Orc_Crest.png" class="internal" title="Enlarge"></a></div><a href="/wiki/Icon_of_Battle" title="Icon of Battle">Icon of Battle</a></div></div></div>
<p><span class="linkicon raceicon RaceLink"><a href="/wiki/Orc" title="Orc"><img alt="Orc" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_Orc_Male.gif/16px-IconSmall_Orc_Male.gif?f251d1" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a><a href="/wiki/Orc" title="Orc"><img alt="Orc" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_Orc_Female.gif/16px-IconSmall_Orc_Female.gif?575305" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a></span>&#160;<a href="/wiki/Orc" title="Orc">Orcs</a> - The surviving <a href="/wiki/Orc" title="Orc">orcs</a> of Azeroth's <a href="/wiki/First_War" title="First War">First</a>, <a href="/wiki/Second_War" title="Second War">Second</a> and <a href="/wiki/Third_War" title="Third War">Third</a> Wars, who were released by <a href="/wiki/Thrall" title="Thrall">Thrall</a> from the <a href="/wiki/Internment_camps" class="mw-redirect" title="Internment camps">Internment camps</a> of <a href="/wiki/Lordaeron" title="Lordaeron">Lordaeron</a> and led by him to form their new destiny in <a href="/wiki/Kalimdor" title="Kalimdor">Kalimdor</a> while attempting to re-embrace their once honorable, shamanistic ways and fight the corruption of <a href="/wiki/Fel" title="Fel">demonic</a> <a href="/wiki/Magic" title="Magic">magic</a>. They founded their capital of <a href="/wiki/Orgrimmar" title="Orgrimmar">Orgrimmar</a> in <a href="/wiki/Durotar" title="Durotar">Durotar</a>, previously led by High Overlord <a href="/wiki/Varok_Saurfang" title="Varok Saurfang">Varok Saurfang</a>. Their culture is split mostly between the orcs' warrior spirit and their shamanistic roots, which has recently wrought on a conflict between the younger and older generations. Dedicated to carving out an existence in a world that has come to largely revile them, the orcs have recently turned their attention to reinvigorated conquests. <a href="/wiki/Ashenvale" title="Ashenvale">Ashenvale</a> and <a href="/wiki/Stonetalon" class="mw-redirect" title="Stonetalon">Stonetalon</a> were among their first targets, and ultimately Warchief Hellscream intended to unite the entirety of <a href="/wiki/Azeroth" title="Azeroth">Azeroth</a> under a single banner.<sup id="cite&#95;ref-94" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-94"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>94<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup> His campaign resulted in Garrosh's dethroning as Warchief.
</p>
<ul><li>Led by <span class="linkicon raceicon"><a href="/wiki/File:IconSmall_Thrall2.gif" class="image"><img alt="IconSmall Thrall2.gif" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_Thrall2.gif/16px-IconSmall_Thrall2.gif?7167fa" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a></span> <a href="/wiki/Thrall" title="Thrall">Chieftain Thrall</a>.</li>
<li><a href="/wiki/Kor%27kron" title="Kor&#39;kron">Kor'kron</a> - The elite fighting force of the Horde, once serving as the personal bodyguards of the Warchief and enforcers of their command, they now answer to the <a href="/wiki/Horde_Council" title="Horde Council">Horde Council</a>. They can be considered the Horde's closest counterpart to the <a href="/wiki/Alliance" title="Alliance">Alliance</a>'s <a href="/wiki/7th_Legion" title="7th Legion">7th Legion</a>.
<ul><li>Led by <span class="linkicon raceicon"><a href="/wiki/File:IconSmall_Orc_Male.gif" class="image"><img alt="IconSmall Orc Male.gif" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_Orc_Male.gif/16px-IconSmall_Orc_Male.gif?f251d1" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a></span> <a href="/wiki/Talgar" title="Talgar">General Talgar</a></li></ul></li></ul>
<p>The orcs in the Horde are from the following clans:
</p>
<ul><li><a href="/wiki/Frostwolf_clan" title="Frostwolf clan">Frostwolf clan</a> - The <b>Frostwolf clan</b> is an <a href="/wiki/Orc" title="Orc">orc</a> clan that originally dwelled in the southwestern parts of <a href="/wiki/Frostfire_Ridge" title="Frostfire Ridge">Frostfire Ridge</a>. Named for their close kinship with Frostfire's native <a href="/wiki/Frost_wolf" title="Frost wolf">frost wolves</a>, the clan placed great value in the noble ideals of family and community and sought to live in harmony with the land. After crossing the Dark Portal many years ago, they are one of the few orcish clans left in the Eastern Kingdoms, the Frostwolf settled in the hidden <a href="/wiki/Alterac_Mountains" title="Alterac Mountains">Alterac Mountains</a> after their exile by Gul'dan. However, the <a href="/wiki/Stormpike" title="Stormpike">Stormpike</a> dwarves later arrived to search for artifacts causing conflict between the two in <a href="/wiki/Alterac_Valley" title="Alterac Valley">Alterac Valley</a>. The Frostwolf clan derived its symbol and name from its kinship with <a href="/wiki/Frostfire_Ridge" title="Frostfire Ridge">Frostfire Ridge</a>'s native frost wolves, whom the orcs hunted alongside, befriended, and even tamed as companions
<ul><li>Led by <span class="linkicon raceicon"><a href="/wiki/File:IconSmall_Drek%27Thar.gif" class="image"><img alt="IconSmall Drek&#39;Thar.gif" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_Drek%27Thar.gif/16px-IconSmall_Drek%27Thar.gif?d5898f" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a></span> <a href="/wiki/Farseer" title="Farseer">Farseer</a> <a href="/wiki/Drek%27Thar" title="Drek&#39;Thar">Drek'Thar</a>, the elder <a href="/wiki/Shaman" title="Shaman">shaman</a> and <a href="/wiki/Seer" title="Seer">seer</a> of the <a href="/wiki/Frostwolf_clan" title="Frostwolf clan">Frostwolf clan</a>, who served as <a href="/wiki/Thrall" title="Thrall">Thrall</a>'s tutor many years ago.</li></ul></li></ul>
<ul><li><a href="/wiki/Warsong_clan" title="Warsong clan">Warsong clan</a> - A powerful warrior clan native to <a href="/wiki/Nagrand" title="Nagrand">Nagrand</a> that battle the <a href="/wiki/Night_elf" title="Night elf">night elves</a> for dominance over <a href="/wiki/Ashenvale" title="Ashenvale">Ashenvale</a>.
<ul><li>Led by <span class="linkicon raceicon"><a href="/wiki/File:IconSmall_Orc_Female.gif" class="image"><img alt="IconSmall Orc Female.gif" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_Orc_Female.gif/16px-IconSmall_Orc_Female.gif?575305" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a></span><a href="/wiki/Gorgonna" title="Gorgonna">Gorgonna</a>
<ul><li><a href="/wiki/Warsong_Outriders" title="Warsong Outriders">Warsong Outriders</a> – Following the Third War, those of the Warsong clan who invaded Ashenvale and began deforestation have not ceased their operation and have come into direct conflict with the <a href="/wiki/Silverwing_Sentinels" title="Silverwing Sentinels">Silverwing Sentinels</a>.
<ul><li>Led by <span class="linkicon raceicon"><a href="/wiki/File:IconSmall_Orc_Male.gif" class="image"><img alt="IconSmall Orc Male.gif" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_Orc_Male.gif/16px-IconSmall_Orc_Male.gif?f251d1" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a></span> <a href="/wiki/Gargok" title="Gargok">Gargok</a>.</li></ul></li></ul></li></ul></li></ul>
<ul><li><a href="/wiki/Blackrock_clan" title="Blackrock clan">Blackrock clan</a> - Many Blackrock orcs joined with Thrall under <a href="/wiki/Orgrim" class="mw-redirect" title="Orgrim">Orgrim</a> and when he freed them from the internment camps. Notable individuals are <a href="/wiki/Eitrigg" title="Eitrigg">Eitrigg</a> and <a href="/wiki/Varok_Saurfang" title="Varok Saurfang">Varok Saurfang</a>. Furthermore, the former Warchief <a href="/wiki/Garrosh_Hellscream" title="Garrosh Hellscream">Garrosh Hellscream</a> granted the Blackrock clan of the <a href="/wiki/Dark_Horde" title="Dark Horde">Dark Horde</a> led by Rend amnesty and recruited a formidable amount of them into the ranks of the Horde.
<ul><li>Led by <span class="linkicon raceicon"><a href="/wiki/File:IconSmall_Eitrigg.gif" class="image"><img alt="IconSmall Eitrigg.gif" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_Eitrigg.gif/16px-IconSmall_Eitrigg.gif?a7f30a" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a></span> <a href="/wiki/Eitrigg" title="Eitrigg">Eitrigg</a></li></ul></li></ul>
<ul><li><a href="/wiki/Bleeding_Hollow_clan" title="Bleeding Hollow clan">Bleeding Hollow clan</a> - The clan returned to Draenor after the Second War, but as it was torn apart, some members crossed safely into Azeroth at the same time as the Warsong clan. They were captured and later rescued from the internment camps, and now they serve the new Horde. Additionally they have a notable presence in the <a href="/wiki/Mag%27har_(faction)" title="Mag&#39;har (faction)">Mag'har</a>.
<ul><li>Led by: <span class="linkicon raceicon"><a href="/wiki/File:IconSmall_OrcBrown_Male.gif" class="image"><img alt="IconSmall OrcBrown Male.gif" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_OrcBrown_Male.gif/16px-IconSmall_OrcBrown_Male.gif?16fed8" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a></span> <a href="/wiki/Jorin_Deadeye" title="Jorin Deadeye">Jorin Deadeye</a>.</li></ul></li></ul>
<ul><li><span class="linkicon raceicon"><a href="/wiki/File:IconSmall_Dragonmaw_Male.gif" class="image"><img alt="IconSmall Dragonmaw Male.gif" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_Dragonmaw_Male.gif/16px-IconSmall_Dragonmaw_Male.gif?1b1d98" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a></span><span class="linkicon raceicon"><a href="/wiki/File:IconSmall_Dragonmaw_Female.gif" class="image"><img alt="IconSmall Dragonmaw Female.gif" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_Dragonmaw_Female.gif/16px-IconSmall_Dragonmaw_Female.gif?ee20c1" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a></span> <a href="/wiki/Dragonmaw_clan" title="Dragonmaw clan">Dragonmaw clan</a> - The Dragonmaw residing in the <a href="/wiki/Twilight_Highlands" title="Twilight Highlands">Twilight Highlands</a> joined the new Horde during the <a href="/wiki/Cataclysm_(event)" title="Cataclysm (event)">Cataclysm</a>.<sup id="cite&#95;ref-95" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-95"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>95<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup>
<ul><li>Led by <span class="linkicon raceicon"><a href="/wiki/File:IconSmall_Gorfax.gif" class="image"><img alt="IconSmall Gorfax.gif" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_Gorfax.gif/16px-IconSmall_Gorfax.gif?e25458" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a></span> <a href="/wiki/Gorfax_Angerfang" title="Gorfax Angerfang">Gorfax Angerfang</a>.</li></ul></li></ul>
<ul><li><a href="/wiki/Shadowmoon_clan" title="Shadowmoon clan">Shadowmoon clan</a> - Scouts and peons are seen serving the New Horde at <a href="/wiki/Shadowmoon_Village" title="Shadowmoon Village">Shadowmoon Village</a>.</li></ul>
<ul><li><a href="/wiki/Burning_Blade_clan" title="Burning Blade clan">Burning Blade clan</a> - The <a href="/wiki/Blademaster" title="Blademaster">blademasters</a> were once part of the Burning Blade, although the clan consumed itself in the throes of demonic corruption, these few swordsmen are still part of the Horde.</li></ul>
<ul><li><a href="/wiki/Rageroar_clan" title="Rageroar clan">Rageroar clan</a> - A newly created clan that was assaulting Northwatch hold. It was almost destroyed at the end of the Alliance questline.<sup id="cite&#95;ref-96" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-96"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>96<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup> Later joined the Horde army in the Razing of Northwatch.<sup id="cite&#95;ref-ToW&#95;35-1" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-ToW-35"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>35<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup>
<ul><li>Led by <span class="linkicon raceicon"><a href="/wiki/File:IconSmall_Orc_Male.gif" class="image"><img alt="IconSmall Orc Male.gif" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_Orc_Male.gif/16px-IconSmall_Orc_Male.gif?f251d1" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a></span> <a href="/wiki/Karga_Rageroar" title="Karga Rageroar">Karga Rageroar</a>.</li></ul></li></ul>
<ul><li><a href="/wiki/Shattered_Hand_clan" title="Shattered Hand clan">Shattered Hand</a> - The Azerothian part of the clan have joined Thrall and serve as the Horde's guild of assassins admitting other races like trolls into the organization.
<ul><li>Led by <span class="linkicon raceicon"><a href="/wiki/File:IconSmall_Orc_Female.gif" class="image"><img alt="IconSmall Orc Female.gif" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_Orc_Female.gif/16px-IconSmall_Orc_Female.gif?575305" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a></span> <a href="/wiki/Thega_Graveblade" title="Thega Graveblade">Thega Graveblade</a>.</li></ul></li></ul>
<ul><li><span class="linkicon raceicon"><a href="/wiki/File:IconSmall_DireOrc.gif" class="image"><img alt="IconSmall DireOrc.gif" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_DireOrc.gif/16px-IconSmall_DireOrc.gif?d98ade" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a></span> <a href="/wiki/Dire_orc" title="Dire orc">Dire orc</a> - A small number of these mutated orcs are found in the ranks of the main timeline orc nation, such as <a href="/wiki/Ariok" title="Ariok">Ariok</a>, <a href="/wiki/Korm_Bonegrind" title="Korm Bonegrind">Korm Bonegrind</a> and a few others.</li></ul>
<h4><span class="mw-headline" id="Darkspear_tribe"><a href="/wiki/File:Darkspear.png" class="image" title="Darkspear tribe"><img alt="Darkspear tribe" src="/images/thumb/Darkspear.png/30px-Darkspear.png?d19dc5" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="30" height="30" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a> <a href="/wiki/Darkspear_tribe" title="Darkspear tribe">Darkspear tribe</a></span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/wiki/Horde?action=edit&amp;section=20" title="Edit section: Darkspear tribe"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h4>
<div class="thumb tright"><div class="thumbinner" style="width:242px;"><a href="/wiki/File:Troll_Crest.png" class="image"><img src="/images/thumb/Troll_Crest.png/240px-Troll_Crest.png?43b8d6" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="240" height="240" class="thumbimage" data-file-width="280" data-file-height="280" /></a>  <div class="thumbcaption"><div class="magnify"><a href="/wiki/File:Troll_Crest.png" class="internal" title="Enlarge"></a></div><a href="/wiki/Icon_of_Shadows" title="Icon of Shadows">Icon of Shadows</a></div></div></div>
<p><span class="linkicon raceicon RaceLink"><a href="/wiki/Jungle_troll" title="Jungle troll"><img alt="Jungle troll" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_Troll_Male.gif/16px-IconSmall_Troll_Male.gif?7580a0" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a><a href="/wiki/Jungle_troll" title="Jungle troll"><img alt="Jungle troll" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_Troll_Female.gif/16px-IconSmall_Troll_Female.gif?9723e4" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a></span>&#160;<a href="/wiki/Jungle_troll" title="Jungle troll">Jungle trolls</a> - The jungle trolls of the <a href="/wiki/Darkspear_Islands" title="Darkspear Islands">Darkspear Islands</a> in the <a href="/wiki/Great_Sea" title="Great Sea">Great Sea</a> have a very savage and dark history studying voodoo arts and worshiping the <a href="/wiki/Loa" title="Loa">loa</a>, however after Thrall had saved their tribe from extinction, <a href="/wiki/Vol%27jin" title="Vol&#39;jin">Vol'jin</a> son of <a href="/wiki/Sen%27jin" title="Sen&#39;jin">Sen'jin</a> pledged his tribe to the Horde and founded a new home on the <a href="/wiki/Darkspear_Isle" title="Darkspear Isle">Darkspear Isle</a> of the <a href="/wiki/Echo_Isles" title="Echo Isles">Echo Isles</a> off the coast of <a href="/wiki/Durotar" title="Durotar">Durotar</a>. Some trolls have now begun to study shamanism and more recently druidism, some of these trolls even mix shamanism with voodoo arts, most of the members of the tribe have or are attempting to give up the ways of cannibalism and the other traits of their race's darker history. After retaking much of their territory from the <a href="/wiki/Kul_Tiras_(kingdom)" title="Kul Tiras (kingdom)">Kul Tiras</a> marines and the treacherous <a href="/wiki/Zalazane" title="Zalazane">Zalazane</a>, the Darkspear have been invited to join a revitalized troll empire under the <a href="/wiki/Zandalari" class="mw-redirect" title="Zandalari">Zandalari</a>'s leadership - though Vol'jin declined this offer.
</p>
<ul><li>Led by: <span class="linkicon raceicon"><a href="/wiki/File:IconSmall_Rokhan.gif" class="image"><img alt="IconSmall Rokhan.gif" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_Rokhan.gif/16px-IconSmall_Rokhan.gif?f0e085" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a></span> <a href="/wiki/Rokhan" title="Rokhan">Chieftain Rokhan</a>.</li>
<li><a href="/wiki/Atal-Mhuto" title="Atal-Mhuto">Atal-Mhuto</a> - are the elite <a href="/wiki/Shadow_hunter" title="Shadow hunter">shadow hunter</a> military group of the <a href="/wiki/Darkspear_tribe" title="Darkspear tribe">Darkspear trolls</a>.<sup id="cite&#95;ref-97" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-97"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>97<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup></li></ul>
<ul><li><a href="/wiki/Ravasaur_Trainers" title="Ravasaur Trainers">Ravasaur Trainers</a> - They were a faction of trolls in <a href="/wiki/Un%27Goro_Crater" title="Un&#39;Goro Crater">Un'Goro Crater</a>, part of an elite group of <a href="/wiki/Raptor" title="Raptor">raptor</a> riders that provided their mounts to members of the Horde who prove themselves able to survive the creature's poison.</li></ul>
<h4><span class="mw-headline" id="United_tauren_tribes_of_Thunder_Bluff"><a href="/wiki/File:Thunder_Bluff.png" class="image" title="Thunder Bluff"><img alt="Thunder Bluff" src="/images/thumb/Thunder_Bluff.png/30px-Thunder_Bluff.png?b21701" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="30" height="30" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a> <a href="/wiki/United_tauren_tribes" title="United tauren tribes">United tauren tribes of Thunder Bluff</a></span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/wiki/Horde?action=edit&amp;section=21" title="Edit section: United tauren tribes of Thunder Bluff"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h4>
<div class="thumb tright"><div class="thumbinner" style="width:242px;"><a href="/wiki/File:Tauren_Crest.png" class="image"><img src="/images/thumb/Tauren_Crest.png/240px-Tauren_Crest.png?e638f5" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="240" height="294" class="thumbimage" data-file-width="280" data-file-height="343" /></a>  <div class="thumbcaption"><div class="magnify"><a href="/wiki/File:Tauren_Crest.png" class="internal" title="Enlarge"></a></div><a href="/wiki/Icon_of_the_Earth_Mother" title="Icon of the Earth Mother">Icon of the Earth Mother</a></div></div></div>
<p><span class="linkicon raceicon RaceLink"><a href="/wiki/Tauren" title="Tauren"><img alt="Tauren" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_Tauren_Male.gif/16px-IconSmall_Tauren_Male.gif?b94d34" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a><a href="/wiki/Tauren" title="Tauren"><img alt="Tauren" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_Tauren_Female.gif/16px-IconSmall_Tauren_Female.gif?494a7d" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a></span>&#160;<a href="/wiki/Tauren" title="Tauren">Tauren</a> - The noble tauren of Kalimdor are a race that had hunted the lands of central Kalimdor for generations; however, when Thrall and his orcs had arrived onto the lands of Kalimdor they had already been driven near extinction by the marauding <a href="/wiki/Centaur" title="Centaur">centaur</a> that rampaged through their lands. Under the leadership of High Chieftain <a href="/wiki/Cairne_Bloodhoof" title="Cairne Bloodhoof">Cairne Bloodhoof</a> and the young <a href="/wiki/Warchief" title="Warchief">Warchief</a> <a href="/wiki/Thrall" title="Thrall">Thrall</a>, the tauren managed to drive back most of the centaur, and find a permanent home in <a href="/wiki/Thunder_Bluff" title="Thunder Bluff">Thunder Bluff</a>. Since then, Cairne has pledged his people to the Horde, offering the orcs spiritual guidance and aid. After Cairne's death, <a href="/wiki/Baine_Bloodhoof" title="Baine Bloodhoof">Baine Bloodhoof</a> has arisen to take his father's place and lead the tauren into a brighter future. With the recent Alliance incursion into the <a href="/wiki/Barrens" title="Barrens">Barrens</a>, the tauren have created a <a href="/wiki/Great_Gate" title="Great Gate">mighty gate</a> to protect their homeland of <a href="/wiki/Mulgore" title="Mulgore">Mulgore</a>.
</p>
<ul><li>Led by <span class="linkicon raceicon"><a href="/wiki/File:IconSmall_Baine.gif" class="image"><img alt="IconSmall Baine.gif" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_Baine.gif/16px-IconSmall_Baine.gif?65ba9b" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a></span> <a href="/wiki/Chieftain" title="Chieftain">High Chieftain</a> <a href="/wiki/Baine_Bloodhoof" title="Baine Bloodhoof">Baine Bloodhoof</a>.</li></ul>
<ul><li><a href="/wiki/Sunwalker" class="mw-redirect" title="Sunwalker">Sunwalkers</a> - Tauren paladins who take their power from <a href="/wiki/An%27she" title="An&#39;she">An'she</a>, the sun.
<ul><li>Led by <span class="raceicon"><img alt="" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_Tauren_Female.gif/16px-IconSmall_Tauren_Female.gif?494a7d" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" />&#160;</span><a href="/wiki/Aponi_Brightmane" title="Aponi Brightmane"><span style="color:">Aponi Brightmane</span></a>.</li></ul></li></ul>
<ul><li><a href="/wiki/Seers" title="Seers">Seers</a> - Tauren priests who take their power from <a href="/wiki/An%27she" title="An&#39;she">An'she</a>, the sun.
<ul><li>Created by <span class="raceicon"><img alt="" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_Tauren_Male.gif/16px-IconSmall_Tauren_Male.gif?b94d34" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" />&#160;</span><a href="/wiki/Tahu_Sagewind" title="Tahu Sagewind"><span style="color:">Tahu Sagewind</span></a>.</li></ul></li></ul>
<p>The Tauren of the Horde come from various tribes:
</p>
<ul><li><a href="/wiki/Bloodhoof_tribe" title="Bloodhoof tribe">Bloodhoof tribe</a> - The first tauren tribe to side with the Horde, they are led by <a href="/wiki/Baine_Bloodhoof" title="Baine Bloodhoof">Baine Bloodhoof</a> in the wake of <span class="raceicon"></span><a href="/wiki/Cairne_Bloodhoof" title="Cairne Bloodhoof"><span style="color:">his father's</span></a> death at the hands of <a href="/wiki/Magatha_Grimtotem" title="Magatha Grimtotem">Magatha Grimtotem</a>.
<ul><li>Led by <span class="raceicon"><img alt="" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_Baine.gif/16px-IconSmall_Baine.gif?65ba9b" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" />&#160;</span><a href="/wiki/Baine_Bloodhoof" title="Baine Bloodhoof"><span style="color:">Baine Bloodhoof</span></a>.</li></ul></li></ul>
<ul><li><a href="/wiki/Ragetotem_tribe" title="Ragetotem tribe">Ragetotem tribe</a> - Proud <a href="/wiki/Warrior" title="Warrior">warriors</a> and the fiercest of <a href="/wiki/Thunder_Bluff" title="Thunder Bluff">Thunder Bluff</a>.</li></ul>
<ul><li><a href="/wiki/Dawnstrider_tribe" title="Dawnstrider tribe">Dawnstrider tribe</a> - Experienced enchanters and skilled shaman.</li></ul>
<ul><li><a href="/wiki/Runetotem_tribe" class="mw-redirect" title="Runetotem tribe">Runetotem tribe</a> - These tauren have recently discovered the ways of the <a href="/wiki/Druid" title="Druid">druidism</a>.
<ul><li>Led by <span class="raceicon"><img alt="" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_Hamuul.gif/16px-IconSmall_Hamuul.gif?5fc33e" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" />&#160;</span><a href="/wiki/Hamuul_Runetotem" title="Hamuul Runetotem"><span style="color:">Hamuul Runetotem</span></a>.</li></ul></li></ul>
<ul><li><a href="/wiki/Highmountain_tribe_(Legends)" title="Highmountain tribe (Legends)">Highmountain tribe</a> - A tribe with several notable members who fought (and still fight) against the Burning Legion.
<ul><li>Led by <span class="raceicon"><img alt="" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_Tauren_Male.gif/16px-IconSmall_Tauren_Male.gif?b94d34" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" />&#160;</span><a href="/wiki/Sulamm" title="Sulamm"><span style="color:">Sulamm</span></a> and/or <span class="raceicon"><img alt="" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_Tauren_Male.gif/16px-IconSmall_Tauren_Male.gif?b94d34" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" />&#160;</span><a href="/wiki/Ornamm" title="Ornamm"><span style="color:">Ornamm</span></a>.</li></ul></li></ul>
<ul><li><a href="/wiki/Skychaser_tribe" title="Skychaser tribe">Skychaser tribe</a> - The spiritual leaders of the tauren. They were nearly wiped out during the <a href="/wiki/Grimtotem" class="mw-redirect" title="Grimtotem">Grimtotem</a> coup to take over <a href="/wiki/Thunder_Bluff" title="Thunder Bluff">Thunder Bluff</a>.</li>
<li><a href="/wiki/Thunderhorn_tribe" class="mw-redirect" title="Thunderhorn tribe">Thunderhorn tribe</a> - Most of the Thunderhorn tribe are hunters, the greatest among the tauren.</li></ul>
<ul><li><a href="/wiki/Cloudsong_tribe" title="Cloudsong tribe">Cloudsong tribe</a> – They picked up the role of spiritual leaders of the tauren shaman after the Skychaser tribe was nearly wiped out.</li></ul>
<ul><li><a href="/wiki/Eagletalon_tribe" title="Eagletalon tribe">Eagletalon tribe</a> – This tribe hunts harpies in Mulgore.</li></ul>
<ul><li><a href="/wiki/Mistrunner_tribe" class="mw-redirect" title="Mistrunner tribe">Mistrunner tribe</a> – The Mistrunners are dedicated to the druidic path and seeks to heal the earth and ease the suffering of the Earthmother.</li></ul>
<ul><li><a href="/wiki/Stonehoof_tribe" title="Stonehoof tribe">Stonehoof tribe</a> – The Stonehoof are the most talented smiths among the tauren.
<ul><li>Led by: Unknown</li></ul></li></ul>
<ul><li><a href="/wiki/Wildmane_tribe" class="mw-redirect" title="Wildmane tribe">Wildmane tribe</a> – This tribe has largely joined the Runetotem in seeking the ways of the druid.</li></ul>
<ul><li><a href="/wiki/Winterhoof_tribe" title="Winterhoof tribe">Winterhoof tribe</a> – The Winterhoof tribe represents some of the greatest herbalists and alchemists the tauren have to offer.</li></ul>
<ul><li><a href="/wiki/Stonespire_tribe" title="Stonespire tribe">Stonespire tribe</a> - A tauren tribe that lived in the <a href="/wiki/Southern_Barrens" title="Southern Barrens">Southern Barrens</a>. Most were killed by the forces of <a href="/wiki/Bael%27dun_Keep" title="Bael&#39;dun Keep">Bael'dun Keep</a> but there are a few survivors.</li></ul>
<ul><li><a href="/wiki/Cliffwalker_tribe" class="mw-redirect" title="Cliffwalker tribe">Cliffwalker tribe</a> - A tribe based in <a href="/wiki/Cliffwalker_Post" title="Cliffwalker Post">Cliffwalker Post</a> with strong ties to <a href="/wiki/Druidism" class="mw-redirect" title="Druidism">druidism</a>.
<ul><li>Led by <span class="raceicon"><img alt="" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_Tauren_Male.gif/16px-IconSmall_Tauren_Male.gif?b94d34" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" />&#160;</span><a href="/wiki/High_Chieftain_Cliffwalker" title="High Chieftain Cliffwalker"><span style="color:">High Chieftain Cliffwalker</span></a>.</li></ul></li></ul>
<ul><li><a href="/wiki/Dawnchaser_tribe" title="Dawnchaser tribe">Dawnchaser tribe</a> - A tribe of Horde <a href="/wiki/Tauren" title="Tauren">tauren</a> from <a href="/wiki/Thunder_Bluff" title="Thunder Bluff">Thunder Bluff</a> that have come to <a href="/wiki/Pandaria" title="Pandaria">Pandaria</a>. One of their main outposts in Pandaria is located in the <a href="/wiki/Krasarang_Wilds" title="Krasarang Wilds">Krasarang Wilds</a> at <a href="/wiki/Thunder_Cleft" title="Thunder Cleft">Thunder Cleft</a>.
<ul><li>Led by <span class="raceicon"><img alt="" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_Tauren_Male.gif/16px-IconSmall_Tauren_Male.gif?b94d34" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" />&#160;</span><a href="/wiki/Sunwalker_Dezco" title="Sunwalker Dezco"><span style="color:">Chieftain Dezco</span></a>.</li></ul></li></ul>
<ul><li><span class="linkicon raceicon"><a href="/wiki/File:IconSmall_Grimtotem_Male.gif" class="image"><img alt="IconSmall Grimtotem Male.gif" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_Grimtotem_Male.gif/16px-IconSmall_Grimtotem_Male.gif?26cfbb" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a></span><span class="linkicon raceicon"><a href="/wiki/File:IconSmall_Grimtotem_Female.gif" class="image"><img alt="IconSmall Grimtotem Female.gif" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_Grimtotem_Female.gif/16px-IconSmall_Grimtotem_Female.gif?472453" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a></span> <a href="/wiki/Grimtotem_tribe" title="Grimtotem tribe">Grimtotem tribe</a> - The Grimtotem tauren who survived the battle at <a href="/wiki/Thunder_Bluff" title="Thunder Bluff">Thunder Bluff</a>, chose to follow <a href="/wiki/Jevan_Grimtotem" title="Jevan Grimtotem">Jevan Grimtotem</a> and join the Horde.
<ul><li>Led by <span class="linkicon raceicon"><a href="/wiki/File:IconSmall_Grimtotem_Male.gif" class="image"><img alt="IconSmall Grimtotem Male.gif" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_Grimtotem_Male.gif/16px-IconSmall_Grimtotem_Male.gif?26cfbb" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a></span> <a href="/wiki/Jevan_Grimtotem" title="Jevan Grimtotem">Jevan Grimtotem</a>. Formerly led by <span class="linkicon raceicon"><a href="/wiki/File:IconSmall_Magatha.gif" class="image"><img alt="IconSmall Magatha.gif" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_Magatha.gif/16px-IconSmall_Magatha.gif?a00463" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a></span> <a href="/wiki/Magatha_Grimtotem" title="Magatha Grimtotem">Magatha Grimtotem</a>.</li></ul></li></ul>
<h4><span class="mw-headline" id="The_Forsaken"><a href="/wiki/File:Undercity.png" class="image" title="Forsaken"><img alt="Forsaken" src="/images/thumb/Undercity.png/30px-Undercity.png?1f14eb" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="30" height="34" data-file-width="56" data-file-height="64" /></a> <a href="/wiki/Forsaken" title="Forsaken">The Forsaken</a></span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/wiki/Horde?action=edit&amp;section=22" title="Edit section: The Forsaken"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h4>
<div class="thumb tright"><div class="thumbinner" style="width:242px;"><a href="/wiki/File:Forsaken_Crest.png" class="image"><img src="/images/thumb/Forsaken_Crest.png/240px-Forsaken_Crest.png?a1c59d" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="240" height="321" class="thumbimage" data-file-width="280" data-file-height="375" /></a>  <div class="thumbcaption"><div class="magnify"><a href="/wiki/File:Forsaken_Crest.png" class="internal" title="Enlarge"></a></div><a href="/wiki/Icon_of_Torment" title="Icon of Torment">Icon of Torment</a></div></div></div>
<p><span class="linkicon raceicon RaceLink"><a href="/wiki/Forsaken" title="Forsaken"><img alt="Forsaken" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_Undead_Male.gif/16px-IconSmall_Undead_Male.gif?6cfd1d" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a><a href="/wiki/Forsaken" title="Forsaken"><img alt="Forsaken" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_Undead_Female.gif/16px-IconSmall_Undead_Female.gif?6e00fb" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a></span>&#160;<a href="/wiki/Forsaken" title="Forsaken">Forsaken</a> - The Horde of the <a href="/wiki/Eastern_Kingdoms" title="Eastern Kingdoms">Eastern Kingdoms</a> was initially next to non-existent, with only the <a href="/wiki/Frostwolf" title="Frostwolf">Frostwolf</a> orcs and other smaller forces in operation around the continent. In recent years, however, the Horde's presence has been bolstered by the inclusion of the <a href="/wiki/Forsaken" title="Forsaken">Forsaken</a> of <a href="/wiki/Eastern_Kingdoms" title="Eastern Kingdoms">Eastern Kingdoms</a> and the <a href="/wiki/Blood_elf" title="Blood elf">blood elves</a> of <a href="/wiki/Quel%27Thalas_(kingdom)" title="Quel&#39;Thalas (kingdom)">Quel'Thalas</a>. While considered members of the Horde, their bonds with the western Horde are perhaps not on the same level as the orcs, trolls, and tauren.
</p><p>The Forsaken were once part of the <a href="/wiki/Scourge" title="Scourge">Scourge</a>, forced into mindless servitude under the <a href="/wiki/Lich_King" title="Lich King">Lich King</a>. After the Lich King's grip began to weaken, the <a href="/wiki/Banshee" title="Banshee">banshee</a> queen <a href="/wiki/Sylvanas_Windrunner" title="Sylvanas Windrunner">Sylvanas Windrunner</a> broke free from his will, and went on to both free and rally other undead beings to join her own empire of undeath, dubbed the "Forsaken." Under Lady Sylvanas' leadership, the Forsaken used any means necessary to conquer the fallen kingdom and remove both the <a href="/wiki/Dreadlords" class="mw-redirect" title="Dreadlords">dreadlords</a> and other foes threatening their dominion. After expelling the Scourge from the capital city and massacring the last remaining holdouts of the <a href="/wiki/Alliance" title="Alliance">Alliance</a>, they took over the <a href="/wiki/Ruins_of_Lordaeron" title="Ruins of Lordaeron">catacombs of the former capital</a> in <a href="/wiki/Tirisfal_Glades" title="Tirisfal Glades">Tirisfal Glades</a>, founding a capital of their own called the <a href="/wiki/Undercity" title="Undercity">Undercity</a>.
</p><p>With the backing of the <a href="/wiki/Tauren" title="Tauren">tauren</a>, Thrall warily accepted the Forsaken into the Horde, though this alliance of convenience has been tested - great betrayal at the hands of <a href="/wiki/Grand_Apothecary_Putress" title="Grand Apothecary Putress">Grand Apothecary Putress</a> has put the Forsaken on a tight leash, made tighter by <a href="/wiki/Garrosh" class="mw-redirect" title="Garrosh">his successor's</a> less lenient stance on their modus operandi. Following <a href="/wiki/Vol%27jin" title="Vol&#39;jin">Vol'jin</a>'s ascension to Warchief and especially once <a href="/wiki/Sylvanas_Windrunner" title="Sylvanas Windrunner">Sylvanas Windrunner</a> took the reins of power, this increased scrutiny has all but evaporated. The group also consists of other undead creatures, such as <a href="/wiki/Abomination" title="Abomination">abominations</a>.
</p>
<ul><li>Led by <span class="linkicon raceicon"><a href="/wiki/File:IconSmall_Calia.gif" class="image"><img alt="IconSmall Calia.gif" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_Calia.gif/16px-IconSmall_Calia.gif?863991" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a></span><span class="linkicon raceicon"><a href="/wiki/File:IconSmall_Voss.gif" class="image"><img alt="IconSmall Voss.gif" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_Voss.gif/16px-IconSmall_Voss.gif?791677" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a></span> <span class="linkicon raceicon"><a href="/wiki/File:IconSmall_Undead_Male.gif" class="image"><img alt="IconSmall Undead Male.gif" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_Undead_Male.gif/16px-IconSmall_Undead_Male.gif?6cfd1d" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a></span> <span class="linkicon raceicon"><a href="/wiki/File:IconSmall_Undead_Male.gif" class="image"><img alt="IconSmall Undead Male.gif" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_Undead_Male.gif/16px-IconSmall_Undead_Male.gif?6cfd1d" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a></span><span class="linkicon raceicon"><a href="/wiki/File:IconSmall_UndeadElf_Female.gif" class="image"><img alt="IconSmall UndeadElf Female.gif" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_UndeadElf_Female.gif/16px-IconSmall_UndeadElf_Female.gif?5eeeb5" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a></span> the <a href="/wiki/Desolate_Council" title="Desolate Council">Desolate Council</a>.</li></ul>
<ul><li><a href="/wiki/Royal_Apothecary_Society" title="Royal Apothecary Society">Royal Apothecary Society</a> - A faction of Forsaken alchemists trying to create a <a href="/wiki/New_plague" class="mw-redirect" title="New plague">new plague</a> to wipe out the <a href="/wiki/Scourge" title="Scourge">Scourge</a> and, perhaps, humanity.<sup id="cite&#95;ref-98" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-98"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>98<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup>
<ul><li>Led by <span class="linkicon raceicon"><a href="/wiki/File:IconSmall_Undead_Male.gif" class="image"><img alt="IconSmall Undead Male.gif" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_Undead_Male.gif/16px-IconSmall_Undead_Male.gif?6cfd1d" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a></span> <a href="/wiki/Master_Apothecary_Faranell" title="Master Apothecary Faranell">Master Apothecary Faranell</a>. Formerly led by <span class="linkicon raceicon"><a href="/wiki/File:IconSmall_Putress.gif" class="image"><img alt="IconSmall Putress.gif" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_Putress.gif/16px-IconSmall_Putress.gif?fe9948" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a></span> <a href="/wiki/Grand_Apothecary_Putress" title="Grand Apothecary Putress">Grand Apothecary Putress</a>.</li>
<li><span class="linkicon raceicon"><a href="/wiki/File:IconSmall_LeperGnome_Male.gif" class="image"><img alt="IconSmall LeperGnome Male.gif" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_LeperGnome_Male.gif/16px-IconSmall_LeperGnome_Male.gif?48304a" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a></span> <a href="/wiki/Leper_gnome" title="Leper gnome">Leper gnome</a> - A few leper gnomes had sworn fealty to Sylvanas and continue after her betrayal to act as assistants to the apothecaries.</li></ul></li></ul>
<ul><li><a href="/wiki/Queensguard" title="Queensguard">Queensguard</a> - An army formerly led by Warchief Sylvanas in the <a href="/wiki/Broken_Isles" title="Broken Isles">Broken Isles</a>. They are located in <a href="/wiki/Highmountain" title="Highmountain">Highmountain</a>, <a href="/wiki/Val%27sharah" title="Val&#39;sharah">Val'sharah</a>, and <a href="/wiki/Stormheim" title="Stormheim">Stormheim</a> and consist primarily of the Forsaken.
<ul><li>Formerly Led by <span class="linkicon raceicon"><a href="/wiki/File:IconSmall_Sylvanas.gif" class="image"><img alt="IconSmall Sylvanas.gif" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_Sylvanas.gif/16px-IconSmall_Sylvanas.gif?8fee9d" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a></span> <a href="/wiki/Sylvanas_Windrunner" title="Sylvanas Windrunner">Sylvanas Windrunner</a>.</li></ul></li></ul>
<ul><li><a href="/wiki/Deathguard" title="Deathguard">Deathguard</a> - The bulk of the Forsaken military force. They are mostly stationed in the <a href="/wiki/Tirisfal_Glades" title="Tirisfal Glades">Tirisfal Glades</a>, <a href="/wiki/Silverpine_Forest" title="Silverpine Forest">Silverpine Forest</a>, and the <a href="/wiki/Hillsbrad_Foothills" title="Hillsbrad Foothills">Hillsbrad Foothills</a>, as well as Forsaken outposts in <a href="/wiki/Northrend" title="Northrend">Northrend</a>.
<ul><li><a href="/wiki/Dreadguard" title="Dreadguard">Dreadguard</a> - The elite soldiers of Forsaken military. They guard strategically important areas in <a href="/wiki/Lordaeron" title="Lordaeron">Lordaeron</a>.</li></ul></li></ul>
<ul><li><a href="/wiki/Defilers" title="Defilers">Defilers</a> – A force of Forsaken sent to gather the resources of <a href="/wiki/Arathi_Basin" title="Arathi Basin">Arathi Basin</a>, found themselves fighting a vicious war with the <a href="/wiki/League_of_Arathor" title="League of Arathor">League of Arathor</a>, humans who are native to the <a href="/wiki/Arathi_Highlands" title="Arathi Highlands">Arathi Highlands</a> who in turn are fighting to gather resources for the Alliance.
<ul><li>Led by <span class="linkicon raceicon"><a href="/wiki/File:IconSmall_Undead_Female.gif" class="image"><img alt="IconSmall Undead Female.gif" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_Undead_Female.gif/16px-IconSmall_Undead_Female.gif?6e00fb" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a></span> <a href="/wiki/The_Black_Bride" title="The Black Bride">The Black Bride</a>.</li></ul></li></ul>
<ul><li><a href="/wiki/Hand_of_Vengeance" title="Hand of Vengeance">Hand of Vengeance</a> - A group of Forsaken forces that have been sent to Northrend by Sylvanas Windrunner to bring Undercity's vengeance upon the Lich King. Their method is the delivery of the latest strain of their deadly Plague.
<ul><li>Led by <span class="linkicon raceicon"><a href="/wiki/File:IconSmall_Undead_Male.gif" class="image"><img alt="IconSmall Undead Male.gif" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_Undead_Male.gif/16px-IconSmall_Undead_Male.gif?6cfd1d" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a></span> <a href="/wiki/High_Executor_Anselm" title="High Executor Anselm">Anselm</a>, <span class="linkicon raceicon"><a href="/wiki/File:IconSmall_Undead_Male.gif" class="image"><img alt="IconSmall Undead Male.gif" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_Undead_Male.gif/16px-IconSmall_Undead_Male.gif?6cfd1d" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a></span> <a href="/wiki/High_Executor_Wroth" title="High Executor Wroth">Wroth</a> and <span class="linkicon raceicon"><a href="/wiki/File:IconSmall_Undead_Male.gif" class="image"><img alt="IconSmall Undead Male.gif" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_Undead_Male.gif/16px-IconSmall_Undead_Male.gif?6cfd1d" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a></span> <a href="/wiki/Chief_Plaguebringer_Harris" title="Chief Plaguebringer Harris">Harris</a>.</li></ul></li></ul>
<ul><li><a href="/wiki/Cult_of_the_Forgotten_Shadow" class="mw-redirect" title="Cult of the Forgotten Shadow">Cult of the Forgotten Shadow</a> - The official Forsaken religion. There is no official leader but some minor characters have a major influence over it.</li></ul>
<ul><li><a href="/wiki/Deathstalkers" title="Deathstalkers">Deathstalkers</a> - The Forsaken military faction of <a href="/wiki/Assassin" title="Assassin">assassins</a> and <a href="/wiki/Rogue" title="Rogue">rogues</a>.
<ul><li><a href="/wiki/Shadowstalkers" title="Shadowstalkers">Shadowstalkers</a> - The most elite <a href="/wiki/Assassin" title="Assassin">assassins</a> from ranks of the Forsaken. They were sent to <a href="/wiki/Northrend" title="Northrend">Northrend</a> to help <a href="/wiki/Horde_Expedition" title="Horde Expedition">Horde Expedition</a>.</li>
<li>Led by <span class="linkicon raceicon"><a href="/wiki/File:IconSmall_Undead_Male.gif" class="image"><img alt="IconSmall Undead Male.gif" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_Undead_Male.gif/16px-IconSmall_Undead_Male.gif?6cfd1d" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a></span> <a href="/wiki/Deathstalker_Commander_Belmont" title="Deathstalker Commander Belmont">Deathstalker Commander Belmont</a></li></ul></li>
<li><a href="/wiki/Executor" title="Executor">Executors</a> - Military leaders of Forsaken army and overseers over their towns.
<ul><li>Led by <span class="linkicon raceicon"><a href="/wiki/File:IconSmall_Undead_Male.gif" class="image"><img alt="IconSmall Undead Male.gif" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_Undead_Male.gif/16px-IconSmall_Undead_Male.gif?6cfd1d" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a></span> <a href="/wiki/Grand_Executor_Mortuus" class="mw-redirect" title="Grand Executor Mortuus">Grand Executor Mortuus</a> (presumed)</li></ul></li></ul>
<p>The Forsaken employ various different types of undead.
</p>
<ul><li><span class="linkicon raceicon"><a href="/wiki/File:IconSmall_Skeleton.gif" class="image"><img alt="IconSmall Skeleton.gif" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_Skeleton.gif/16px-IconSmall_Skeleton.gif?9748b9" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a></span><span class="linkicon raceicon"><a href="/wiki/File:IconSmall_SkeletalOrc.gif" class="image"><img alt="IconSmall SkeletalOrc.gif" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_SkeletalOrc.gif/16px-IconSmall_SkeletalOrc.gif?1a6082" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a></span> <a href="/wiki/Skeleton" title="Skeleton">Skeleton</a> - Forsaken necromancers are known to summon skeletons to battle.</li>
<li><span class="linkicon raceicon RaceLink"><a href="/wiki/Elf" title="Undead elf"><img alt="Undead elf" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_UndeadElf_Male.gif/16px-IconSmall_UndeadElf_Male.gif?b73611" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a><a href="/wiki/Elf" title="Undead elf"><img alt="Undead elf" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_UndeadElf_Female.gif/16px-IconSmall_UndeadElf_Female.gif?5eeeb5" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a></span><span class="linkicon raceicon"><a href="/wiki/File:IconSmall_BloodElfDeathKnight_Male.gif" class="image"><img alt="IconSmall BloodElfDeathKnight Male.gif" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_BloodElfDeathKnight_Male.gif/16px-IconSmall_BloodElfDeathKnight_Male.gif?af5d27" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a></span><span class="linkicon raceicon"><a href="/wiki/File:IconSmall_BloodElfDeathKnight_Female.gif" class="image"><img alt="IconSmall BloodElfDeathKnight Female.gif" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_BloodElfDeathKnight_Female.gif/16px-IconSmall_BloodElfDeathKnight_Female.gif?c9da7b" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a></span> <a href="/wiki/Darkfallen" title="Darkfallen">Darkfallen</a> - Various former <a href="/wiki/High_elf" title="High elf">high elven</a> citizens continue to serve the Forsaken after the Banshee Queen's betrayal.</li>
<li><span class="linkicon raceicon"><a href="/wiki/File:IconSmall_Banshee.gif" class="image"><img alt="IconSmall Banshee.gif" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_Banshee.gif/16px-IconSmall_Banshee.gif?1e3a79" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a></span> <a href="/wiki/Banshee" title="Banshee">Banshee</a> - <span class="raceicon"></span><a href="/wiki/Sylvanas_Windrunner" title="Sylvanas Windrunner"><span style="color:">Sylvanas</span></a> had many <a href="/wiki/Banshee" title="Banshee">banshee</a> allies and is known as the banshee queen.</li>
<li><span class="linkicon raceicon"><a href="/wiki/File:IconSmall_Abomination.gif" class="image"><img alt="IconSmall Abomination.gif" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_Abomination.gif/16px-IconSmall_Abomination.gif?d9ba61" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a></span> <a href="/wiki/Abomination" title="Abomination">Abomination</a> - The Forsaken keep a large amount of <a href="/wiki/Abomination" title="Abomination">abominations</a> in their service.</li>
<li><span class="linkicon raceicon"><a href="/wiki/File:IconSmall_Ghoul.gif" class="image"><img alt="IconSmall Ghoul.gif" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_Ghoul.gif/16px-IconSmall_Ghoul.gif?6f8f4b" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a></span> <a href="/wiki/Ghoul" title="Ghoul">Ghoul</a> - Sylvanas used ghouls in conquering the <a href="/wiki/Ruins_of_Lordaeron" title="Ruins of Lordaeron">Ruins of Lordaeron</a>.</li>
<li><span class="linkicon raceicon"><a href="/wiki/File:IconSmall_Zombie.gif" class="image"><img alt="IconSmall Zombie.gif" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_Zombie.gif/16px-IconSmall_Zombie.gif?72c724" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a></span> <a href="/wiki/Zombie" title="Zombie">Zombie</a> - The Forsaken have zombies within their ranks such as <a href="/wiki/Algernon" title="Algernon">Algernon</a> and <a href="/wiki/Boyle" title="Boyle">Boyle</a>.</li>
<li><span class="linkicon raceicon"><a href="/wiki/File:IconSmall_Wraith.gif" class="image"><img alt="IconSmall Wraith.gif" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_Wraith.gif/16px-IconSmall_Wraith.gif?c5b400" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a></span> <a href="/wiki/Wraith" title="Wraith">Wraith</a> - <a href="/wiki/Ophelia_Montague" title="Ophelia Montague">Ophelia Montague</a> was a banker in the Undercity, and during the Fourth War wraiths that haunted <a href="/wiki/Dun_Garok" title="Dun Garok">Dun Garok</a> were enslaved by the Forsaken.</li>
<li><span class="linkicon raceicon"><a href="/wiki/File:IconSmall_Gargoyle.gif" class="image"><img alt="IconSmall Gargoyle.gif" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_Gargoyle.gif/16px-IconSmall_Gargoyle.gif?e4fc2a" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a></span> <a href="/wiki/Gargoyle" title="Gargoyle">Gargoyle</a> - The Forsaken were seen employing gargoyles in their <a href="/wiki/Battle_for_Darkshore" title="Battle for Darkshore">fight</a> against the <a href="/wiki/Night_elf" title="Night elf">night elves</a> in <a href="/wiki/Darkshore" title="Darkshore">Darkshore</a>.<sup id="cite&#95;ref-99" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-99"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>99<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup></li>
<li><span class="linkicon raceicon"><a href="/wiki/File:IconSmall_Geist.gif" class="image"><img alt="IconSmall Geist.gif" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_Geist.gif/16px-IconSmall_Geist.gif?992986" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a></span> <a href="/wiki/Geist" title="Geist">Geist</a> - The Forsaken were seen employing geist in their <a href="/wiki/Battle_for_Darkshore" title="Battle for Darkshore">fight</a> against the <a href="/wiki/Night_elf" title="Night elf">night elves</a> in <a href="/wiki/Darkshore" title="Darkshore">Darkshore</a>.</li>
<li><span class="linkicon raceicon"><a href="/wiki/File:IconSmall_UndeadNightElf_Male.gif" class="image"><img alt="IconSmall UndeadNightElf Male.gif" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_UndeadNightElf_Male.gif/16px-IconSmall_UndeadNightElf_Male.gif?f28f3e" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a></span><span class="linkicon raceicon"><a href="/wiki/File:IconSmall_UndeadNightElf_Female.gif" class="image"><img alt="IconSmall UndeadNightElf Female.gif" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_UndeadNightElf_Female.gif/16px-IconSmall_UndeadNightElf_Female.gif?4645af" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a></span><span class="linkicon raceicon"><a href="/wiki/File:IconSmall_DarkWarden.gif" class="image"><img alt="IconSmall DarkWarden.gif" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_DarkWarden.gif/16px-IconSmall_DarkWarden.gif?4eef0b" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a></span> <a href="/wiki/Night_elf" title="Night elf">Undead night elf</a> - These former night elves chose to accept rebirth at the hands of the Val'kyr.<sup id="cite&#95;ref-Dark&#95;Range&#95;Round-Up&#95;100-0" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-Dark_Range_Round-Up-100"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>100<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup></li>
<li><span class="linkicon raceicon"><a href="/wiki/File:IconSmall_FleshMutant.gif" class="image"><img alt="IconSmall FleshMutant.gif" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_FleshMutant.gif/16px-IconSmall_FleshMutant.gif?d723dc" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a></span> <a href="/wiki/Mutated_abomination" title="Mutated abomination">Mutated abomination</a> - The Forsaken used one, <a href="/wiki/Gorerot" title="Gorerot">Gorerot</a>, in the <a href="/wiki/Invasion_of_Gilneas" title="Invasion of Gilneas">invasion of Gilneas</a><sup id="cite&#95;ref-101" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-101"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>101<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup></li>
<li><span class="linkicon raceicon"><a href="/wiki/File:IconSmall_PlagueEruptor.gif" class="image"><img alt="IconSmall PlagueEruptor.gif" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_PlagueEruptor.gif/16px-IconSmall_PlagueEruptor.gif?6221fb" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a></span> <a href="/wiki/Plague_eruptor" title="Plague eruptor">Plague Eruptor</a> - The Forsaken are seen employing them during <a href="/wiki/Faction_Assault_on_Tiragarde_Sound" title="Faction Assault on Tiragarde Sound">Faction Assaults on Tiragarde Sound.</a><sup id="cite&#95;ref-102" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-102"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>102<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup><sup id="cite&#95;ref-103" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-103"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>103<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup></li></ul>
<h4><span id="Kingdom_of_Quel.27Thalas"></span><span class="mw-headline" id="Kingdom_of_Quel'Thalas"><a href="/wiki/World_of_Warcraft:_The_Burning_Crusade" title="Burning Crusade"><img alt="Burning Crusade" src="/images/Bc_icon.gif?6fe702" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="30" height="14" style="vertical-align: middle" data-file-width="30" data-file-height="14" /></a> <a href="/wiki/File:Silvermoon.png" class="image" title="Kingdom of Quel&#39;Thalas"><img alt="Kingdom of Quel&#39;Thalas" src="/images/thumb/Silvermoon.png/30px-Silvermoon.png?e9fc3d" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="30" height="30" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a> <a href="/wiki/Quel%27Thalas_(kingdom)" title="Quel&#39;Thalas (kingdom)">Kingdom of Quel'Thalas</a></span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/wiki/Horde?action=edit&amp;section=23" title="Edit section: Kingdom of Quel&#039;Thalas"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h4>
<div class="thumb tright"><div class="thumbinner" style="width:242px;"><a href="/wiki/File:Icon_of_Blood.png" class="image"><img src="/images/thumb/Icon_of_Blood.png/240px-Icon_of_Blood.png?cdf12d" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="240" height="322" class="thumbimage" data-file-width="280" data-file-height="376" /></a>  <div class="thumbcaption"><div class="magnify"><a href="/wiki/File:Icon_of_Blood.png" class="internal" title="Enlarge"></a></div><a href="/wiki/Icon_of_Blood" title="Icon of Blood">Icon of Blood</a></div></div></div>
<p><span class="linkicon raceicon RaceLink"><a href="/wiki/Blood_elf" title="Blood elf"><img alt="Blood elf" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_BloodElf_Male.png/16px-IconSmall_BloodElf_Male.png?215d28" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a><a href="/wiki/Blood_elf" title="Blood elf"><img alt="Blood elf" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_BloodElf_Female.png/16px-IconSmall_BloodElf_Female.png?681eed" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a></span>&#160;<a href="/wiki/Blood_elf" title="Blood elf">Blood elf</a> - The <a href="/wiki/Sin%27dorei" class="mw-redirect" title="Sin&#39;dorei">sin'dorei</a> of <a href="/wiki/Quel%27Thalas_(kingdom)" title="Quel&#39;Thalas (kingdom)">Quel'Thalas</a> preside over the other major kingdom of the Eastern Horde from their capital of <a href="/wiki/Silvermoon_City" title="Silvermoon City">Silvermoon City</a> in <a href="/wiki/Eversong_Woods_(Burning_Crusade)" title="Eversong Woods (Burning Crusade)">Eversong Woods</a>. After the <a href="/wiki/Scourge_invasion_of_Quel%27Thalas" title="Scourge invasion of Quel&#39;Thalas">Scourge invasion of Quel'Thalas</a> essentially culled the <a href="/wiki/High_elf" title="High elf">high elven</a> race during the Third War, with over 90% of their people killed, the remaining 10% vowed to avenge the destruction of their once-great nation and dubbed themselves the "blood elves" in honor of their fallen brethren. The blood elves worked tirelessly to reclaim their homeland from lingering <a href="/wiki/Scourge" title="Scourge">Scourge</a> and the ever-vengeful <a href="/wiki/Amani_tribe" title="Amani tribe">Amani tribe</a>, with the volatile new powers the <a href="/wiki/Magisters" title="Magisters">magisters</a> advocated, the kingdom of Quel'Thalas was offered aid from an unlikely source - the Forsaken, whose queen, <a href="/wiki/Lady" title="Lady">Lady</a> Sylvanas Windrunner, a former <a href="/wiki/Ranger-general_of_Silvermoon" class="mw-redirect" title="Ranger-general of Silvermoon">Ranger-general of Silvermoon</a> who had lost her life during the Scourge invasion. With their assistance, the blood elves were able to fend off the lingering Scourge in the kingdom and were able to enter the Horde as an equal nation of its own.
</p><p>Quel'Thalas suffered another dark hour in recent years when its twisted scion <a href="/wiki/Kael%27thas_Sunstrider" title="Kael&#39;thas Sunstrider">Kael'thas Sunstrider</a> attacked his homeland in a mad bid to usher the <a href="/wiki/Burning_Legion" title="Burning Legion">Burning Legion</a>'s master into Azeroth. He was put to death for this on <a href="/wiki/Isle_of_Quel%27Danas" title="Isle of Quel&#39;Danas">Quel'Danas</a>, and <a href="/wiki/Lor%27themar_Theron" title="Lor&#39;themar Theron">Lor'themar Theron</a> has thus become the sole leader of the blood elven people. With the <a href="/wiki/Sunwell" title="Sunwell">Sunwell</a> restored and the monumental betrayal of their prince behind them, a bright future now lies ahead for the blood elves of Quel'Thalas.<sup id="cite&#95;ref-104" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-104"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>104<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup> Following the <a href="/wiki/Battle_for_Lordaeron" title="Battle for Lordaeron">Battle for Lordaeron</a>, they are one of the final bastions of Horde power left within the Eastern Kingdoms.
</p>
<ul><li>Led by <span class="linkicon raceicon"><a href="/wiki/File:IconSmall_Lor%27themar.gif" class="image"><img alt="IconSmall Lor&#39;themar.gif" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_Lor%27themar.gif/16px-IconSmall_Lor%27themar.gif?899b90" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a></span> <a href="/wiki/Lor%27themar_Theron" title="Lor&#39;themar Theron">Regent Lord Lor'themar Theron</a>.</li></ul>
<ul><li><a href="/wiki/Magisters" title="Magisters">Magisters</a> - The primary <a href="/wiki/Magic" title="Magic">magic</a> users of Quel'Thalas.
<ul><li>Led by <span class="linkicon raceicon"><a href="/wiki/File:IconSmall_Rommath.gif" class="image"><img alt="IconSmall Rommath.gif" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_Rommath.gif/16px-IconSmall_Rommath.gif?7d7b80" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a></span> <a href="/wiki/Grand_Magister_Rommath" title="Grand Magister Rommath">Grand Magister Rommath</a>.</li></ul></li></ul>
<ul><li><a href="/wiki/Farstriders" title="Farstriders">Farstriders</a> - The ancient order of <a href="/wiki/Thalassian" title="Thalassian">Thalassian</a> rangers. Now blood elves, they are dedicated to safeguarding their homeland from any and all threats, near and far while maintaining the old, nature-revering ways of their people.
<ul><li>Led by <span class="linkicon raceicon"><a href="/wiki/File:IconSmall_Halduron.gif" class="image"><img alt="IconSmall Halduron.gif" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_Halduron.gif/16px-IconSmall_Halduron.gif?9697ec" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a></span> <a href="/wiki/Ranger-general_of_Silvermoon" class="mw-redirect" title="Ranger-general of Silvermoon">Ranger-general</a> <a href="/wiki/Halduron_Brightwing" title="Halduron Brightwing">Halduron Brightwing</a>.</li></ul></li></ul>
<ul><li><a href="/wiki/Blood_Knights" title="Blood Knights">Blood Knights</a> - Silvermoon's order of blood elf paladins formed after the capture of the <a href="/wiki/Naaru" title="Naaru">Naaru</a> <a href="/wiki/M%27uru" title="M&#39;uru">M'uru</a> at <a href="/wiki/Tempest_Keep" title="Tempest Keep">Tempest Keep</a>. Once, they drained power from the Naaru but, after the events at the <a href="/wiki/Sunwell_Plateau" title="Sunwell Plateau">Sunwell Plateau</a> during the <a href="/wiki/Invasion_of_Outland" title="Invasion of Outland">invasion of Outland</a>, the Naaru's spark transformed the <a href="/wiki/Sunwell" title="Sunwell">Sunwell</a> into a font of Holy Light from which these paladins could draw power.
<ul><li>Led by <span class="linkicon raceicon"><a href="/wiki/File:IconSmall_Liadrin.gif" class="image"><img alt="IconSmall Liadrin.gif" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_Liadrin.gif/16px-IconSmall_Liadrin.gif?93c46f" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a></span> <a href="/wiki/Lady_Liadrin" title="Lady Liadrin">Lady Liadrin</a>.</li>
<li>The <a href="/wiki/Sunsworn" title="Sunsworn">Sunsworn</a> - A military force of blood elves on <a href="/wiki/Draenor_(alternate_universe)" title="Draenor (alternate universe)">Draenor</a>. Headed by the Blood Knights, they have combined forces with <a href="/wiki/Farstriders" title="Farstriders">rangers</a> and <a href="/wiki/Magisters" title="Magisters">magi</a> and based themselves in <a href="/wiki/Talador" title="Talador">Talador</a>. They have also deployed the blood elves' recently-acquired <a href="/wiki/Blood_golem" title="Blood golem">blood golem</a> technology.</li></ul></li></ul>
<ul><li><a href="/wiki/Thalassian_navy" title="Thalassian navy">Thalassian navy</a> - The maritime military force of the blood elves. Currently a part of the <a href="/wiki/Horde_navy" title="Horde navy">Horde navy</a>.
<ul><li>Led by <span class="linkicon raceicon"><a href="/wiki/File:IconSmall_Lor%27themar.gif" class="image"><img alt="IconSmall Lor&#39;themar.gif" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_Lor%27themar.gif/16px-IconSmall_Lor%27themar.gif?899b90" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a></span> <a href="/wiki/Lor%27themar_Theron" title="Lor&#39;themar Theron">Regent Lord Lor'themar Theron</a>.</li></ul></li></ul>
<ul><li>The <a href="/wiki/Sunreavers" title="Sunreavers">Sunreavers</a> - A faction of blood elves who represented the Horde in <a href="/wiki/Dalaran" title="Dalaran">Dalaran</a>. Named after their leader, the Sunreavers sought to have the blood elves admitted as members of the <a href="/wiki/Kirin_Tor" title="Kirin Tor">Kirin Tor</a>, and joined the <a href="/wiki/Horde_Expedition" title="Horde Expedition">Horde Expedition</a> in <a href="/wiki/Northrend" title="Northrend">Northrend</a>. They were eventually <a href="/wiki/Purge_of_Dalaran" title="Purge of Dalaran">forced to leave the city</a> some time later after <a href="/wiki/Jaina_Proudmoore" title="Jaina Proudmoore">Jaina Proudmoore</a> came into power.
<ul><li>Led by <span class="linkicon raceicon"><a href="/wiki/File:IconSmall_Aethas.gif" class="image"><img alt="IconSmall Aethas.gif" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_Aethas.gif/16px-IconSmall_Aethas.gif?9e4b4e" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a></span> <a href="/wiki/Aethas_Sunreaver" title="Aethas Sunreaver">Aethas Sunreaver</a></li>
<li>The <a href="/wiki/Sunreaver_Onslaught" title="Sunreaver Onslaught">Sunreaver Onslaught</a> - The Sunreaver Onslaught is the Horde reputation faction introduced in <a href="/wiki/Patch_5.2" class="mw-redirect" title="Patch 5.2">Patch 5.2</a>. It is personally led by <a href="/wiki/Lor%27themar_Theron" title="Lor&#39;themar Theron">Lor'themar Theron</a>, Regent Lord of Quel'Thalas, and bears the namesake of the <a href="/wiki/Sunreavers" title="Sunreavers">Sunreavers</a>.
<ul><li>Led by <span class="linkicon raceicon"><a href="/wiki/File:IconSmall_Lor%27themar.gif" class="image"><img alt="IconSmall Lor&#39;themar.gif" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_Lor%27themar.gif/16px-IconSmall_Lor%27themar.gif?899b90" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a></span> <a href="/wiki/Lor%27themar_Theron" title="Lor&#39;themar Theron">Lor'themar Theron</a></li></ul></li></ul></li></ul>
<ul><li>The <a href="/wiki/Sunfury" title="Sunfury">Sunfury</a> - A ruthless blood elven military force once under the command of <a href="/wiki/Prince" class="mw-redirect" title="Prince">Prince</a> <a href="/wiki/Kael%27thas" class="mw-redirect" title="Kael&#39;thas">Kael'thas</a>. Many have since returned home to Quel'Thalas, following the betrayal of their prince for the <a href="/wiki/Burning_Legion" title="Burning Legion">Burning Legion</a>.<sup id="cite&#95;ref-105" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-105"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>105<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup></li></ul>
<ul><li>The <a href="/wiki/Reliquary" title="Reliquary">Reliquary</a> - A group of examiners and archaeologists out to give the Horde a monopoly on magical artifacts.
<ul><li>Led by <span class="linkicon raceicon"><a href="/wiki/File:IconSmall_BloodElf_Male.png" class="image"><img alt="IconSmall BloodElf Male.png" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_BloodElf_Male.png/16px-IconSmall_BloodElf_Male.png?215d28" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a></span> <a href="/wiki/Tae%27thelan_Bloodwatcher" title="Tae&#39;thelan Bloodwatcher">Tae'thelan Bloodwatcher</a>.</li></ul></li></ul>
<ul><li>The <a href="/wiki/Blood_Hawks" title="Blood Hawks">Blood Hawks</a> - The guards of Eversong Woods.
<ul><li>Led by <span class="linkicon raceicon"><a href="/wiki/File:IconSmall_BloodElf_Male.png" class="image"><img alt="IconSmall BloodElf Male.png" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_BloodElf_Male.png/16px-IconSmall_BloodElf_Male.png?215d28" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a></span> <a href="/wiki/Aeldon_Sunbrand" title="Aeldon Sunbrand">Aeldon Sunbrand</a>.</li></ul></li>
<li>They also have holdings in the <a href="/wiki/Ghostlands" title="Ghostlands">Ghostlands</a>, alongside the Forsaken and an encampment in <a href="/wiki/Hellfire_Peninsula" title="Hellfire Peninsula">Hellfire Peninsula</a> in Outland, to support blood elf pilgrims traveling through the <a href="/wiki/Dark_Portal" title="Dark Portal">Dark Portal</a> from Azeroth. The <a href="/wiki/Reliquary" title="Reliquary">Reliquary</a> has a number of blood elven camps in and adjacent to the <a href="/wiki/Badlands" title="Badlands">Badlands</a>, <a href="/wiki/Tanaris" title="Tanaris">Tanaris</a>, and the <a href="/wiki/Blasted_Lands" title="Blasted Lands">Blasted Lands</a>.</li></ul>
<p><span class="linkicon raceicon"><a href="/wiki/File:IconSmall_BloodElfDemonHunter_Male.gif" class="image"><img alt="IconSmall BloodElfDemonHunter Male.gif" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_BloodElfDemonHunter_Male.gif/16px-IconSmall_BloodElfDemonHunter_Male.gif?284629" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a></span> <span class="linkicon raceicon"><a href="/wiki/File:IconSmall_BloodElfDemonHunter_Female.gif" class="image"><img alt="IconSmall BloodElfDemonHunter Female.gif" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_BloodElfDemonHunter_Female.gif/16px-IconSmall_BloodElfDemonHunter_Female.gif?f19408" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a></span> <a href="/wiki/Demon_hunter" title="Demon hunter">Demon hunters</a> from the <a href="/wiki/Illidari" title="Illidari">Illidari</a> - Although the vast majority appeared to be relatively unaligned, several have joined the Horde as of the <a href="/wiki/Fourth_War" title="Fourth War">Fourth War</a>, fighting alongside Horde forces in <a href="/wiki/Arathi_Basin" title="Arathi Basin">Arathi Basin</a>, including <a href="/wiki/Celaryn_the_Frenzied" title="Celaryn the Frenzied">Celaryn the Frenzied</a> and <a href="/wiki/Sylareon_Silverblade" title="Sylareon Silverblade">Sylareon Silverblade</a>. <a href="/wiki/Elthyn_Da%27rai" title="Elthyn Da&#39;rai">Elthyn Da'rai</a> and <a href="/wiki/Adept_Yad_M%27Sivart" title="Adept Yad M&#39;Sivart">Adept Yad M'Sivart</a> apparently led the Illidari delegation to the Horde, establishing a camp at the <a href="/wiki/Orgrimmar_Skyway" title="Orgrimmar Skyway">Orgrimmar Skyway</a>.<sup id="cite&#95;ref-106" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-106"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>106<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup>
</p>
<h4><span class="mw-headline" id="Bilgewater_Cartel"><a href="/wiki/World_of_Warcraft:_Cataclysm" title="Cataclysm"><img alt="Cataclysm" src="/images/Cata-Logo-Small.png?cd16d8" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="39" height="15" style="vertical-align: middle" data-file-width="39" data-file-height="15" /></a> <a href="/wiki/File:Bilgewater_Cartel.png" class="image" title="Bilgewater Cartel"><img alt="Bilgewater Cartel" src="/images/thumb/Bilgewater_Cartel.png/30px-Bilgewater_Cartel.png?922819" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="30" height="30" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a> <a href="/wiki/Bilgewater_Cartel" title="Bilgewater Cartel">Bilgewater Cartel</a></span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/wiki/Horde?action=edit&amp;section=24" title="Edit section: Bilgewater Cartel"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h4>
<div class="thumb tright"><div class="thumbinner" style="width:242px;"><a href="/wiki/File:Goblin-Icon.png" class="image"><img src="/images/thumb/Goblin-Icon.png/240px-Goblin-Icon.png?af75b3" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="240" height="221" class="thumbimage" data-file-width="810" data-file-height="745" /></a>  <div class="thumbcaption"><div class="magnify"><a href="/wiki/File:Goblin-Icon.png" class="internal" title="Enlarge"></a></div><a href="/wiki/Icon_of_Kezan" title="Icon of Kezan">Icon of Kezan</a></div></div></div>
<p><span class="linkicon raceicon RaceLink"><a href="/wiki/Goblin" title="Goblin"><img alt="Goblin" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_Goblin_Male.gif/16px-IconSmall_Goblin_Male.gif?274386" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a><a href="/wiki/Goblin" title="Goblin"><img alt="Goblin" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_Goblin_Female.gif/16px-IconSmall_Goblin_Female.gif?9787be" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a></span>&#160;<a href="/wiki/Goblin" title="Goblin">Goblins</a> - The cunning <a href="/wiki/Bilgewater_Cartel" title="Bilgewater Cartel">Bilgewater Cartel</a> <a href="/wiki/Goblin" title="Goblin">goblins</a> from <a href="/wiki/Kezan" title="Kezan">Kezan</a>, one of the most (if not the most) powerful goblin cartels that dominates trade in the south seas, have recently joined the Horde. After <a href="/wiki/Deathwing" title="Deathwing">Deathwing</a> assaulted Kezan and the goblins were forced to flee, their first escape ships were attacked by an Alliance fleet in the <a href="/wiki/Battle_of_the_Lost_Isles" title="Battle of the Lost Isles">Battle of the Lost Isles</a>. With a common enemy, the goblins assisted the captive former Warchief <a href="/wiki/Thrall" title="Thrall">Thrall</a> in breaking his bonds and obliterating the Alliance on the <a href="/wiki/Lost_Isles" title="Lost Isles">Lost Isles</a>. Their future was tested when their leader, the angered <a href="/wiki/Trade_Prince_Gallywix" class="mw-redirect" title="Trade Prince Gallywix">Trade Prince Gallywix</a>, attempted to defeat Thrall and take over <a href="/wiki/Azshara" title="Azshara">Azshara</a> for himself, though this ultimately ended in failure. Thrall mercifully allowed him to retain his position as <a href="/wiki/Trade_Prince" class="mw-redirect" title="Trade Prince">Trade Prince</a>, and thus the Bilgewater goblins were accepted into the Horde, founding <a href="/wiki/Bilgewater_Harbor" title="Bilgewater Harbor">Bilgewater Harbor</a> in the heavily-reshaped Azshara and the <a href="/wiki/Goblin_Slums" title="Goblin Slums">Goblin Slums</a> of <a href="/wiki/Orgrimmar" title="Orgrimmar">Orgrimmar</a> led by <a href="/wiki/Boss_Mida" title="Boss Mida">Boss Mida</a>
</p>
<ul><li>Led by <span class="linkicon raceicon"><a href="/wiki/File:IconSmall_Gazlowe.gif" class="image"><img alt="IconSmall Gazlowe.gif" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_Gazlowe.gif/16px-IconSmall_Gazlowe.gif?e74a1d" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a></span> <a href="/wiki/Trade_Prince" class="mw-redirect" title="Trade Prince">Trade Prince</a> <a href="/wiki/Monte_Gazlowe" title="Monte Gazlowe">Monte Gazlowe</a>.</li></ul>
<ul><li><a href="/wiki/Bilgewater_Battalion" title="Bilgewater Battalion">Bilgewater Battalion</a> - The new army and defense force of the <a href="/wiki/Bilgewater_Cartel" title="Bilgewater Cartel">Bilgewater Cartel</a> of <a href="/wiki/Goblin" title="Goblin">goblins</a> created after the cartel pledged allegiance to the Horde.
