<ul><li>Led by <span class="linkicon raceicon"><a href="/wiki/File:IconSmall_Goblin_Male.gif" class="image"><img alt="IconSmall Goblin Male.gif" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_Goblin_Male.gif/16px-IconSmall_Goblin_Male.gif?274386" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a></span> <a href="/wiki/Uncle_Bedlam" title="Uncle Bedlam">Uncle Bedlam</a>.</li></ul></li></ul>
<ul><li><a href="/wiki/Gob_Squad" title="Gob Squad">Gob Squad</a> - The Gob Squad is an elite goblin squadron working for the Bilgewater Cartel.
<ul><li>Led by <span class="linkicon raceicon"><a href="/wiki/File:IconSmall_Goblin_Male.gif" class="image"><img alt="IconSmall Goblin Male.gif" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_Goblin_Male.gif/16px-IconSmall_Goblin_Male.gif?274386" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a></span> <a href="/wiki/Patch_(goblin)" title="Patch (goblin)">Patch</a>.</li></ul></li></ul>
<ul><li><span class="linkicon raceicon"><a href="/wiki/File:IconSmall_Hobgoblin.gif" class="image"><img alt="IconSmall Hobgoblin.gif" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_Hobgoblin.gif/16px-IconSmall_Hobgoblin.gif?442984" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a></span> <a href="/wiki/Hobgoblin" title="Hobgoblin">Hobgoblins</a> - Huge chemically altered goblins have come with the goblins. In-game goblins can summon them to access the <a href="/wiki/Bank" title="Bank">bank</a>.</li></ul>
<h4><span class="mw-headline" id="Huojin_Pandaren"><a href="/wiki/World_of_Warcraft:_Mists_of_Pandaria" title="Mists of Pandaria"><img alt="Mists of Pandaria" src="/images/Mists-Logo-Small.png?f96b23" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="46" height="14" style="vertical-align: middle" data-file-width="46" data-file-height="14" /></a> <a href="/wiki/File:Huojin.png" class="image" title="Huojin Pandaren"><img alt="Huojin Pandaren" src="/images/thumb/Huojin.png/30px-Huojin.png?354387" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="30" height="30" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a> <a href="/wiki/Huojin_Pandaren" title="Huojin Pandaren">Huojin Pandaren</a></span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/wiki/Horde?action=edit&amp;section=25" title="Edit section: Huojin Pandaren"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h4>
<div class="thumb tright"><div class="thumbinner" style="width:242px;"><a href="/wiki/File:Pandaren_Crest.png" class="image"><img src="/images/thumb/Pandaren_Crest.png/240px-Pandaren_Crest.png?3971fe" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="240" height="240" class="thumbimage" data-file-width="350" data-file-height="350" /></a>  <div class="thumbcaption"><div class="magnify"><a href="/wiki/File:Pandaren_Crest.png" class="internal" title="Enlarge"></a></div><a href="/wiki/Icon_of_Pandaria" title="Icon of Pandaria">Icon of Pandaria</a></div></div></div>
<p><span class="linkicon raceicon"><a href="/wiki/File:IconSmall_Pandaren2_Male.gif" class="image"><img alt="IconSmall Pandaren2 Male.gif" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_Pandaren2_Male.gif/16px-IconSmall_Pandaren2_Male.gif?5e26f8" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a></span><span class="linkicon raceicon"><a href="/wiki/File:IconSmall_Pandaren2_Female.gif" class="image"><img alt="IconSmall Pandaren2 Female.gif" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_Pandaren2_Female.gif/16px-IconSmall_Pandaren2_Female.gif?ba611a" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a></span> <a href="/wiki/Pandaren" title="Pandaren">Pandaren</a> - These pandaren who hail from the <a href="/wiki/Wandering_Isle" title="Wandering Isle">Wandering Isle</a> follow the path of the <a href="/wiki/Huojin" class="mw-redirect" title="Huojin">Huojin</a>, a philosophy among the pandaren that inaction is the greatest injustice, and that there is no shame in defending one's homes and loved ones regardless of cost. Those pandaren who follow the path of Huojin are allies of the <a class="mw-selflink selflink">Horde</a>, and are represented and led by the intrepid <a href="/wiki/Ji_Firepaw" title="Ji Firepaw">Ji Firepaw</a>, a staunch adherent of this philosophy.<sup id="cite&#95;ref-mmo-c&#95;press&#95;tour&#95;19&#95;march&#95;107-0" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-mmo-c_press_tour_19_march-107"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>107<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup>
</p><p>The Huojin are quick to act and quicker to fight for what they believe in. Impulsive and practical, they feel that the ends can justify the means.<sup id="cite&#95;ref-mmo-c&#95;press&#95;tour&#95;19&#95;march&#95;107-1" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-mmo-c_press_tour_19_march-107"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>107<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup> These followers now integrated into the Horde community and have a small base of operations in Orgrimmar's Valley of Honor.
</p>
<ul><li>Led by <span class="linkicon raceicon"><a href="/wiki/File:IconSmall_Ji.gif" class="image"><img alt="IconSmall Ji.gif" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_Ji.gif/16px-IconSmall_Ji.gif?9062ca" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a></span> <a href="/wiki/Ji_Firepaw" title="Ji Firepaw">Master Ji Firepaw</a></li></ul>
<h4><span class="mw-headline" id="Kingdom_of_Suramar"><a href="/wiki/World_of_Warcraft:_Battle_for_Azeroth" title="Battle for Azeroth"><img alt="Battle for Azeroth" src="/images/thumb/BattleForAzeroth-Logo-Small.png/40px-BattleForAzeroth-Logo-Small.png?25e36c" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="40" height="15" style="vertical-align: middle" data-file-width="80" data-file-height="29" /></a> <a href="/wiki/File:Nightborne_Crest.png" class="image" title="Kingdom of Suramar"><img alt="Kingdom of Suramar" src="/images/thumb/Nightborne_Crest.png/30px-Nightborne_Crest.png?5e82ca" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="30" height="38" data-file-width="533" data-file-height="675" /></a> <a href="/wiki/Suramar_(kingdom)" title="Suramar (kingdom)">Kingdom of Suramar</a></span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/wiki/Horde?action=edit&amp;section=26" title="Edit section: Kingdom of Suramar"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h4>
<div class="thumb tright"><div class="thumbinner" style="width:242px;"><a href="/wiki/File:Nightborne_Crest.png" class="image"><img src="/images/thumb/Nightborne_Crest.png/240px-Nightborne_Crest.png?5e82ca" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="240" height="304" class="thumbimage" data-file-width="533" data-file-height="675" /></a>  <div class="thumbcaption"><div class="magnify"><a href="/wiki/File:Nightborne_Crest.png" class="internal" title="Enlarge"></a></div>The nightborne crest</div></div></div>
<p><span class="linkicon raceicon RaceLink"><a href="/wiki/Nightborne" title="Nightborne"><img alt="Nightborne" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_Nightborne_Male.gif/16px-IconSmall_Nightborne_Male.gif?ad661a" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a><a href="/wiki/Nightborne" title="Nightborne"><img alt="Nightborne" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_Nightborne_Female.gif/16px-IconSmall_Nightborne_Female.gif?4a121f" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a></span>&#160;<a href="/wiki/Nightborne" title="Nightborne">Nightborne</a> - The <a href="/wiki/Shal%27dorei" class="mw-redirect" title="Shal&#39;dorei">shal'dorei</a> are a powerful and mystical race of <a href="/wiki/Elf" title="Elf">night elves</a> who live in <a href="/wiki/Suramar_City" title="Suramar City">Suramar City</a> in <a href="/wiki/Suramar" title="Suramar">Suramar</a>. Since the city was first separated from the rest of the world over 10,000 years ago, isolated behind a protective barrier, they have evolved by the <a href="/wiki/Nightwell" title="Nightwell">Nightwell</a> into an arcane-enhanced version of their former selves, and in the process, grew increasingly dependent upon its arcane magic. To protect this font of power, the leaders of the nightborne struck a bargain with the Burning Legion that plunged their kingdom into civil war. After fighting for freedom from their demonic masters, the nightborne seek allies to help them reclaim their place in the world.
</p><p>After the death of <a href="/wiki/Elisande" title="Elisande">Grand Magistrix Elisande</a> and the end of the <a href="/wiki/Third_invasion_of_the_Burning_Legion" title="Third invasion of the Burning Legion">third invasion of the Burning Legion</a>, the shal'dorei wished to rejoin the world. After a harsh exchange between <a href="/wiki/Thalyssra" title="Thalyssra">Thalyssra</a> and <a href="/wiki/Tyrande_Whisperwind" title="Tyrande Whisperwind">Tyrande Whisperwind</a>, and an offer of mutual friendship from <a href="/wiki/Lady_Liadrin" title="Lady Liadrin">Lady Liadrin</a>, the shal'dorei leader decided that it would be best to ally with the Horde, who share ideals similar to those of the nightborne.<sup id="cite&#95;ref-108" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-108"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>108<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup>
</p>
<ul><li>Led by <span class="linkicon raceicon"><a href="/wiki/File:IconSmall_Thalyssra.gif" class="image"><img alt="IconSmall Thalyssra.gif" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_Thalyssra.gif/16px-IconSmall_Thalyssra.gif?86e008" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a></span> <a href="/wiki/First_Arcanist_Thalyssra" class="mw-redirect" title="First Arcanist Thalyssra">First Arcanist Thalyssra</a>.</li></ul>
<ul><li>The <a href="/wiki/Duskwatch" title="Duskwatch">Duskwatch</a> - The primary body responsible for maintaining law and order in the city of <a href="/wiki/Suramar_City" title="Suramar City">Suramar</a>. Boasting a wide array of skilled mages and warriors alike, the Duskwatch represents the bulk of <a href="/wiki/Nightborne" title="Nightborne">Nightborne</a> armed forces.
<ul><li>Led by <span class="linkicon raceicon"><a href="/wiki/File:IconSmall_Thalyssra.gif" class="image"><img alt="IconSmall Thalyssra.gif" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_Thalyssra.gif/16px-IconSmall_Thalyssra.gif?86e008" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a></span> <a href="/wiki/First_Arcanist_Thalyssra" class="mw-redirect" title="First Arcanist Thalyssra">First Arcanist Thalyssra</a> and secondarily by <span class="linkicon raceicon"><a href="/wiki/File:IconSmall_Nightborne_Female.gif" class="image"><img alt="IconSmall Nightborne Female.gif" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_Nightborne_Female.gif/16px-IconSmall_Nightborne_Female.gif?4a121f" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a></span> <a href="/wiki/Victoire" title="Victoire">First Blade Victoire</a>.</li></ul></li></ul>
<h4><span class="mw-headline" id="Highmountain_tauren_tribes"><a href="/wiki/World_of_Warcraft:_Battle_for_Azeroth" title="Battle for Azeroth"><img alt="Battle for Azeroth" src="/images/thumb/BattleForAzeroth-Logo-Small.png/40px-BattleForAzeroth-Logo-Small.png?25e36c" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="40" height="15" style="vertical-align: middle" data-file-width="80" data-file-height="29" /></a> <a href="/wiki/File:Highmountain_Tauren_Crest.png" class="image" title="Highmountain Tribe"><img alt="Highmountain Tribe" src="/images/thumb/Highmountain_Tauren_Crest.png/30px-Highmountain_Tauren_Crest.png?c34dda" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="30" height="32" data-file-width="632" data-file-height="675" /></a> <a href="/wiki/Highmountain_Tribe" title="Highmountain Tribe">Highmountain tauren tribes</a></span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/wiki/Horde?action=edit&amp;section=27" title="Edit section: Highmountain tauren tribes"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h4>
<div class="thumb tright"><div class="thumbinner" style="width:242px;"><a href="/wiki/File:Highmountain_Tauren_Crest.png" class="image"><img src="/images/thumb/Highmountain_Tauren_Crest.png/240px-Highmountain_Tauren_Crest.png?c34dda" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="240" height="256" class="thumbimage" data-file-width="632" data-file-height="675" /></a>  <div class="thumbcaption"><div class="magnify"><a href="/wiki/File:Highmountain_Tauren_Crest.png" class="internal" title="Enlarge"></a></div>The Highmountain tauren crest</div></div></div>
<p><span class="linkicon raceicon RaceLink"><a href="/wiki/Highmountain_tauren" title="Highmountain tauren"><img alt="Highmountain tauren" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_Highmountain_Male.gif/16px-IconSmall_Highmountain_Male.gif?bff9a3" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a><a href="/wiki/Highmountain_tauren" title="Highmountain tauren"><img alt="Highmountain tauren" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_Highmountain_Female.gif/16px-IconSmall_Highmountain_Female.gif?a3985a" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a></span>&#160;<a href="/wiki/Highmountain_tauren" title="Highmountain tauren">Highmountain tauren</a> - The Highmountain tauren are found in the region of <a href="/wiki/Highmountain" title="Highmountain">Highmountain</a> on the <a href="/wiki/Broken_Isles" title="Broken Isles">Broken Isles</a> in their capital of <a href="/wiki/Thunder_Totem" title="Thunder Totem">Thunder Totem</a>. Divided into four tribes (<a href="/wiki/Highmountain_Tribe" title="Highmountain Tribe">Highmountain</a>, <a href="/wiki/Rivermane_tribe" title="Rivermane tribe">Rivermane</a>, <a href="/wiki/Skyhorn_tribe" title="Skyhorn tribe">Skyhorn</a> and <a href="/wiki/Bloodtotem_tribe" title="Bloodtotem tribe">Bloodtotem</a>), they are the direct descendants of the tauren led by <a href="/wiki/Huln_Highmountain" title="Huln Highmountain">Huln Highmountain</a> who assisted the <a href="/wiki/Kaldorei_Resistance" title="Kaldorei Resistance">Kaldorei Resistance</a> during the <a href="/wiki/War_of_the_Ancients" title="War of the Ancients">War of the Ancients</a>.<sup id="cite&#95;ref-BlizzCon&#95;109-0" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-BlizzCon-109"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>109<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup> During the war, Huln received a blessing from <a href="/wiki/Cenarius" title="Cenarius">Cenarius</a>, so that his people would forever have the demigod's favor, granting them the <a href="/wiki/Horns_of_Eche%27ro" class="mw-redirect" title="Horns of Eche&#39;ro">Horns of Eche'ro</a>: <a href="/wiki/Moose" title="Moose">moose</a>-like antlers.<sup id="cite&#95;ref-Stormrage&#95;110-0" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-Stormrage-110"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>110<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup> The Highmountain tauren honor the spirits of earth, river, and sky. 
</p>
<ul><li>Led by <span class="linkicon raceicon"><a href="/wiki/File:IconSmall_Mayla.gif" class="image"><img alt="IconSmall Mayla.gif" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_Mayla.gif/16px-IconSmall_Mayla.gif?292d64" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a></span> <a href="/wiki/Mayla_Highmountain" title="Mayla Highmountain">High Chieftain Mayla Highmountain</a>.</li></ul>
<ul><li><a href="/wiki/Highmountain_Tribe" title="Highmountain Tribe">Highmountain Tribe</a> - The dominant tribe of the Highmountain tauren of the Broken Isles, they are led by <a href="/wiki/Mayla_Highmountain" title="Mayla Highmountain">Mayla Highmountain</a>.
<ul><li>Led by <span class="linkicon raceicon"><a href="/wiki/File:IconSmall_Mayla.gif" class="image"><img alt="IconSmall Mayla.gif" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_Mayla.gif/16px-IconSmall_Mayla.gif?292d64" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a></span> <a href="/wiki/Mayla_Highmountain" title="Mayla Highmountain">Mayla Highmountain</a>.</li></ul></li></ul>
<ul><li><a href="/wiki/Rivermane_tribe" title="Rivermane tribe">Rivermane tribe</a> - They are a highly peaceful and diplomatic tribe, focused on fishing, agriculture, and <a href="/wiki/Shaman" title="Shaman">shamanism</a>. In exchange for protection and equipment, they provide the other tribes with food and healing.<sup id="cite&#95;ref-Ormgul&#95;111-0" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-Ormgul-111"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>111<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup>
<ul><li>Led by <span class="linkicon raceicon"><a href="/wiki/File:IconSmall_Jale.gif" class="image"><img alt="IconSmall Jale.gif" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_Jale.gif/16px-IconSmall_Jale.gif?31a1cc" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a></span>  <a href="/wiki/Jale_Rivermane" title="Jale Rivermane">Jale Rivermane</a></li></ul></li></ul>
<ul><li><a href="/wiki/Skyhorn_tribe" title="Skyhorn tribe">Skyhorn tribe</a> - A proud people, the Skyhorn sports some of the greatest pathfinders and hunters Azeroth has ever known.
<ul><li>Led by <span class="linkicon raceicon"><a href="/wiki/File:IconSmall_Lasan.gif" class="image"><img alt="IconSmall Lasan.gif" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_Lasan.gif/16px-IconSmall_Lasan.gif?354e0f" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a></span> <a href="/wiki/Lasan_Skyhorn" title="Lasan Skyhorn">Lasan Skyhorn</a>.</li></ul></li></ul>
<ul><li><a href="/wiki/Bloodtotem_tribe" title="Bloodtotem tribe">Bloodtotem tribe</a> - During <a href="/wiki/Third_invasion_of_the_Burning_Legion" title="Third invasion of the Burning Legion">third invasion of the Burning Legion</a> the Bloodtotem pledged their allegiance to the <a href="/wiki/Burning_Legion" title="Burning Legion">Burning Legion</a> and became Fel Totem. However, in the aftermath of the Legion's defeat on Argus, Bloodtotem who rejected to serve the Legion have emerged and rejoined their people at <a href="/wiki/Thunder_Totem" title="Thunder Totem">Thunder Totem</a>.
<ul><li>Led by: Unknown</li></ul></li></ul>
<h4><span id="Mag.27har_Clans"></span><span class="mw-headline" id="Mag'har_Clans"><a href="/wiki/World_of_Warcraft:_Battle_for_Azeroth" title="Battle for Azeroth"><img alt="Battle for Azeroth" src="/images/thumb/BattleForAzeroth-Logo-Small.png/40px-BattleForAzeroth-Logo-Small.png?25e36c" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="40" height="15" style="vertical-align: middle" data-file-width="80" data-file-height="29" /></a> <a href="/wiki/File:Mag%27har_crest.png" class="image" title="Mag&#39;har Clans"><img alt="Mag&#39;har Clans" src="/images/thumb/Mag%27har_crest.png/30px-Mag%27har_crest.png?9e3941" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="30" height="33" data-file-width="302" data-file-height="335" /></a> <a href="/wiki/Mag%27har_(alternate_universe)" class="mw-redirect" title="Mag&#39;har (alternate universe)">Mag'har Clans</a></span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/wiki/Horde?action=edit&amp;section=28" title="Edit section: Mag&#039;har Clans"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h4>
<div class="thumb tright"><div class="thumbinner" style="width:242px;"><a href="/wiki/File:Mag%27har_crest.png" class="image"><img src="/images/thumb/Mag%27har_crest.png/240px-Mag%27har_crest.png?9e3941" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="240" height="266" class="thumbimage" data-file-width="302" data-file-height="335" /></a>  <div class="thumbcaption"><div class="magnify"><a href="/wiki/File:Mag%27har_crest.png" class="internal" title="Enlarge"></a></div>The Mag'har crest</div></div></div>
<p><span class="linkicon raceicon RaceLink"><a href="/wiki/Mag%27har_orc" title="Mag&#39;har orc"><img alt="Mag&#39;har orc" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_OrcBrown_Male.gif/16px-IconSmall_OrcBrown_Male.gif?16fed8" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a><a href="/wiki/Mag%27har_orc" title="Mag&#39;har orc"><img alt="Mag&#39;har orc" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_OrcBrown_Female.gif/16px-IconSmall_OrcBrown_Female.gif?be2b11" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a></span><span class="linkicon raceicon"><a href="/wiki/File:IconSmall_OrcGray_Male.gif" class="image"><img alt="IconSmall OrcGray Male.gif" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_OrcGray_Male.gif/16px-IconSmall_OrcGray_Male.gif?468e00" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a></span><span class="linkicon raceicon"><a href="/wiki/File:IconSmall_OrcGray_Female.gif" class="image"><img alt="IconSmall OrcGray Female.gif" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_OrcGray_Female.gif/16px-IconSmall_OrcGray_Female.gif?46550e" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a></span> <a href="/wiki/Mag%27har_orc" title="Mag&#39;har orc">Mag'har orc</a> - The Mag'har are a noble race with a <a href="/wiki/Shaman" title="Shaman">shamanistic</a> tradition cultivating a mighty tribal society that was centered around survival, regulating themselves through ritualized combat and personal honor. For untold generations, the orc clans of Draenor battled one another in endless war, but when <a href="/wiki/Gul%27dan_(alternate_universe)" title="Gul&#39;dan (alternate universe)">Gul'dan</a> offered them <a href="/wiki/Blood-curse" title="Blood-curse">the blood of his demonic masters</a>, the disparate tribes of Mag'har — the orcish word for "uncorrupted" — refused the dark bargain and banded together to drive out the <a href="/wiki/Burning_Legion" title="Burning Legion">Burning Legion</a>, after their victory, the clans stood united as one tribe under the leadership of <a href="/wiki/Grommash_Hellscream_(alternate_universe)" title="Grommash Hellscream (alternate universe)">Grommash Hellscream</a>.
</p><p>After their escape from <a href="/wiki/Draenor_(alternate_universe)" title="Draenor (alternate universe)">Draenor</a> and their <a href="/wiki/Grommash_Hellscream_(alternate_universe)" title="Grommash Hellscream (alternate universe)">Warchief's</a> sacrifice against the fanatical crusade of the <a href="/wiki/Lightbound" title="Lightbound">Lightbound</a>, the clans pledged their loyalty to the Horde. 
</p><p>Amid the armistice at the end of the <a href="/wiki/Fourth_War" title="Fourth War">Fourth War</a>, the Mag'har Clans were granted <a href="/wiki/Hammerfall" title="Hammerfall">Hammerfall</a> in the <a href="/wiki/Arathi_Highlands" title="Arathi Highlands">Arathi Highlands</a> as a place for their people to make a fresh start on Azeroth.
</p>
<ul><li>Led by <span class="linkicon raceicon"><a href="/wiki/File:IconSmall_Geya%27rah.gif" class="image"><img alt="IconSmall Geya&#39;rah.gif" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_Geya%27rah.gif/16px-IconSmall_Geya%27rah.gif?4d7876" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a></span> <a href="/wiki/Overlord_Geya%27rah" class="mw-redirect" title="Overlord Geya&#39;rah">Overlord Geya'rah</a>.</li></ul>
<ul><li><a href="/wiki/Blackrock_clan_(alternate_universe)" title="Blackrock clan (alternate universe)">Blackrock clan</a> - A disciplined, organized, and militaristic clan who boast expert smiths and metalworkers. Well-armored, they utilize brute strength and weaponry to destroy their foes.
<ul><li>Led by: Unknown</li></ul></li></ul>
<ul><li><a href="/wiki/Bleeding_Hollow_clan_(alternate_universe)" title="Bleeding Hollow clan (alternate universe)">Bleeding Hollow clan</a> - A fanatical and crude clan who fight with a berserker fury and gain the boon of prophecy from their elders, who sacrifice an eye to see into the future.
<ul><li>Led by: Unknown</li></ul></li></ul>
<ul><li><a href="/wiki/Burning_Blade_clan_(alternate_universe)" title="Burning Blade clan (alternate universe)">Burning Blade clan</a> - A powerful clan known for their deadly <a href="/wiki/Blademaster" title="Blademaster">blademasters</a>.
<ul><li>Led by: Unknown</li></ul></li></ul>
<ul><li><a href="/wiki/Frostwolf_Orcs" title="Frostwolf Orcs">Frostwolf clan</a> - A strong, close-knit clan renowned for their prowess as one-on-one combatants and their bond with wolves only adds to their strength, though their numbers remain smaller than most clans. The clan spends much of their time in Frostfire Ridge, they migrate to Nagrand with the seasons.
<ul><li>Led by: <span class="linkicon raceicon"><a href="/wiki/File:IconSmall_Geya%27rah.gif" class="image"><img alt="IconSmall Geya&#39;rah.gif" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_Geya%27rah.gif/16px-IconSmall_Geya%27rah.gif?4d7876" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a></span> <a href="/wiki/Overlord_Geya%27rah" class="mw-redirect" title="Overlord Geya&#39;rah">Overlord Geya'rah</a></li></ul></li></ul>
<ul><li><a href="/wiki/Laughing_Skull_Orcs" title="Laughing Skull Orcs">Laughing Skull clan</a> - Known for their violent and bloodthirsty nature, these orcs take great pride in their clan identity. In an attempt to avoid extinction from the <a href="/wiki/Iron_Horde" title="Iron Horde">Iron Horde</a>, the Laughing Skulls have allied with the Frostwolves and the Horde.
<ul><li>Led by <span class="linkicon raceicon"><a href="/wiki/File:IconSmall_Dragonmaw_Female.gif" class="image"><img alt="IconSmall Dragonmaw Female.gif" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_Dragonmaw_Female.gif/16px-IconSmall_Dragonmaw_Female.gif?ee20c1" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a></span> <a href="/wiki/Kaz_the_Shrieker" title="Kaz the Shrieker">Kaz the Shrieker</a> (presumed)</li></ul></li></ul>
<ul><li><a href="/wiki/Shadowmoon_clan_(alternate_universe)" title="Shadowmoon clan (alternate universe)">Shadowmoon clan</a> - A highly spiritual clan, they have always spoken the language of the stars and the earth.
<ul><li>Led by: Unknown</li></ul></li></ul>
<ul><li><a href="/wiki/Shattered_Hand_clan_(alternate_universe)" title="Shattered Hand clan (alternate universe)">Shattered Hand clan</a> - They are sadistic warriors who maim their bodies, perhaps in a show of solidarity or perhaps because they simply enjoy the pain.
<ul><li>Led by: Unknown</li></ul></li></ul>
<ul><li><a href="/wiki/Thunderlord_clan_(alternate_universe)" title="Thunderlord clan (alternate universe)">Thunderlord clan</a> - The clan is known for their excellent <a href="/wiki/Hunters" class="mw-redirect" title="Hunters">hunters</a>, who are specialized in hunting down (and even taming) <a href="/wiki/Rylak" title="Rylak">rylaks</a> and other beasts, and even <a href="/wiki/Gronn" title="Gronn">gronn</a> and <a href="/wiki/Magnaron" title="Magnaron">magnaron</a>.
<ul><li>Led by: Unknown</li></ul></li></ul>
<ul><li><a href="/wiki/Warsong_clan_(alternate_universe)" title="Warsong clan (alternate universe)">Warsong clan</a> - A warlike orcish clan, their war cries inspire their allies and strike fear into all those who dare get in their way.
<ul><li>Led by: Unknown</li></ul></li></ul>
<ul><li><span class="linkicon raceicon"><a href="/wiki/File:IconSmall_Ogre2.gif" class="image"><img alt="IconSmall Ogre2.gif" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_Ogre2.gif/16px-IconSmall_Ogre2.gif?931cca" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a></span><span class="linkicon raceicon"><a href="/wiki/File:IconSmall_OgreEye.gif" class="image"><img alt="IconSmall OgreEye.gif" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_OgreEye.gif/16px-IconSmall_OgreEye.gif?368c7f" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a></span> <a href="/wiki/Stonemaul_clan_(alternate_universe)" title="Stonemaul clan (alternate universe)">Stonemaul clan</a> - This clan of <a href="/wiki/Ogre" title="Ogre">ogres</a> was subdued by the Mag'har to further strengthen their forces
<ul><li>Led by: Unknown</li></ul></li></ul>
<ul><li><span class="linkicon raceicon"><a href="/wiki/File:IconSmall_Gronn.gif" class="image"><img alt="IconSmall Gronn.gif" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_Gronn.gif/16px-IconSmall_Gronn.gif?42e4a7" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a></span> <a href="/wiki/Gronn" title="Gronn">Gronn</a> - The Mag'har have managed to subjugate and strapping great cannons to their backs using harnesses engineered in such a way that the gronn hardly notice the weight and can fire the cannons at will.</li></ul>
<ul><li><span class="linkicon raceicon"><a href="/wiki/File:IconSmall_Gronnling.gif" class="image"><img alt="IconSmall Gronnling.gif" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_Gronnling.gif/16px-IconSmall_Gronnling.gif?705f1c" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a></span> <a href="/wiki/Gronnling" title="Gronnling">Gronnling</a> - The Mag'har have managed to subjugate them to strengthen their army.</li></ul>
<ul><li><span class="linkicon raceicon"><a href="/wiki/File:IconSmall_Ogron.gif" class="image"><img alt="IconSmall Ogron.gif" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_Ogron.gif/16px-IconSmall_Ogron.gif?426ec3" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a></span> <a href="/wiki/Ogron" title="Ogron">Ogron</a> - The Mag'har have managed to subjugate them to strengthen the might of their army.</li></ul>
<ul><li><span class="linkicon raceicon"><a href="/wiki/File:IconSmall_Goren.gif" class="image"><img alt="IconSmall Goren.gif" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_Goren.gif/16px-IconSmall_Goren.gif?c7dd63" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a></span> <a href="/wiki/Goren" title="Goren">Goren</a> - The Mag'har have managed to subjugate them to strengthen their army and fight in the defense of Zuldadar.</li></ul>
<ul><li><span class="linkicon raceicon"><a href="/wiki/File:IconSmall_SavageWolf.gif" class="image"><img alt="IconSmall SavageWolf.gif" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_SavageWolf.gif/16px-IconSmall_SavageWolf.gif?66ceff" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a></span> <a href="/wiki/Dire_wolf" title="Dire wolf">Dire wolf</a> - These wolves are preferred by the Mag'har for their ferocity, loyalty, and for something to snuggle on those cool winter nights.</li></ul>
<ul><li><span class="linkicon raceicon"><a href="/wiki/File:IconSmall_Clefthoof2.gif" class="image"><img alt="IconSmall Clefthoof2.gif" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_Clefthoof2.gif/16px-IconSmall_Clefthoof2.gif?df6b26" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a></span> <a href="/wiki/Clefthoof" title="Clefthoof">Clefthoof</a> - The Mag'har bred clefthooves not only as sturdy pack animals but also powerful instruments of war.</li></ul>
<h4><span class="mw-headline" id="Zandalari_Empire"><a href="/wiki/World_of_Warcraft:_Battle_for_Azeroth" title="Battle for Azeroth"><img alt="Battle for Azeroth" src="/images/thumb/BattleForAzeroth-Logo-Small.png/40px-BattleForAzeroth-Logo-Small.png?25e36c" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="40" height="15" style="vertical-align: middle" data-file-width="80" data-file-height="29" /></a> <a href="/wiki/File:Zandalari_crest.png" class="image" title="Zandalari Empire"><img alt="Zandalari Empire" src="/images/thumb/Zandalari_crest.png/30px-Zandalari_crest.png?2bb7b8" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="30" height="37" data-file-width="2354" data-file-height="2914" /></a> <a href="/wiki/Zandalari_Empire" title="Zandalari Empire">Zandalari Empire</a></span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/wiki/Horde?action=edit&amp;section=29" title="Edit section: Zandalari Empire"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h4>
<div class="thumb tright"><div class="thumbinner" style="width:242px;"><a href="/wiki/File:Zandalari_crest.png" class="image"><img src="/images/thumb/Zandalari_crest.png/240px-Zandalari_crest.png?2bb7b8" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="240" height="297" class="thumbimage" data-file-width="2354" data-file-height="2914" /></a>  <div class="thumbcaption"><div class="magnify"><a href="/wiki/File:Zandalari_crest.png" class="internal" title="Enlarge"></a></div>The Zandalari crest</div></div></div>
<p><span class="linkicon raceicon RaceLink"><a href="/wiki/Zandalari_troll" title="Zandalari troll"><img alt="Zandalari troll" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_Zandalari_Male.gif/16px-IconSmall_Zandalari_Male.gif?5daf82" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a><a href="/wiki/Zandalari_troll" title="Zandalari troll"><img alt="Zandalari troll" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_Zandalari_Female.gif/16px-IconSmall_Zandalari_Female.gif?3f392f" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a></span>&#160;<a href="/wiki/Zandalari_troll" title="Zandalari troll">Zandalari troll</a>- The Zandalari trolls in <a href="/wiki/Dazar%27alor" title="Dazar&#39;alor">Dazar'alor</a> of <a href="/wiki/Zuldazar" title="Zuldazar">Zuldazar</a> are a powerful and superstitious tribe descended from the first troll tribes, they settled on the highest peaks of this mountain range populated with <a href="/wiki/Wild_God" title="Wild God">loa</a>, where they built crude shrines that eventually became the massive temple city of <a href="/wiki/Zuldazar" title="Zuldazar">Zuldazar</a>. These trolls are scholarly and value knowledge; they preserve troll history but are also deeply steeped in ritual and superstition. They are also masters of war and strategy, having led the trolls in many battles before, and having for many millennia tamed massive <a href="/wiki/Dinosaur" title="Dinosaur">dinosaurs</a>.<sup id="cite&#95;ref-Chronicle&#95;112-0" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-Chronicle-112"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>112<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup> Currently they possess one of the most formidable fleets in the known world, using it in their work to further the goals of troll society as a whole. But as enemies encroach upon the borders of Zuldazar, unrest within the king's council threatens to topple the empire.
</p>
<ul><li>Led by <span class="linkicon raceicon"><a href="/wiki/File:IconSmall_Talanji.gif" class="image"><img alt="IconSmall Talanji.gif" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_Talanji.gif/16px-IconSmall_Talanji.gif?1bcb18" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a></span> <a href="/wiki/Talanji" title="Talanji">Queen Talanji</a>.</li></ul>
<ul><li><a href="/wiki/Zanchuli_Council" title="Zanchuli Council">Zanchuli Council</a> - The council advises the ruler of the <a href="/wiki/Zandalari_Empire" title="Zandalari Empire">Zandalari Empire</a>, and ensures their every command is executed upon.
<ul><li>Led by: Unknown</li></ul></li></ul>
<ul><li><a href="/wiki/Zandalari_army" title="Zandalari army">Zandalari army</a> - The army has been an integral part of the survival of the empire throughout the many events following the <a href="/wiki/Cataclysm_(event)" title="Cataclysm (event)">Cataclysm</a>.
<ul><li>Led by <span class="linkicon raceicon"><a href="/wiki/File:IconSmall_Talanji.gif" class="image"><img alt="IconSmall Talanji.gif" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_Talanji.gif/16px-IconSmall_Talanji.gif?1bcb18" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a></span> <a href="/wiki/Talanji" title="Talanji">Queen Talanji</a> and secondarily by <span class="linkicon raceicon"><a href="/wiki/File:IconSmall_Zandalari_Female.gif" class="image"><img alt="IconSmall Zandalari Female.gif" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_Zandalari_Female.gif/16px-IconSmall_Zandalari_Female.gif?3f392f" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a></span> <a href="/wiki/Rakera" title="Rakera">General Rakera</a>.</li></ul></li></ul>
<ul><li><a href="/wiki/Golden_Fleet" title="Golden Fleet">Golden Fleet</a> - Is the maritime military force of the <a href="/wiki/Zandalari_Empire" title="Zandalari Empire">Zandalari Empire</a>.
<ul><li>Led by <span class="linkicon raceicon"><a href="/wiki/File:IconSmall_Talanji.gif" class="image"><img alt="IconSmall Talanji.gif" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_Talanji.gif/16px-IconSmall_Talanji.gif?1bcb18" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a></span> <a href="/wiki/Talanji" title="Talanji">Queen Talanji</a>.</li></ul></li></ul>
<ul><li><a href="/wiki/Talanji%27s_Expedition" title="Talanji&#39;s Expedition">Talanji's Expedition</a> - An expeditionary force of Zandalari led by Princess Talanji into <a href="/wiki/Nazmir" title="Nazmir">Nazmir</a> enable to find a way to stop the blood troll threat. They are aided by Horde forces in the form of Darkspear led by <a href="/wiki/Rokhan" title="Rokhan">Rokhan</a> and the <a href="/wiki/Gob_Squad" title="Gob Squad">Gob Squad</a>.
<ul><li>Led by <span class="linkicon raceicon"><a href="/wiki/File:IconSmall_Talanji.gif" class="image"><img alt="IconSmall Talanji.gif" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_Talanji.gif/16px-IconSmall_Talanji.gif?1bcb18" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a></span> <a href="/wiki/Princess_Talanji" class="mw-redirect" title="Princess Talanji">Princess Talanji</a></li>
<li><a href="/wiki/File:IconSmall_BloodTick.gif" class="image"><img alt="IconSmall BloodTick.gif" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_BloodTick.gif/18px-IconSmall_BloodTick.gif?ee83df" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="18" height="18" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a> <a href="/wiki/Blood_tick" class="mw-redirect" title="Blood tick">Blood tick</a> - Talanji's Expedition re-trained blood ticks to serve as <a href="/wiki/Reins_of_the_Expedition_Bloodswarmer" title="Reins of the Expedition Bloodswarmer">mounts</a>.<sup id="cite&#95;ref-113" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-113"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>113<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup></li></ul></li></ul>
<ul><li><span class="linkicon raceicon"><a href="/wiki/File:IconSmall_DireZandalari.gif" class="image"><img alt="IconSmall DireZandalari.gif" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_DireZandalari.gif/16px-IconSmall_DireZandalari.gif?afda44" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a></span> <a href="/wiki/Dire_troll" title="Dire troll">Dire troll</a> - Among the military of <a href="/wiki/Zandalar" title="Zandalar">Zandalar</a> are the elite dire trolls. These trolls are warriors who have perfected their arts, traded portions of their lives for the dark gift of brutal strength, and serve Zandalar as her greatest weapons of war.</li></ul>
<ul><li><span class="linkicon raceicon RaceLink"><a href="/wiki/Zandalari_troll" title="Zandalari troll"><img alt="Zandalari troll" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_Zandalari_Male.gif/16px-IconSmall_Zandalari_Male.gif?5daf82" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a><a href="/wiki/Zandalari_troll" title="Zandalari troll"><img alt="Zandalari troll" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_Zandalari_Female.gif/16px-IconSmall_Zandalari_Female.gif?3f392f" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a></span> <a href="/wiki/Scorched_Sands_tribe" title="Scorched Sands tribe">Scorched Sands tribe</a> - A group of <a href="/wiki/Zandalari_troll" title="Zandalari troll">Zandalari troll</a> outcasts that once made their home within <a href="/wiki/Vol%27dun" title="Vol&#39;dun">Vol'dun</a>. The tribe sought only to live in peace, but due to being constantly harassed by the thugs of <a href="/wiki/Mojambo" title="Mojambo">Mojambo</a> they turned to the Horde. At an unknown point in time, their exile was ended and they were welcomed back into their homes by Talanji.<sup id="cite&#95;ref-114" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-114"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>114<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup>
<ul><li>Led by <span class="linkicon raceicon"><a href="/wiki/File:IconSmall_Zandalari_Male.gif" class="image"><img alt="IconSmall Zandalari Male.gif" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_Zandalari_Male.gif/16px-IconSmall_Zandalari_Male.gif?5daf82" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a></span> <a href="/wiki/Razgaji" title="Razgaji">Razgaji</a>.</li></ul></li></ul>
<h4><span class="mw-headline" id="Voldunai_vulpera"><a href="/wiki/World_of_Warcraft:_Battle_for_Azeroth" title="Battle for Azeroth"><img alt="Battle for Azeroth" src="/images/thumb/BattleForAzeroth-Logo-Small.png/40px-BattleForAzeroth-Logo-Small.png?25e36c" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="40" height="15" style="vertical-align: middle" data-file-width="80" data-file-height="29" /></a> <a href="/wiki/File:Vulpera_crest.png" class="image" title="Voldunai"><img alt="Voldunai" src="/images/thumb/Vulpera_crest.png/30px-Vulpera_crest.png?71f531" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="30" height="45" data-file-width="238" data-file-height="357" /></a> <a href="/wiki/Voldunai" title="Voldunai">Voldunai vulpera</a></span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/wiki/Horde?action=edit&amp;section=30" title="Edit section: Voldunai vulpera"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h4>
<div class="thumb tright"><div class="thumbinner" style="width:240px;"><a href="/wiki/File:Vulpera_crest.png" class="image"><img src="/images/Vulpera_crest.png?71f531" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="238" height="357" class="thumbimage" data-file-width="238" data-file-height="357" /></a>  <div class="thumbcaption"><div class="magnify"><a href="/wiki/File:Vulpera_crest.png" class="internal" title="Enlarge"></a></div>The Voldunai crest</div></div></div>
<p><span class="linkicon raceicon RaceLink"><a href="/wiki/Vulpera" title="Vulpera"><img alt="Vulpera" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_Vulpera_Male.gif/16px-IconSmall_Vulpera_Male.gif?ed2cbd" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a><a href="/wiki/Vulpera" title="Vulpera"><img alt="Vulpera" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_Vulpera_Female.gif/16px-IconSmall_Vulpera_Female.gif?a40550" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a></span>&#160;<a href="/wiki/Vulpera" title="Vulpera">Vulpera</a> - The vulpera in <a href="/wiki/Vol%27dun" title="Vol&#39;dun">Vol'dun</a> are a keen and intelligent race of nomadic scavengers capable of turning what they find into opportunities to thrive.<sup id="cite&#95;ref-Visitor&#39;s&#95;Guide&#95;115-0" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-Visitor&#39;s_Guide-115"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>115<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup> They travel in <a href="/wiki/Caravan" title="Caravan">caravans</a>, scavenging for supplies, trading where they can,<sup id="cite&#95;ref-116" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-116"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>116<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup> and spreading information they pick up along the way.<sup id="cite&#95;ref-Visitor&#39;s&#95;Guide&#95;115-1" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-Visitor&#39;s_Guide-115"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>115<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup> Dwelling in <a href="/wiki/Vol%27dun" title="Vol&#39;dun">Vol'dun</a>, the vulpera can't afford to waste resources. Over time, they have learned to take anything that looks even remotely useful and be creative using what they have,<sup id="cite&#95;ref-117" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-117"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>117<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup> and they have learned to recognize value wherever it hides in the desert.<sup id="cite&#95;ref-118" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-118"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>118<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup> Eager to join the ranks of the Horde, their caravans have departed from the dunes in search of adventure.
</p>
<ul><li>Led by <span class="linkicon raceicon"><a href="/wiki/File:IconSmall_Kiro.gif" class="image"><img alt="IconSmall Kiro.gif" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_Kiro.gif/16px-IconSmall_Kiro.gif?b3bdf1" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a></span> <a href="/wiki/Kiro" title="Kiro">Kiro</a>.</li></ul>
<h4><span class="mw-headline" id="Dark_Talons_weyrn"><span class="icon df-inline"><a href="/wiki/World_of_Warcraft:_Dragonflight" title="Dragonflight"><img alt="Dragonflight" src="/images/thumb/Dragonflight-Icon-Inline.png/48px-Dragonflight-Icon-Inline.png?be4c0e" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="48" height="17" style="vertical-align: middle" data-file-width="80" data-file-height="29" /></a></span> <a href="/wiki/Dark_Talons" title="Dark Talons">Dark Talons weyrn</a></span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/wiki/Horde?action=edit&amp;section=31" title="Edit section: Dark Talons weyrn"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h4>
<div class="thumb tright"><div class="thumbinner" style="width:242px;"><a href="/wiki/File:Dracthyr_Crest.png" class="image"><img src="/images/thumb/Dracthyr_Crest.png/240px-Dracthyr_Crest.png?c19b1f" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="240" height="273" class="thumbimage" data-file-width="354" data-file-height="402" /></a>  <div class="thumbcaption"><div class="magnify"><a href="/wiki/File:Dracthyr_Crest.png" class="internal" title="Enlarge"></a></div>The Dracthyr crest</div></div></div>
<p><span class="linkicon raceicon RaceLink"><a href="/wiki/Dracthyr" title="Dracthyr"><img alt="Dracthyr" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_Dracthyr_Male.gif/16px-IconSmall_Dracthyr_Male.gif?b5e418" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a><a href="/wiki/Dracthyr" title="Dracthyr"><img alt="Dracthyr" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_Dracthyr_Female.gif/16px-IconSmall_Dracthyr_Female.gif?68ad26" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a><a href="/wiki/Dracthyr" title="Dracthyr"><img alt="Dracthyr" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_Dracthyr.gif/16px-IconSmall_Dracthyr.gif?54f2bf" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a></span>&#160;<a href="/wiki/Dracthyr" title="Dracthyr">Dracthyr</a> - Where the enemies of the <a href="/wiki/Deathwing" title="Deathwing">Earth-Warder</a> tread, the Dark Talons shall harrow their skies, crush their will, and topple their leaders, denying the <a href="/wiki/Black_dragonflight" title="Black dragonflight">black dragonflight</a>'s enemies solace in walls and borders. For every talon in their ranks, they strike down ten of their foes from behind. This weyrn, currently under the authority of the <a href="/wiki/Scalecommander_Cindrethresh" title="Scalecommander Cindrethresh">Scalecommander Cindrethresh</a>, trained to be the elite force that shall be feared by all enemies of the black dragonflight.<sup id="cite&#95;ref-119" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-119"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>119<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup>
</p>
<ul><li>Led by <span class="linkicon raceicon"><a href="/wiki/File:IconSmall_Cindrethresh.gif" class="image"><img alt="IconSmall Cindrethresh.gif" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_Cindrethresh.gif/16px-IconSmall_Cindrethresh.gif?dcd920" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a></span><span class="linkicon raceicon"><a href="/wiki/File:IconSmall_Cindrethresh2.gif" class="image"><img alt="IconSmall Cindrethresh2.gif" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_Cindrethresh2.gif/16px-IconSmall_Cindrethresh2.gif?3e1b86" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a></span> <a href="/wiki/Scalecommander_Cindrethresh" title="Scalecommander Cindrethresh">Scalecommander Cindrethresh</a>.</li></ul>
<h3><span class="mw-headline" id="Awakened_earthen"><span class="icon tww-inline"><a href="/wiki/World_of_Warcraft:_The_War_Within" title="The War Within"><img alt="The War Within" src="/images/thumb/TheWarWithin-Petopia-Logo.png/40px-TheWarWithin-Petopia-Logo.png?c7c8d5" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="40" height="22" style="vertical-align: middle" data-file-width="100" data-file-height="54" /></a></span><a href="/wiki/Awakened_(earthen)" title="Awakened (earthen)">Awakened</a> <a href="/wiki/Earthen" title="Earthen">earthen</a></span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/wiki/Horde?action=edit&amp;section=32" title="Edit section: Awakened earthen"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h3>
<div class="thumb tright"><div class="thumbinner" style="width:242px;"><a href="/wiki/File:Earthen_Crest.png" class="image"><img src="/images/thumb/Earthen_Crest.png/240px-Earthen_Crest.png?679363" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="240" height="273" class="thumbimage" data-file-width="354" data-file-height="402" /></a>  <div class="thumbcaption"><div class="magnify"><a href="/wiki/File:Earthen_Crest.png" class="internal" title="Enlarge"></a></div>The Earthen crest</div></div></div>
<p><span class="linkicon raceicon RaceLink"><a href="/wiki/Earthen" title="Earthen"><img alt="Earthen" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_Earthen_Male.gif/16px-IconSmall_Earthen_Male.gif?702e3e" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a><a href="/wiki/Earthen" title="Earthen"><img alt="Earthen" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_Earthen_Female.gif/16px-IconSmall_Earthen_Female.gif?87e21f" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a></span>&#160;<a href="/wiki/Earthen" title="Earthen">Earthen</a> - The titan-forged earthen once toiled under the edicts of the titans to shape Azeroth itself. The earthen of <a href="/wiki/Khaz_Algar" title="Khaz Algar">Khaz Algar</a>, aided by the efforts of outsiders, were revitalized by the <a href="/wiki/Hall_of_Awakening" title="Hall of Awakening">Hall of Awakening</a> after it was restored by <a href="/wiki/Magni_Bronzebeard" title="Magni Bronzebeard">Magni Bronzebeard</a>.<sup id="cite&#95;ref-120" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-120"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>120<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup> These earthen who have awoken from the Hall have found themselves imbued the magic of the Worldsoul itself and have been given new freedoms that were never available to them.<sup id="cite&#95;ref-121" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-121"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>121<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup><sup id="cite&#95;ref-122" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-122"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>122<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup> They now pledge service to the Alliance or Horde to learn more about Azeroth for their people.<sup id="cite&#95;ref-123" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-123"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>123<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup><sup id="cite&#95;ref-124" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-124"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>124<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup><sup id="cite&#95;ref-125" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-125"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>125<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup> Unlike other races, the Awakened earthen are still neutral (as part of the <a href="/wiki/Dornogal_(nation)" title="Dornogal (nation)">nation of Dornogal</a>), allowing them to individually pledge their allegiances as they see fit. Stormguard <a href="/wiki/Olbarig" title="Olbarig">Olbarig</a> and Geomender <a href="/wiki/Harmot" title="Harmot">Harmot</a> serve as two of the primary emissaries for the earthen to the Horde in the city of Orgrimmar.
</p>
<ul><li>Led by the <span class="linkicon raceicon"><a href="/wiki/File:IconSmall_Merrix.gif" class="image"><img alt="IconSmall Merrix.gif" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_Merrix.gif/16px-IconSmall_Merrix.gif?da62b7" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a></span><span class="linkicon raceicon"><a href="/wiki/File:IconSmall_Adelgonn.gif" class="image"><img alt="IconSmall Adelgonn.gif" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_Adelgonn.gif/16px-IconSmall_Adelgonn.gif?375c09" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a></span><span class="linkicon raceicon"><a href="/wiki/File:IconSmall_Brinthe.gif" class="image"><img alt="IconSmall Brinthe.gif" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_Brinthe.gif/16px-IconSmall_Brinthe.gif?c8d410" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a></span><span class="linkicon raceicon"><a href="/wiki/File:IconSmall_Lufsela.gif" class="image"><img alt="IconSmall Lufsela.gif" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_Lufsela.gif/16px-IconSmall_Lufsela.gif?3541ac" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a></span> <a href="/wiki/Council_of_Dornogal" title="Council of Dornogal">Council of Dornogal</a></li></ul>
<h2><span class="mw-headline" id="Horde_forces">Horde forces</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/wiki/Horde?action=edit&amp;section=33" title="Edit section: Horde forces"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h2>
<p>While a huge portion of the Horde's military power consists of representatives from the faction's member states, there are also several examples of organizations within the Horde which do not belong to any of the member states and instead of answering to a particular racial leader, they are organized by the leader of the Horde, the Warchief, and their advisors. However, while these organizations are not tied directly to any of the member states, the sub-organizations within them are often sent by one of the member states.
</p><p><span class="linkicon"><a href="/wiki/Horde" title="Horde"><img alt="Horde" src="/images/Horde_15.png?c852a8" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="15" height="15" data-file-width="15" data-file-height="15" /></a></span> <a href="/wiki/Horde_Army" title="Horde Army">Horde Army</a> - The collective land-based military force of the various members of the Horde.
</p>
<ul><li>Led by <span class="linkicon"><a href="/wiki/Horde" title="Horde"><img alt="Horde" src="/images/Horde_15.png?c852a8" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="15" height="15" data-file-width="15" data-file-height="15" /></a></span> <a href="/wiki/Horde_Council" title="Horde Council">The Horde Council</a></li></ul>
<p><span class="linkicon"><a href="/wiki/Horde" title="Horde"><img alt="Horde" src="/images/Horde_15.png?c852a8" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="15" height="15" data-file-width="15" data-file-height="15" /></a></span> <a href="/wiki/Horde_navy" title="Horde navy">Horde navy</a> - The collective maritime military force of the various members of the Horde. 
</p>
<ul><li>Led by <span class="linkicon"><a href="/wiki/Horde" title="Horde"><img alt="Horde" src="/images/Horde_15.png?c852a8" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="15" height="15" data-file-width="15" data-file-height="15" /></a></span> <a href="/wiki/Horde_Council" title="Horde Council">The Horde Council</a>
<ul><li><a href="/wiki/Dredge_Fleet" title="Dredge Fleet">Dredge Fleet</a></li>
<li><a href="/wiki/Forsaken_navy" title="Forsaken navy">Forsaken Fleet</a></li>
<li><a href="/wiki/Golden_Fleet" title="Golden Fleet">Golden Fleet</a></li>
<li><a href="/wiki/Gorok%27s_Sea_Dogs" title="Gorok&#39;s Sea Dogs">Gorok's Sea Dogs</a></li>
<li><a href="/wiki/Greenbelly%27s_Raiders" title="Greenbelly&#39;s Raiders">Greenbelly's Raiders</a></li>
<li><a href="/wiki/Hellscream_warfleet" title="Hellscream warfleet">Hellscream warfleet</a></li>
<li><i><a href="/wiki/Thalassian_navy" title="Thalassian navy">Thalassian navy</a></i></li>
<li><a href="/wiki/Warchief%27s_fleet" title="Warchief&#39;s fleet">Warchief's fleet</a></li>
<li><a href="/wiki/Windrunner_Fleet" title="Windrunner Fleet">Windrunner Fleet</a></li></ul></li></ul>
<p><span class="linkicon"><a href="/wiki/Horde" title="Horde"><img alt="Horde" src="/images/Horde_15.png?c852a8" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="15" height="15" data-file-width="15" data-file-height="15" /></a></span> <a href="/wiki/Horde_air_force" title="Horde air force">Horde air force</a> - The collective aerial military force of the various members of the Horde.
</p>
<ul><li>Led by <span class="linkicon"><a href="/wiki/Horde" title="Horde"><img alt="Horde" src="/images/Horde_15.png?c852a8" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="15" height="15" data-file-width="15" data-file-height="15" /></a></span> <a href="/wiki/Horde_Council" title="Horde Council">The Horde Council</a></li></ul>
<p><span class="linkicon"><a href="/wiki/Horde" title="Horde"><img alt="Horde" src="/images/Horde_15.png?c852a8" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="15" height="15" data-file-width="15" data-file-height="15" /></a></span> <a href="/wiki/Thrallmar" title="Thrallmar">Thrallmar</a> - The Horde expeditionary forces to <a href="/wiki/Outland" title="Outland">Outland</a>, who quickly found many native allies. Consist of all the Horde races. The expedition's settlements are scattered all over Outland's zones, except Nagrand and Netherstorm.
</p>
<ul><li>Led by <span class="linkicon raceicon"><a href="/wiki/File:IconSmall_Nazgrel.gif" class="image"><img alt="IconSmall Nazgrel.gif" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_Nazgrel.gif/16px-IconSmall_Nazgrel.gif?39abe0" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a></span> <a href="/wiki/Nazgrel" title="Nazgrel">Nazgrel</a>.</li></ul>
<p><span class="linkicon"><a href="/wiki/Horde" title="Horde"><img alt="Horde" src="/images/Horde_15.png?c852a8" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="15" height="15" data-file-width="15" data-file-height="15" /></a></span> <a href="/wiki/Horde_Expedition" title="Horde Expedition">Horde Expedition</a> - The military umbrella organization overseeing the Horde forces that have established themselves throughout Northrend, formerly unified under <a href="/wiki/Garrosh_Hellscream" title="Garrosh Hellscream">Garrosh Hellscream</a> who had been chosen to lead the expedition by the Warchief.
</p>
<ul><li><a href="/wiki/Warsong_Offensive" title="Warsong Offensive">Warsong Offensive</a> - The main forces of the Horde in Northrend. It is named after the clan, but not a part of it.
<ul><li>Led by: Unknown.</li>
<li><a href="/wiki/Kor%27kron_Guard" class="mw-redirect" title="Kor&#39;kron Guard">Kor'kron Guard</a> - the elite fighting forces of the Warsong Offensive in Northrend.</li></ul></li>
<li><span class="linkicon raceicon RaceLink"><a href="/wiki/Forsaken" title="Forsaken"><img alt="Forsaken" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_Undead_Male.gif/16px-IconSmall_Undead_Male.gif?6cfd1d" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a><a href="/wiki/Forsaken" title="Forsaken"><img alt="Forsaken" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_Undead_Female.gif/16px-IconSmall_Undead_Female.gif?6e00fb" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a></span> <a href="/wiki/Hand_of_Vengeance" title="Hand of Vengeance">Hand of Vengeance</a> - A group of Forsaken forces that have been sent to Northrend by Sylvanas Windrunner to bring Undercity's vengeance upon the Lich King. Their method is the delivery of the latest strain of their deadly Plague.
<ul><li>Led by <span class="linkicon raceicon"><a href="/wiki/File:IconSmall_Undead_Male.gif" class="image"><img alt="IconSmall Undead Male.gif" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_Undead_Male.gif/16px-IconSmall_Undead_Male.gif?6cfd1d" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a></span> <a href="/wiki/High_Executor_Anselm" title="High Executor Anselm">Anselm</a>, <span class="linkicon raceicon"><a href="/wiki/File:IconSmall_Undead_Male.gif" class="image"><img alt="IconSmall Undead Male.gif" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_Undead_Male.gif/16px-IconSmall_Undead_Male.gif?6cfd1d" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a></span> <a href="/wiki/High_Executor_Wroth" title="High Executor Wroth">Wroth</a> and <span class="linkicon raceicon"><a href="/wiki/File:IconSmall_MadScientist.gif" class="image"><img alt="IconSmall MadScientist.gif" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_MadScientist.gif/16px-IconSmall_MadScientist.gif?351736" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a></span> <a href="/wiki/Chief_Plaguebringer_Harris" title="Chief Plaguebringer Harris">Harris</a>.</li></ul></li>
<li><span class="linkicon raceicon RaceLink"><a href="/wiki/Taunka" title="Taunka"><img alt="Taunka" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_Taunka_Male.gif/16px-IconSmall_Taunka_Male.gif?d6e57c" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a><a href="/wiki/Taunka" title="Taunka"><img alt="Taunka" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_Taunka_Female.gif/16px-IconSmall_Taunka_Female.gif?c8bb75" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a></span> <a href="/wiki/The_Taunka" class="mw-redirect" title="The Taunka">The Taunka</a> - Ancient relatives of the tauren, were thought to be lost until re-discovered by <a href="/wiki/Garrosh_Hellscream" title="Garrosh Hellscream">Garrosh Hellscream</a> and his orc forces upon their arrival to Northrend. They are welcomed into the Horde soon after <a href="/wiki/Roanauk_Icemist" title="Roanauk Icemist">their leader</a> is <a href="/wiki/All_Hail_Roanauk!" title="All Hail Roanauk!">rescued</a>.
<ul><li>Led by <span class="linkicon raceicon"><a href="/wiki/File:IconSmall_Taunka_Male.gif" class="image"><img alt="IconSmall Taunka Male.gif" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_Taunka_Male.gif/16px-IconSmall_Taunka_Male.gif?d6e57c" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a></span> <a href="/wiki/Roanauk_Icemist" title="Roanauk Icemist">Roanauk Icemist</a></li></ul></li>
<li><span class="linkicon raceicon RaceLink"><a href="/wiki/Blood_elf" title="Blood elf"><img alt="Blood elf" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_BloodElf_Male.png/16px-IconSmall_BloodElf_Male.png?215d28" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a><a href="/wiki/Blood_elf" title="Blood elf"><img alt="Blood elf" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_BloodElf_Female.png/16px-IconSmall_BloodElf_Female.png?681eed" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a></span> <a href="/wiki/Sunreavers" title="Sunreavers">Sunreavers</a> - Former blood elven members of the <a href="/wiki/Kirin_Tor" title="Kirin Tor">Kirin Tor</a>, named for their leader, <a href="/wiki/Aethas_Sunreaver" title="Aethas Sunreaver">Aethas Sunreaver</a>. The Horde, traditionally barred from <a href="/wiki/Dalaran" title="Dalaran">Dalaran</a>, was allowed entry into the city due to the Sunreaver's efforts. However, they were, later, <a href="/wiki/Purge_of_Dalaran" title="Purge of Dalaran">expelled</a> from the city. Now they fight as a part of the <a href="/wiki/Sunreaver_Onslaught" title="Sunreaver Onslaught">Sunreaver Onslaught</a> to strengthen the might of <a href="/wiki/Quel%27Thalas_(kingdom)" title="Quel&#39;Thalas (kingdom)">Quel'Thalas</a>.
<ul><li>Led by <span class="linkicon raceicon"><a href="/wiki/File:IconSmall_Aethas.gif" class="image"><img alt="IconSmall Aethas.gif" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_Aethas.gif/16px-IconSmall_Aethas.gif?9e4b4e" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a></span> <a href="/wiki/Aethas_Sunreaver" title="Aethas Sunreaver">Aethas Sunreaver</a></li></ul></li></ul>
<div class="thumb tright"><div class="thumbinner" style="width:322px;"><a href="/wiki/File:Defender_of_Draenor_-_Horde_-_Monument.png" class="image"><img src="/images/thumb/Defender_of_Draenor_-_Horde_-_Monument.png/320px-Defender_of_Draenor_-_Horde_-_Monument.png?fbbc46" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="320" height="370" class="thumbimage" data-file-width="935" data-file-height="1080" /></a>  <div class="thumbcaption"><div class="magnify"><a href="/wiki/File:Defender_of_Draenor_-_Horde_-_Monument.png" class="internal" title="Enlarge"></a></div>The Horde crest garrison <a href="/wiki/Monuments" title="Monuments">monument</a>.</div></div></div>
<p><span class="linkicon"><a href="/wiki/Horde" title="Horde"><img alt="Horde" src="/images/Horde_15.png?c852a8" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="15" height="15" data-file-width="15" data-file-height="15" /></a></span> <a href="/wiki/Krom%27gar" title="Krom&#39;gar">Krom'gar</a> - An army named after it's leader, Krom'gar, given by Garrosh Hellscream with the intention to conquer the <a href="/wiki/Stonetalon_Mountains" title="Stonetalon Mountains">Stonetalon Mountains</a>. Following the end of the questline, it is implied the army is dissolved entirely.
</p>
<ul><li>Previously led by <span class="linkicon raceicon"><a href="/wiki/File:IconSmall_Orc_Male.gif" class="image"><img alt="IconSmall Orc Male.gif" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_Orc_Male.gif/16px-IconSmall_Orc_Male.gif?f251d1" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a></span> <a href="/wiki/Overlord_Krom%27gar" title="Overlord Krom&#39;gar">Overlord Krom'gar</a></li></ul>
<p><span class="linkicon raceicon RaceLink"><a href="/wiki/Orc" title="Orc"><img alt="Orc" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_Orc_Male.gif/16px-IconSmall_Orc_Male.gif?f251d1" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a><a href="/wiki/Orc" title="Orc"><img alt="Orc" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_Orc_Female.gif/16px-IconSmall_Orc_Female.gif?575305" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a></span> <a href="/wiki/Hellscream%27s_Reach" title="Hellscream&#39;s Reach">Hellscream's Reach</a> - An elite Horde regiment comprised solely of veterans of multiple tours of duty. Handpicked by <a href="/wiki/Garrosh" class="mw-redirect" title="Garrosh">Garrosh</a> himself, he has commanded them to take complete control of <a href="/wiki/Tol_Barad" title="Tol Barad">Tol Barad</a>. 
</p>
<ul><li>Led by <span class="linkicon raceicon"><a href="/wiki/File:IconSmall_Garrosh.gif" class="image"><img alt="IconSmall Garrosh.gif" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_Garrosh.gif/16px-IconSmall_Garrosh.gif?4e6cf4" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a></span> <a href="/wiki/Garrosh_Hellscream" title="Garrosh Hellscream">Garrosh Hellscream</a> until his <a href="/wiki/Siege_of_Orgrimmar" title="Siege of Orgrimmar">deposal</a>. Current leader unknown.</li></ul>
<p><span class="linkicon"><a href="/wiki/Horde" title="Horde"><img alt="Horde" src="/images/Horde_15.png?c852a8" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="15" height="15" data-file-width="15" data-file-height="15" /></a></span> <a href="/wiki/Dominance_Offensive" title="Dominance Offensive">Dominance Offensive</a> - The major Horde force for the Pandaria campaign.
</p>
<ul><li>Led by <span class="raceicon"><img alt="" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_Garrosh.gif/16px-IconSmall_Garrosh.gif?4e6cf4" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" />&#160;</span><a href="/wiki/Garrosh_Hellscream" title="Garrosh Hellscream"><span style="color:">Garrosh Hellscream</span></a> until his <a href="/wiki/Siege_of_Orgrimmar" title="Siege of Orgrimmar">deposal</a>. Current leader unknown.</li></ul>
<p><span class="linkicon"><a href="/wiki/Horde" title="Horde"><img alt="Horde" src="/images/Horde_15.png?c852a8" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="15" height="15" data-file-width="15" data-file-height="15" /></a></span> <a href="/wiki/Vol%27jin%27s_Spear" title="Vol&#39;jin&#39;s Spear">Vol'jin's Spear</a> - Horde forces in <a href="/wiki/Ashran" title="Ashran">Ashran</a>, stationed at <a href="/wiki/Warspear" title="Warspear">Warspear</a>.
</p>
<ul><li>Led by <span class="linkicon raceicon"><a href="/wiki/File:IconSmall_Troll_Male.gif" class="image"><img alt="IconSmall Troll Male.gif" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_Troll_Male.gif/16px-IconSmall_Troll_Male.gif?7580a0" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a></span> <a href="/wiki/High_Warlord_Volrath" title="High Warlord Volrath">High Warlord Volrath</a>.</li></ul>
<p><span class="linkicon"><a href="/wiki/Horde" title="Horde"><img alt="Horde" src="/images/Horde_15.png?c852a8" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="15" height="15" data-file-width="15" data-file-height="15" /></a></span> <a href="/wiki/Vol%27jin%27s_Headhunters" title="Vol&#39;jin&#39;s Headhunters">Vol'jin's Headhunters</a> - Horde forces in <a href="/wiki/Tanaan_Jungle" title="Tanaan Jungle">Tanaan Jungle</a>, stationed at <a href="/wiki/Vol%27mar" title="Vol&#39;mar">Vol'mar</a>.
</p><p><span class="linkicon"><a href="/wiki/Horde" title="Horde"><img alt="Horde" src="/images/Horde_15.png?c852a8" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="15" height="15" data-file-width="15" data-file-height="15" /></a></span> <a href="/wiki/Honorbound" title="Honorbound">Honorbound</a> - Special force of the Horde army tasked with disrupting Alliance operations within Kul Tiras.
</p>
<ul><li>Led by <span class="linkicon raceicon"><a href="/wiki/File:IconSmall_Nathanos.gif" class="image"><img alt="IconSmall Nathanos.gif" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_Nathanos.gif/16px-IconSmall_Nathanos.gif?531d8d" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a></span> <a href="/wiki/Nathanos_Blightcaller" title="Nathanos Blightcaller">Nathanos Blightcaller</a> until he <a href="/wiki/Banshee_loyalists" title="Banshee loyalists">joined</a> Sylvanas in <a href="/wiki/Most_Loyal" title="Most Loyal">abandoning</a> the Horde. Current leader unknown.</li></ul>
<h3><span class="mw-headline" id="Other_members_and_allies">Other members and allies</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/wiki/Horde?action=edit&amp;section=34" title="Edit section: Other members and allies"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h3>
<p><span class="linkicon raceicon RaceLink"><a href="/wiki/Ogre_mage" title="Ogre mage"><img alt="Ogre mage" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_OgreMage2.gif/16px-IconSmall_OgreMage2.gif?6160cf" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a></span><span class="linkicon raceicon RaceLink"><a href="/wiki/Ogre" title="Ogre"><img alt="Ogre" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_Ogre2.gif/16px-IconSmall_Ogre2.gif?931cca" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a></span> <a href="/wiki/Stonemaul_clan" title="Stonemaul clan">Stonemaul clan</a> - This <a href="/wiki/Faction" title="Faction">faction</a> joined the Horde after <a href="/wiki/Rexxar" title="Rexxar">Rexxar</a> bested <a href="/wiki/Kor%27gall" title="Kor&#39;gall">Kor'gall</a> in a duel for the leadership of the <a href="/wiki/Clan" title="Clan">clan</a>. After, Rexxar disappeared into the Barren wilderness and made the ogre <a href="/wiki/Mok%27Morokk" class="mw-redirect" title="Mok&#39;Morokk">Mok'Morokk</a> the leader of the clan. Although Mok'Morokk fell prey to temptations of power after <a href="/wiki/Rexxar" title="Rexxar">Rexxar</a>'s departure and so the <a href="/wiki/Stonemaul_Village" class="mw-redirect" title="Stonemaul Village">Stonemaul Village</a>, actual former capital of the <a href="/wiki/Clan" title="Clan">clan</a>, was raided by <a href="/wiki/Onyxia" title="Onyxia">Onyxia</a>'s brood. Mok’Morokk led the clan to <a href="/wiki/Brackenwall_Village" title="Brackenwall Village">Brackenwall Village</a>, current capital, where he ruled unquestioned until an unknown challenger drove him out.
</p>
<ul><li>Currently many support <span class="linkicon raceicon"><a href="/wiki/File:IconSmall_Ogre.gif" class="image"><img alt="IconSmall Ogre.gif" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_Ogre.gif/16px-IconSmall_Ogre.gif?d2c50c" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a></span> <a href="/wiki/Tharg" title="Tharg">Tharg</a> as a new leader.</li></ul>
<p><span class="linkicon raceicon RaceLink"><a href="/wiki/Ogre_mage" title="Ogre mage"><img alt="Ogre mage" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_OgreMage2.gif/16px-IconSmall_OgreMage2.gif?6160cf" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a></span><span class="linkicon raceicon RaceLink"><a href="/wiki/Ogre" title="Ogre"><img alt="Ogre" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_Ogre2.gif/16px-IconSmall_Ogre2.gif?931cca" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a></span> <a href="/wiki/Dunemaul_clan" title="Dunemaul clan">Dunemaul clan</a> - Shortly after the shattering the Dunemaul band joined the Horde due to the efforts of <a href="/wiki/Megs_Dreadshredder" title="Megs Dreadshredder">Megs Dreadshredder</a> and the Dunemaul "Emissary" who are stationed at the <a href="/wiki/Dunemaul_Recruitment_Camp" title="Dunemaul Recruitment Camp">Dunemaul Recruitment Camp</a>.
</p>
<ul><li>Organized by <span class="linkicon raceicon"><a href="/wiki/File:IconSmall_Goblin_Female.gif" class="image"><img alt="IconSmall Goblin Female.gif" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_Goblin_Female.gif/16px-IconSmall_Goblin_Female.gif?9787be" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a></span> <a href="/wiki/Megs_Dreadshredder" title="Megs Dreadshredder">Megs Dreadshredder</a>.</li></ul>
<p><span class="linkicon raceicon RaceLink"><a href="/wiki/Forest_troll" title="Forest troll"><img alt="Forest troll" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_ForestTroll_Male.gif/16px-IconSmall_ForestTroll_Male.gif?8b13b8" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a><a href="/wiki/Forest_troll" title="Forest troll"><img alt="Forest troll" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_OldForestTroll_Female.gif/16px-IconSmall_OldForestTroll_Female.gif?52312a" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a></span><span class="linkicon raceicon RaceLink"><a href="/wiki/Dire_troll" title="Dire troll"><img alt="Dire troll" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_DireTroll.gif/16px-IconSmall_DireTroll.gif?fa1016" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a></span> <a href="/wiki/Revantusk_tribe" title="Revantusk tribe">Revantusk tribe</a> - Situated in the <a href="/wiki/Hinterlands" title="Hinterlands">Hinterlands</a>, this tribe was allied with the <a href="/wiki/Old_Horde" title="Old Horde">Old Horde</a> and still was loyal to their allies even after the Second War. The moment they heard about the creation of the new Horde this tribe once again allied themself with the Horde. At <a href="/wiki/Elder_Torntusk" title="Elder Torntusk">Elder Torntusk</a>'s return, the Revantusk have joined the Horde as full members.
</p>
<ul><li>Led by <span class="linkicon raceicon"><a href="/wiki/File:IconSmall_OldForestTroll_Female.gif" class="image"><img alt="IconSmall OldForestTroll Female.gif" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_OldForestTroll_Female.gif/16px-IconSmall_OldForestTroll_Female.gif?52312a" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a></span> <a href="/wiki/Primal_Torntusk" title="Primal Torntusk">Primal Torntusk</a> and her husband <span class="linkicon raceicon"><a href="/wiki/File:IconSmall_ForestTroll_Male.gif" class="image"><img alt="IconSmall ForestTroll Male.gif" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_ForestTroll_Male.gif/16px-IconSmall_ForestTroll_Male.gif?8b13b8" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a></span> <a href="/wiki/Elder_Torntusk" title="Elder Torntusk">Elder Torntusk</a>.</li></ul>
<p><span class="linkicon raceicon RaceLink"><a href="/wiki/Jungle_troll" title="Jungle troll"><img alt="Jungle troll" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_Troll_Male.gif/16px-IconSmall_Troll_Male.gif?7580a0" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a><a href="/wiki/Jungle_troll" title="Jungle troll"><img alt="Jungle troll" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_Troll_Female.gif/16px-IconSmall_Troll_Female.gif?9723e4" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a></span> <a href="/wiki/Shatterspear_tribe" title="Shatterspear tribe">Shatterspear tribe</a> - Personally recruited by Garrosh Hellscream to fight against the <a href="/wiki/Night_elf" title="Night elf">night elves</a>.<sup id="cite&#95;ref-126" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-126"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>126<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup> Mostly situated in the <a href="/wiki/Shatterspear_Village" class="mw-redirect" title="Shatterspear Village">Shatterspear Village</a>. After they helped the Horde during the <a href="/wiki/War_of_the_Thorns" title="War of the Thorns">War of the Thorns</a>, the tribe was attacked in retaliation by the night elf forces. They are rebuilding the village since the end of the <a href="/wiki/Fourth_War" title="Fourth War">Fourth War</a>, in hopes that the tribe can ever answer the Horde's call.<sup id="cite&#95;ref-127" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-127"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>127<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup>
</p>
