<ul><li>Led by: <span class="linkicon raceicon"><a href="/wiki/File:IconSmall_Troll_Male.gif" class="image"><img alt="IconSmall Troll Male.gif" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_Troll_Male.gif/16px-IconSmall_Troll_Male.gif?7580a0" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a></span> <a href="/wiki/Ohseso" title="Ohseso">Ohseso</a>.</li></ul>
<p><span class="linkicon raceicon RaceLink"><a href="/wiki/Mag%27har_orc" title="Mag&#39;har orc"><img alt="Mag&#39;har orc" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_OrcBrown_Male.gif/16px-IconSmall_OrcBrown_Male.gif?16fed8" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a><a href="/wiki/Mag%27har_orc" title="Mag&#39;har orc"><img alt="Mag&#39;har orc" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_OrcBrown_Female.gif/16px-IconSmall_OrcBrown_Female.gif?be2b11" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a></span> <a href="/wiki/Mag%27har_(faction)" title="Mag&#39;har (faction)">The Mag'har</a> of <a href="/wiki/Outland" title="Outland">Outland</a> - <a href="/wiki/Mag%27har_orc" title="Mag&#39;har orc">Mag'har orcs</a> are orcs from various clans that escaped corruption and remained in Outland throughout all three wars.
</p>
<ul><li>Led by <span class="linkicon raceicon"><a href="/wiki/File:IconSmall_Geyah.gif" class="image"><img alt="IconSmall Geyah.gif" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_Geyah.gif/16px-IconSmall_Geyah.gif?9d3417" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a></span> <a href="/wiki/Greatmother_Geyah" title="Greatmother Geyah">Greatmother Geyah</a>. <p><span class="linkicon raceicon RaceLink"><a href="/wiki/Taunka" title="Taunka"><img alt="Taunka" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_Taunka_Male.gif/16px-IconSmall_Taunka_Male.gif?d6e57c" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a><a href="/wiki/Taunka" title="Taunka"><img alt="Taunka" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_Taunka_Female.gif/16px-IconSmall_Taunka_Female.gif?c8bb75" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a></span> <a href="/wiki/The_Taunka" class="mw-redirect" title="The Taunka">The Taunka</a> - Ancient relatives of the tauren, were thought to be lost until re-discovered by <a href="/wiki/Garrosh_Hellscream" title="Garrosh Hellscream">Garrosh Hellscream</a> and his orc forces upon their arrival to Northrend. They are welcomed into the Horde soon after <a href="/wiki/Roanauk_Icemist" title="Roanauk Icemist">their leader</a> is <a href="/wiki/All_Hail_Roanauk!" title="All Hail Roanauk!">rescued</a>.
</p>
<ul><li>Led by <span class="raceicon"><img alt="" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_Taunka_Male.gif/16px-IconSmall_Taunka_Male.gif?d6e57c" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" />&#160;</span><a href="/wiki/Roanauk_Icemist" title="Roanauk Icemist"><span style="color:">Roanauk Icemist</span></a>.</li></ul>
<p><span class="linkicon raceicon"><a href="/wiki/File:IconSmall_Hozen.gif" class="image"><img alt="IconSmall Hozen.gif" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_Hozen.gif/16px-IconSmall_Hozen.gif?ecc720" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a></span> <a href="/wiki/Grookin_tribe" class="mw-redirect" title="Grookin tribe">Grookin tribe</a> - A faction of hozen that has made their home in the <a href="/wiki/Jade_Forest" title="Jade Forest">Jade Forest</a> and was in constant war against the <a href="/wiki/Jinyu" title="Jinyu">Jinyu</a>. They find themselves allied with the Horde after the Horde gains their favor by rescuing some of their members, sating their appetite for fish, and killing some of their most fearsome enemies. Armed and equipped by the Horde, this tribe of hozen are recruited to fight the Horde's war against the <a href="/wiki/Alliance" title="Alliance">Alliance</a>.<sup id="cite&#95;ref-128" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-128"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>128<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup>
</p>
<ul><li>Led by <span class="raceicon"><img alt="" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_Hozen.gif/16px-IconSmall_Hozen.gif?ecc720" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" />&#160;</span><a href="/wiki/Chief_Kah_Kah" title="Chief Kah Kah"><span style="color:">Chief Kah Kah</span></a>.</li></ul>

<p><span class="linkicon raceicon RaceLink"><a href="/wiki/Gilgoblin" title="Gilgoblin"><img alt="Gilgoblin" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_Gilgoblin_Male.gif/16px-IconSmall_Gilgoblin_Male.gif?c96646" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a><a href="/wiki/Gilgoblin" title="Gilgoblin"><img alt="Gilgoblin" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_Gilgoblin_Female.gif/16px-IconSmall_Gilgoblin_Female.gif?7beff2" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a></span><span class="linkicon raceicon"><a href="/wiki/File:IconSmall_Skrog.gif" class="image"><img alt="IconSmall Skrog.gif" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_Skrog.gif/16px-IconSmall_Skrog.gif?6b72f1" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a></span><span class="linkicon raceicon"><a href="/wiki/File:IconSmall_Makrura.gif" class="image"><img alt="IconSmall Makrura.gif" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_Makrura.gif/16px-IconSmall_Makrura.gif?b45bf4" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a></span><span class="linkicon raceicon"><a href="/wiki/File:IconSmall_Murloc_Male.gif" class="image"><img alt="IconSmall Murloc Male.gif" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_Murloc_Male.gif/16px-IconSmall_Murloc_Male.gif?85395b" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a></span> <a href="/wiki/Unshackled" title="Unshackled">Unshackled</a> - A faction of former <a href="/wiki/Naga" title="Naga">naga</a> <a href="/wiki/Slavery" title="Slavery">slaves</a> found in <a href="/wiki/Nazjatar" title="Nazjatar">Nazjatar</a>. An oppressed group consisting of <a href="/wiki/Gilgoblin" title="Gilgoblin">gilgoblins</a>, <a href="/wiki/Makrura" title="Makrura">makrura</a>, <a href="/wiki/Murloc" title="Murloc">murlocs</a>, and <a href="/wiki/Sea_giant" title="Sea giant">sea giants</a>, these <a href="/wiki/Refugee" title="Refugee">refugees</a> have banded together to rise up and seize their freedom. When Horde forces arrive in Nazjatar, they ally with the Unshackled against the naga while the <a href="/wiki/Alliance" title="Alliance">Alliance</a> befriends the <a href="/wiki/Waveblade_Ankoan" title="Waveblade Ankoan">Waveblade Ankoan</a>. <a href="/wiki/Unshackled_Mercenaries" title="Unshackled Mercenaries">Unshackled Mercenaries</a> are gilgoblins that are <a href="/wiki/War_Campaign_followers" title="War Campaign followers">troops</a> that participate in various <a href="/wiki/War_Campaign_missions" title="War Campaign missions">War Campaign missions</a>.
</p>

<p><span class="linkicon raceicon RaceLink"><a href="/wiki/Centaur" title="Centaur"><img alt="Centaur" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_Centaur_Male.gif/16px-IconSmall_Centaur_Male.gif?ebb9de" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a><a href="/wiki/Centaur" title="Centaur"><img alt="Centaur" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_Centaur_Female.gif/16px-IconSmall_Centaur_Female.gif?ecd479" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a></span>&#160;<a href="/wiki/Centaur" title="Centaur">Centaur</a> - During the unification of the centaur under the rule of <a href="/wiki/Aratas" title="Aratas">Aratas</a>, a single member of their race, <a href="/wiki/Dorthar" title="Dorthar">Dorthar</a>, has joined the ranks of the Horde and helped his new allies to defeat the centaur.
</p><p><b>Although not officially part of the Horde, the following groups have allied themselves with the Horde:</b><br />
</p>
<ul><li><span class="linkicon raceicon"><a href="/wiki/File:IconSmall_Mok%27Nathal_Male.gif" class="image"><img alt="IconSmall Mok&#39;Nathal Male.gif" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_Mok%27Nathal_Male.gif/16px-IconSmall_Mok%27Nathal_Male.gif?1b6f8e" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="18" data-file-height="18" /></a></span><span class="linkicon raceicon"><a href="/wiki/File:IconSmall_Mok%27Nathal_Female.gif" class="image"><img alt="IconSmall Mok&#39;Nathal Female.gif" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_Mok%27Nathal_Female.gif/16px-IconSmall_Mok%27Nathal_Female.gif?871bcb" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="18" data-file-height="18" /></a></span> <a href="/wiki/Mok%27nathal" title="Mok&#39;nathal">Mok'Nathal clan</a>- This clan of hunters and beastmasters was discovered by <a href="/wiki/Rexxar" title="Rexxar">Rexxar</a> during his activities in <a href="/wiki/Outland" title="Outland">Outland</a>. After being impressed by Horde adventurers, they agree to aid the Horde.

<ul><li><span class="linkicon raceicon RaceLink"><a href="/wiki/Human" title="Human"><img alt="Human" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_Human_Male.gif/16px-IconSmall_Human_Male.gif?f38ee4" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a><a href="/wiki/Human" title="Human"><img alt="Human" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_Human_Female.gif/16px-IconSmall_Human_Female.gif?4582db" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a></span><span class="linkicon raceicon RaceLink"><a href="/wiki/Human" title="Human"><img alt="Human" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_KulTiran_Male.gif/16px-IconSmall_KulTiran_Male.gif?bbdefd" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a><a href="/wiki/Human" title="Human"><img alt="Human" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_KulTiran_Female.gif/16px-IconSmall_KulTiran_Female.gif?d85227" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a></span> <a href="/wiki/Fogsail_Freebooters" title="Fogsail Freebooters">Fogsail Freebooters</a> - A <a href="/wiki/Pirate" title="Pirate">pirate</a> group found in <a href="/wiki/Kul_Tiras" title="Kul Tiras">Kul Tiras</a>. They allow the Horde to use their base of <a href="/wiki/Plunder_Harbor" title="Plunder Harbor">Plunder Harbor</a> as a foothold on the island after the Horde helped <a href="/wiki/Owings" title="Owings">Owings</a> pull a mutiny on the previous captain <a href="/wiki/Captain_Rhenik" title="Captain Rhenik">Rhenik</a> for wanting to sell the crew out to the <a href="/wiki/Ashvane_Trading_Company" title="Ashvane Trading Company">Ashvane Trading Company</a>.<sup id="cite&#95;ref-132" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-132"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>132<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup>

Together they fought against the <a href="/wiki/Fel_Horde" title="Fel Horde">Fel Horde</a>.<sup id="cite&#95;ref-134" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-134"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>134<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup>
<ul><li>Led by <span class="linkicon raceicon"><a href="/wiki/File:IconSmall_Cultist_Male.gif" class="image"><img alt="IconSmall Cultist Male.gif" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_Cultist_Male.gif/16px-IconSmall_Cultist_Male.gif?fead74" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a></span> <a href="/wiki/Commander_Hogarth" title="Commander Hogarth">Commander Hogarth</a>.</li></ul></li></ul>
<ul><li><span class="linkicon raceicon RaceLink"><a href="/wiki/Quilboar" title="Quilboar"><img alt="Quilboar" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_Quilboar_Male.gif/16px-IconSmall_Quilboar_Male.gif?9b193a" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a><a href="/wiki/Quilboar" title="Quilboar"><img alt="Quilboar" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_Quilboar_Female.gif/16px-IconSmall_Quilboar_Female.gif?977666" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a></span>&#160;<a href="/wiki/Freewind_Post" title="Freewind Post">Freewind Post quilboar</a> - A group of quilboar under the leadership of Shagtusk united with the tauren of <a href="/wiki/Freewind_Post" title="Freewind Post">Freewind Post</a> in order to fight together against the threat of the Grimtotem tribe and the local <a href="/wiki/Harpy" title="Harpy">harpies</a>. Their alliance is fragile and <a href="/wiki/Elmarine" title="Elmarine">Elmarine</a> tries to help keep the peace between the two groups.<sup id="cite&#95;ref-135" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-135"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>135<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup>

<ul><li><span class="linkicon raceicon"><a href="/wiki/File:IconSmall_SkeletalTroll.gif" class="image"><img alt="IconSmall SkeletalTroll.gif" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_SkeletalTroll.gif/16px-IconSmall_SkeletalTroll.gif?f7aaea" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a></span> <a href="/wiki/Skeletal_troll" title="Skeletal troll">Jamboya's pirates</a> - During the Fourth War a group of cursed undead trolls that once were part of <a href="/wiki/Pirate-King_Zem%27lan" title="Pirate-King Zem&#39;lan">Pirate-King Zem'lans</a> crew mutinied against their tyrannical leader, and allied themselves with the Horde to do so.<sup id="cite&#95;ref-136" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-136"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>136<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup> And using the gold they took from their former leader they bought a ship and sailed the seas again,<sup id="cite&#95;ref-137" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-137"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>137<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup> while still trying to find a permanent way to get rid of Zem'lan.<sup id="cite&#95;ref-138" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-138"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>138<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup>
<ul><li>Led by <span class="linkicon raceicon"><a href="/wiki/File:IconSmall_SkeletalTroll.gif" class="image"><img alt="IconSmall SkeletalTroll.gif" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_SkeletalTroll.gif/16px-IconSmall_SkeletalTroll.gif?f7aaea" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a></span> <a href="/wiki/First_Mate_Jamboya" title="First Mate Jamboya">Captain Jamboya</a>.</li></ul></li></ul>
<ul><li><span class="linkicon raceicon RaceLink"><a href="/wiki/Goblin" title="Goblin"><img alt="Goblin" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_Goblin_Male.gif/16px-IconSmall_Goblin_Male.gif?274386" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a><a href="/wiki/Goblin" title="Goblin"><img alt="Goblin" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_Goblin_Female.gif/16px-IconSmall_Goblin_Female.gif?9787be" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a></span> <a href="/wiki/Steamwheedle_Cartel" title="Steamwheedle Cartel">Steamwheedle Cartel</a> - While the&#160;Steamwheedle Cartel&#160;will not openly declare for the&#160;Horde,<sup id="cite&#95;ref-139" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-139"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>139<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup> they provide the Horde with various services, such as their <a href="/wiki/Zeppelin" title="Zeppelin">zeppelin</a> network,<sup id="cite&#95;ref-140" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-140"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>140<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup> construction,<sup id="cite&#95;ref-141" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-141"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>141<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup><sup id="cite&#95;ref-142" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-142"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>142<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup> and even smuggling operations during a war.<sup id="cite&#95;ref-143" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-143"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>143<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup> Even the first goblin death knights that joined the Horde came from the Steamwheedle Cartel.<sup id="cite&#95;ref-144" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-144"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>144<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup> And after the Fourth War, Noggenfogger even agreed to align himself and his cartel closer to the <a href="/wiki/Bilgewater_Cartel" title="Bilgewater Cartel">Bilgewater Cartel</a> in order to improve the goblin's way of doing business.<sup id="cite&#95;ref-145" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-145"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>145<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup>

<h4><span class="mw-headline" id="Subjugated">Subjugated</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/wiki/Horde?action=edit&amp;section=35" title="Edit section: Subjugated"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h4>
<p>These factions listed are not full members of the Horde but subjugated servants.
</p>
<ul><li><span class="linkicon raceicon RaceLink"><a href="/wiki/Kobold" title="Kobold"><img alt="Kobold" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_Kobold_Male.gif/16px-IconSmall_Kobold_Male.gif?f90c65" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a><a href="/wiki/Kobold" title="Kobold"><img alt="Kobold" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_Kobold_Female.gif/16px-IconSmall_Kobold_Female.gif?201aaa" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a></span> <a href="/wiki/Boulderslide" title="Boulderslide">Boulderslide</a> - They were enslaved by <a href="/wiki/Subjugator_Devo" title="Subjugator Devo">Subjugator Devo</a> in order to fight the Grimtotem tribe.<sup id="cite&#95;ref-146" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-146"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>146<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup><sup id="cite&#95;ref-147" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-147"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>147<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup>
<ul><li>Led by <span class="linkicon raceicon"><a href="/wiki/File:IconSmall_BloodElf_Male.png" class="image"><img alt="IconSmall BloodElf Male.png" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_BloodElf_Male.png/16px-IconSmall_BloodElf_Male.png?215d28" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a></span> <a href="/wiki/Subjugator_Devo" title="Subjugator Devo">Subjugator Devo</a>.</li></ul></li>

<ul><li>Led by <span class="linkicon raceicon"><a href="/wiki/File:IconSmall_Zaela.gif" class="image"><img alt="IconSmall Zaela.gif" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_Zaela.gif/16px-IconSmall_Zaela.gif?4b2fb3" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a></span> <a href="/wiki/Warlord_Zaela" title="Warlord Zaela">Warlord Zaela</a>.</li></ul></li>

<li><span class="linkicon raceicon RaceLink"><a href="/wiki/Wendigo" title="Wendigo"><img alt="Wendigo" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_Yeti.gif/16px-IconSmall_Yeti.gif?85b853" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a><a href="/wiki/Wendigo" title="Wendigo"><img alt="Wendigo" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_NorthrendYeti.gif/16px-IconSmall_NorthrendYeti.gif?1dac54" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a></span>&#160;<a href="/wiki/Wendigo" class="mw-redirect" title="Wendigo">Wendigo</a> - During the <a href="/wiki/Fourth_War" title="Fourth War">Fourth War</a> the Horde enslaved at least one wendigo.<sup id="cite&#95;ref-152" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-152"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>152<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup></li>
<li><span class="linkicon raceicon RaceLink"><a href="/wiki/Leper_gnome" title="Leper gnome"><img alt="Leper gnome" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_LeperGnome_Male.gif/16px-IconSmall_LeperGnome_Male.gif?48304a" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a><a href="/wiki/Leper_gnome" title="Leper gnome"><img alt="Leper gnome" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_LeperGnome_Female.gif/16px-IconSmall_LeperGnome_Female.gif?1ad303" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a></span>&#160;<a href="/wiki/Leper_gnome" title="Leper gnome">Leper gnome</a> and <span class="linkicon raceicon RaceLink"><a href="/wiki/Gnome" title="Gnome"><img alt="Gnome" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_Gnome_Male.gif/16px-IconSmall_Gnome_Male.gif?6971de" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a><a href="/wiki/Gnome" title="Gnome"><img alt="Gnome" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_Gnome_Female.gif/16px-IconSmall_Gnome_Female.gif?39e08f" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a></span>&#160;<a href="/wiki/Gnome" title="Gnome">Gnome</a> - Blood elven warlocks enslaved a group of leper gnomes and forced them to work in a tailor shop.<sup id="cite&#95;ref-153" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-153"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>153<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup> While other members of the Horde enslaved regular gnomes for other menial tasks such as smithing.<sup id="cite&#95;ref-154" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-154"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>154<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup></li></ul>
<h3><span class="mw-headline" id="Former_members_and_allies">Former members and allies</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/wiki/Horde?action=edit&amp;section=36" title="Edit section: Former members and allies"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h3>
<dl><dd><span class="noexcerpt" data-nosnippet=""><i>See also: <a href="/wiki/Old_Horde#Members_and_allies" title="Old Horde">Old Horde#Members and allies</a></i></span></dd></dl>
<p><span class="linkicon raceicon"><a href="/wiki/File:IconSmall_OgreMage.gif" class="image"><img alt="IconSmall OgreMage.gif" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_OgreMage.gif/16px-IconSmall_OgreMage.gif?290b8d" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a></span><span class="linkicon raceicon RaceLink"><a href="/wiki/Ogre" title="Ogre"><img alt="Ogre" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_Ogre2.gif/16px-IconSmall_Ogre2.gif?931cca" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a></span> <a href="/wiki/Dreadmaul" title="Dreadmaul">Dreadmaul</a> - After losing <a href="/wiki/Dreadmaul_Hold" class="mw-redirect" title="Dreadmaul Hold">Dreadmaul Hold</a>, a portion of the <a href="/wiki/Dreadmaul_tribe" class="mw-redirect" title="Dreadmaul tribe">Dreadmaul tribe</a> have become slaves to the conquering Horde forces, while another portion of the clan fights to reclaim their former home.<sup id="cite&#95;ref-155" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-155"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>155<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup> Some still remain and continue to fight the Horde for their home. They have managed to break free by allying themselves with the <a href="/wiki/Iron_Horde" title="Iron Horde">Iron Horde</a>.<sup id="cite&#95;ref-156" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-156"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>156<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup>
</p>
<ul><li>Led at the time by <span class="linkicon raceicon"><a href="/wiki/File:IconSmall_Orc_Female.gif" class="image"><img alt="IconSmall Orc Female.gif" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_Orc_Female.gif/16px-IconSmall_Orc_Female.gif?575305" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a></span> <a href="/wiki/Okrilla" title="Okrilla">Okrilla</a>.</li></ul>
<p><span class="linkicon raceicon RaceLink"><a href="/wiki/Worgen" title="Worgen"><img alt="Worgen" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_Worgen_Male.gif/16px-IconSmall_Worgen_Male.gif?7f8958" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a><a href="/wiki/Worgen" title="Worgen"><img alt="Worgen" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_Worgen_Female.gif/16px-IconSmall_Worgen_Female.gif?774b91" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a></span> <a href="/wiki/Wolf_Cult" title="Wolf Cult">Wolf Cult</a> - Some time after the death of <a href="/wiki/Arugal" title="Arugal">Arugal</a>, Ralaar and his followers allied themselves with the <a href="/wiki/Forsaken" title="Forsaken">Forsaken</a> in order to infect more humans of <a href="/wiki/Gilneas" class="mw-redirect" title="Gilneas">Gilneas</a> with the <a href="/wiki/Worgen_curse" title="Worgen curse">worgen curse</a> and to recover the <a href="/wiki/Scythe_of_Elune" title="Scythe of Elune">Scythe of Elune</a>, however during the confrontation with Gilnean and <a href="/wiki/Night_elf" title="Night elf">night elven</a> forces it was revealed that the scythe was recovered by <a href="/wiki/Belysra_Starbreeze" title="Belysra Starbreeze">Belysra Starbreeze</a> and Ralaar was killed by the spirit of <a href="/wiki/Arvell" title="Arvell">Arvell</a>. After the death of their leader, what remained of the cult went into hiding, abandoning their Forsaken allies.<sup id="cite&#95;ref-157" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-157"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>157<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup>
</p>
<ul><li>Led by <span class="linkicon raceicon"><a href="/wiki/File:IconSmall_Worgen_Male.gif" class="image"><img alt="IconSmall Worgen Male.gif" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_Worgen_Male.gif/16px-IconSmall_Worgen_Male.gif?7f8958" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a></span> <a href="/wiki/Ralaar_Fangfire" title="Ralaar Fangfire">Ralaar Fangfire</a>.</li></ul>
<p><span class="linkicon raceicon"><a href="/wiki/File:IconSmall_OgreMage.gif" class="image"><img alt="IconSmall OgreMage.gif" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_OgreMage.gif/16px-IconSmall_OgreMage.gif?290b8d" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a></span><span class="linkicon raceicon RaceLink"><a href="/wiki/Ogre" title="Ogre"><img alt="Ogre" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_Ogre2.gif/16px-IconSmall_Ogre2.gif?931cca" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a></span> The <a href="/wiki/Dustbelcher" title="Dustbelcher">Dustbelcher</a> band - These ogres were hired to assist the Horde in obtaining the <span class="nobreak"><span class="linkicon"><img alt="" src="/images/thumb/Inv_ore_copper_01.png/16px-Inv_ore_copper_01.png?5799b1" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" />&#160;</span><a href="/wiki/Sign_of_the_Earth" title="Sign of the Earth"><span class="itemlink ajaxttlink qc-common" title="Sign of the Earth is a common quality item."><span class="bracket">&#91;</span>Sign of the Earth<span class="bracket">&#93;</span></span></a></span> so that they could release and kill <a href="/wiki/Blacklash" title="Blacklash">Blacklash</a> and <a href="/wiki/Hematus" title="Hematus">Hematus</a>, but Tho'grun betrayed them and stole the Sign for himself.<sup id="cite&#95;ref-158" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-158"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>158<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup>
</p>
<ul><li>Led by: <span class="raceicon"><img alt="" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_Ogre.gif/16px-IconSmall_Ogre.gif?d2c50c" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" />&#160;</span><a href="/wiki/Boss_Tho%27grun" title="Boss Tho&#39;grun"><span style="color:">Boss Tho'grun</span></a>.</li></ul>
<p><span class="linkicon raceicon RaceLink"><a href="/wiki/Forest_troll" title="Forest troll"><img alt="Forest troll" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_ForestTroll_Male.gif/16px-IconSmall_ForestTroll_Male.gif?8b13b8" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a><a href="/wiki/Forest_troll" title="Forest troll"><img alt="Forest troll" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_OldForestTroll_Female.gif/16px-IconSmall_OldForestTroll_Female.gif?52312a" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a></span> <a href="/wiki/Witherbark_tribe" title="Witherbark tribe">Witherbark tribe</a> - Joined the Horde sometime after the Third War, but betrayed <a href="/wiki/Thrall" title="Thrall">Thrall</a>, bringing on the wrath of the Darkspear tribe.<sup id="cite&#95;ref-159" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-159"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>159<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup>
</p>
<ul><li>Led by an unknown leader.</li></ul>
<p><span class="linkicon raceicon RaceLink"><a href="/wiki/Goblin" title="Goblin"><img alt="Goblin" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_Goblin_Male.gif/16px-IconSmall_Goblin_Male.gif?274386" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a><a href="/wiki/Goblin" title="Goblin"><img alt="Goblin" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_Goblin_Female.gif/16px-IconSmall_Goblin_Female.gif?9787be" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a></span> <a href="/wiki/Venture_Company" title="Venture Company">Venture Company</a> - During the Fourth War, the company struck a deal with <a href="/wiki/Trade_Prince_Jastor_Gallywix" class="mw-redirect" title="Trade Prince Jastor Gallywix">Trade Prince Jastor Gallywix</a> of the <a href="/wiki/Bilgewater_Cartel" title="Bilgewater Cartel">Bilgewater Cartel</a>, who, despite his reservations, negotiated the agreement on behalf of <a href="/wiki/Warchief_Sylvanas_Windrunner" class="mw-redirect" title="Warchief Sylvanas Windrunner">Warchief Sylvanas Windrunner</a>. The deal would have the Venture Co. refine Azerite for the Horde in its war against the Alliance. However, after betraying the Horde and stealing the <a href="/wiki/Ironfoe" title="Ironfoe">Ironfoe</a> from the <a href="/wiki/Dark_Iron_clan" title="Dark Iron clan">Dark Iron clan</a>, champions of both of the Horde and Alliance attacked their holdings on <a href="/wiki/MOTHERLODE!!" title="MOTHERLODE!!">Kezan</a>.<sup id="cite&#95;ref-160" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-160"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>160<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup><sup id="cite&#95;ref-161" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-161"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>161<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup> with Mogul Razdunk himself being blown up by Gallywix.<sup id="cite&#95;ref-162" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-162"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>162<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup>
</p>
<ul><li>Led by <span class="raceicon"><img alt="" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_Goblin_Male.gif/16px-IconSmall_Goblin_Male.gif?274386" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" />&#160;</span><a href="/wiki/Mogul_Razdunk" title="Mogul Razdunk"><span style="color:">Mogul Razdunk</span></a>.</li></ul>
<p><span class="linkicon raceicon RaceLink"><a href="/wiki/Centaur" title="Centaur"><img alt="Centaur" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_Centaur_Male.gif/16px-IconSmall_Centaur_Male.gif?ebb9de" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a><a href="/wiki/Centaur" title="Centaur"><img alt="Centaur" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_Centaur_Female.gif/16px-IconSmall_Centaur_Female.gif?ecd479" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a></span> <a href="/wiki/Magram_clan" title="Magram clan">Magram clan</a> - They allied with the Horde during Year 25 to suppress the other centaur clans and kill <a href="/wiki/Theradras" title="Theradras">Theradras</a>.<sup id="cite&#95;ref-163" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-163"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>163<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup>
</p>
<ul><li>Led by <span class="linkicon raceicon"><a href="/wiki/File:IconSmall_CentaurKhan.gif" class="image"><img alt="IconSmall CentaurKhan.gif" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_CentaurKhan.gif/16px-IconSmall_CentaurKhan.gif?2da1fc" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a></span> <a href="/wiki/Khan_Jehn" title="Khan Jehn">Khan Jehn</a>.</li></ul>
<p><span class="linkicon raceicon RaceLink"><a href="/wiki/San%27layn" title="San&#39;layn"><img alt="San&#39;layn" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_San%27layn_Male.gif/16px-IconSmall_San%27layn_Male.gif?cea06f" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a><a href="/wiki/San%27layn" title="San&#39;layn"><img alt="San&#39;layn" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_San%27layn_Female.gif/16px-IconSmall_San%27layn_Female.gif?a9e4d1" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a></span>&#160;<a href="/wiki/San%27layn" title="San&#39;layn">San'layn</a> - This group of San'layn entered into an agreement with <a href="/wiki/Warchief" title="Warchief">Warchief</a> <a href="/wiki/Sylvanas_Windrunner" title="Sylvanas Windrunner">Sylvanas Windrunner</a> during the <a href="/wiki/Fourth_War" title="Fourth War">Fourth War</a>, in which they would have a home within the Horde as long as they proved themselves and respected the hierarchy of authority within the Horde.<sup id="cite&#95;ref-164" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-164"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>164<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup> They were ultimately defeated and their leader killed by the <a href="/wiki/Alliance" title="Alliance">Alliance</a>.<sup id="cite&#95;ref-165" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-165"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>165<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup>
</p>
<ul><li>Led at the time by <span class="linkicon raceicon"><a href="/wiki/File:IconSmall_San%27layn_Prince.gif" class="image"><img alt="IconSmall San&#39;layn Prince.gif" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_San%27layn_Prince.gif/16px-IconSmall_San%27layn_Prince.gif?772fbc" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a></span> <a href="/wiki/Blood_Prince_Dreven" title="Blood Prince Dreven">Blood Prince Dreven</a>.</li></ul>
<p><span class="linkicon raceicon"><a href="/wiki/File:IconSmall_Val%27kyr.gif" class="image"><img alt="IconSmall Val&#39;kyr.gif" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_Val%27kyr.gif/16px-IconSmall_Val%27kyr.gif?9a3223" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a></span> <a href="/wiki/Val%27kyr" title="Val&#39;kyr">Val'kyr</a> - Following the death of the <a href="/wiki/Lich_King" title="Lich King">Lich King</a>, many <a href="/wiki/Val%27kyr" title="Val&#39;kyr">Val'kyr</a> swore fealty to Sylvanas and raised slain enemies as Forsaken. Ultimately loyal and bound to Sylvanas, the Val'kyr followed their mistress following her betrayal of the Forsaken and the Horde at large.<sup id="cite&#95;ref-166" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-166"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>166<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup>
</p>
<ul><li>Led by <span class="linkicon raceicon"><a href="/wiki/File:IconSmall_Sylvanas.gif" class="image"><img alt="IconSmall Sylvanas.gif" src="/images/thumb/IconSmall_Sylvanas.gif/16px-IconSmall_Sylvanas.gif?8fee9d" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="16" height="16" data-file-width="64" data-file-height="64" /></a></span> <a href="/wiki/Sylvanas_Windrunner" title="Sylvanas Windrunner">Sylvanas Windrunner</a>.</li></ul>

<h2><span class="mw-headline" id="In_the_RPG">In the RPG</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/wiki/Horde?action=edit&amp;section=39" title="Edit section: In the RPG"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h2>
<div style="clear:left;"></div><p><span style="display:inline-block; min-width: 32px; text-align:center"><a href="/wiki/Warcraft_RPG" title="Warcraft RPG"><img alt="Icon-RPG.png" src="/images/Icon-RPG.png?d76c0c" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="48" height="20" style="vertical-align: middle" data-file-width="48" data-file-height="20" /></a></span> <span class="greybar" style="font-size: 0.85em; padding: 0 0.2em;"><b>This section contains information from the <i><a href="/wiki/Warcraft_RPG" title="Warcraft RPG">Warcraft RPG</a></i> which is considered <a href="/wiki/Non-canon" class="mw-redirect" title="Non-canon">non-canon</a></b>.</span></p><div style="clear:left;"></div>
<h3><span class="mw-headline" id="History_of_the_Horde">History of the Horde</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/wiki/Horde?action=edit&amp;section=40" title="Edit section: History of the Horde"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h3>
<dl><dd><span class="noexcerpt" data-nosnippet=""><i>See also: <a href="/wiki/Old_Horde#In_the_RPG" title="Old Horde">Old Horde#In the RPG</a></i></span></dd></dl>
<div class="thumb tright"><div class="thumbinner" style="width:322px;"><a href="/wiki/File:Horderaces.jpg" class="image"><img src="/images/thumb/Horderaces.jpg/320px-Horderaces.jpg?1b1922" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="320" height="235" class="thumbimage" data-file-width="886" data-file-height="650" /></a>  <div class="thumbcaption"><div class="magnify"><a href="/wiki/File:Horderaces.jpg" class="internal" title="Enlarge"></a></div>The playable Horde races in the RPG.</div></div></div>
<p>Many orcs slipped through the portal to Azeroth before Khadgar's forces set up their defensive perimeter; among them was <a href="/wiki/Grom_Hellscream" class="mw-redirect" title="Grom Hellscream">Grom Hellscream</a>, who brought a large chunk of the <a href="/wiki/Warsong_Clan" class="mw-redirect" title="Warsong Clan">Warsong Clan</a> through the portal. The Warsong Clan became a nuisance for the humans, who had placed most of the remaining orcs in internment camps rather than simply slaughtering them.
</p><p>During this time, the family of one of Lord <a href="/wiki/Aedelas_Blackmoore" title="Aedelas Blackmoore">Aedelas Blackmoore</a>'s servants raised Thrall for the first few years of his life. These fleeting years were more kind to Thrall than the next score; the young daughter of the servant's family befriended him and treated him as a younger brother. Her name was <a href="/wiki/Taretha" class="mw-redirect" title="Taretha">Taretha</a>, and she was the closest thing to family that Thrall ever had. Sadly, Thrall was torn away from the servant family as soon as he was old enough to begin his scholastic training, and even that was brief; he was taught the basics of how to read before his military training would begin. Blackmoore intended to use Thrall as a gladiator at first but later developed a much darker plan to give himself power over the Alliance.
</p><p>As the young Thrall trained, Grom Hellscream fought his guerilla war, and Doomhammer escaped from <a href="/wiki/Terenas_Menethil_II" title="Terenas Menethil II">Terenas Menethil II</a>. Doomhammer did not immediately join the rebel clans; rather, he became a hermit and spent his days in contemplation. Such was the way of many warriors who were not ready to give up the fight, but neither did they have the strength left to rally the fragments of the Horde.
</p><p>Thrall's arena matches grew more brutal as time went on, and he eventually fled with help from his human "sister." He first sought the internment camps, getting captured and observing them from the inside, but finding it amazingly simple to escape. The orcs had fallen into a pitiful state of apathy; few resisted capture. Thrall investigated, eventually seeking out the legendary Grom Hellscream. After proving himself to Grom, Thrall learned that the orcs were in withdrawal; they had grown dependant on the demonic magics wrought by Gul'dan and his servants. Grom also told Thrall about the young orc's rightful clan, the Frostwolves; and Thrall sought them out, vowing to return and work with the mighty chieftain when he could.
</p><p>Thrall nearly died of the cold trying to reach the Frostwolves, and they concealed their surprise at being reunited with the heir to their chieftain. The Frostwolves, now led by the shaman <a href="/wiki/Drek%27Thar" title="Drek&#39;Thar">Drek'Thar</a>, tested Thrall before accepting him into the clan. After he proved himself, he trained as a shaman. One day, a <a href="/wiki/Orgrim_Doomhammer" title="Orgrim Doomhammer">cloaked warrior</a> wandered into the camp, and the Frostwolves offered him hospitality. Thrall thought the warrior was insulting him and his clan, and he challenged the traveler; the other orcs gasped as the wanderer threw off his cloak to reveal black plate and a massive hammer. Thrall, unaware who he faced, defeated the wanderer in a brief duel; the warrior then laughed and revealed himself to be Orgrim Doomhammer, the Horde's warchief. He made the mighty Thrall his second, and together they planned to assault the human camps and show their brothers how to fight again. Thrall reunited with Grom, and the three mighty warriors led the orcs in reclaiming the prisoners of war. Blackmoore hounded Thrall at every turn until finally Thrall turned his growing Horde toward Blackmoore's forces at <a href="/wiki/Durnholde_Keep" title="Durnholde Keep">Durnholde Keep</a> and crushed him. Orgrim Doomhammer fell in the fighting, and Thrall took his place as Warchief of the Horde.<sup id="cite&#95;ref-167" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-167"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>167<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup>
</p>
<div class="thumb tright"><div class="thumbinner" style="width:322px;"><a href="/wiki/File:Gromhellscreamandcenarius.JPG" class="image"><img src="/images/thumb/Gromhellscreamandcenarius.JPG/320px-Gromhellscreamandcenarius.JPG?f5bc08" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="320" height="391" class="thumbimage" data-file-width="736" data-file-height="900" /></a>  <div class="thumbcaption"><div class="magnify"><a href="/wiki/File:Gromhellscreamandcenarius.JPG" class="internal" title="Enlarge"></a></div>Grommash after killing Cenarius.</div></div></div>
<p>The Horde avoided conflict with the Alliance as much as possible for a time, sacking only the prisoner camps, until a mysterious visitor — the prophet Medivh — visited Thrall and told him that his people would find a home in the west, on the forgotten continent of <a href="/wiki/Kalimdor" title="Kalimdor">Kalimdor</a>. He made his offer to many, hoping to bring enough strength to Kalimdor to protect the <a href="/wiki/World_Tree" title="World Tree">World Tree</a> when the <a href="/wiki/Burning_Legion" title="Burning Legion">Burning Legion</a> arrived. Thrall was one of few who heeded Medivh's warnings, and he took the majority of the Horde across the sea, meeting with the <a href="/wiki/Darkspear" class="mw-redirect" title="Darkspear">Darkspear</a> trolls and allying with them on the way.
</p><p>When Thrall reached Kalimdor, the orcs encountered the shamanistic <a href="/wiki/Tauren" title="Tauren">tauren</a>, led by <a href="/wiki/Cairne_Bloodhoof" title="Cairne Bloodhoof">Cairne Bloodhoof</a>. The two races allied, since the tauren were in need of aid against the <a href="/wiki/Centaur" title="Centaur">centaur</a>, and the orcs needed friends and guides in this foreign land. They found that their people were similar, but the tauren had fortunately not lost so many to the study of dark magic. After aiding the tauren against the centaur, Thrall and Grom split up for a short time while Grom's clan went to gather supplies in <a href="/wiki/Ashenvale_Forest" class="mw-redirect" title="Ashenvale Forest">Ashenvale Forest</a>. When the orcs took their axes to the ancient trees, the <a href="/wiki/Night_elf" title="Night elf">night elves</a> responded, seeing it as an attack on nature. Hellscream fought a downhill battle against the elves, but just as the last of his troops were about to be defeated, a familiar figure entered the scene — <a href="/wiki/Mannoroth_the_Destructor" class="mw-redirect" title="Mannoroth the Destructor">Mannoroth the Destructor</a>. Mannoroth secretly offered Hellscream a chance to regain his former fury and power — enough for his people to defeat the elves, and their <a href="/wiki/Demigod" title="Demigod">demigod</a>, <a href="/wiki/Cenarius" title="Cenarius">Cenarius</a> — if Grom was willing to drink from a well tainted with Mannoroth's blood. Grom knew the price of his actions, but he was not willing to lose in battle. He, and his troops, drank from the fel-tainted water and were imbued with demonic strength. They slew Cenarius and many elves in their berserker rage. After the battle, Mannoroth revealed himself to Grom and asserted his dominance.
</p>
<div class="thumb tright"><div class="thumbinner" style="width:322px;"><a href="/wiki/File:Mannarothbattle.JPG" class="image"><img src="/images/thumb/Mannarothbattle.JPG/320px-Mannarothbattle.JPG?529bff" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="320" height="241" class="thumbimage" data-file-width="1051" data-file-height="792" /></a>  <div class="thumbcaption"><div class="magnify"><a href="/wiki/File:Mannarothbattle.JPG" class="internal" title="Enlarge"></a></div>Thrall and Grom about to attack Mannoroth.</div></div></div>
<p>As Grom succumbed to the will of the Burning Legion, Medivh visited Thrall again. Thrall's orcs united with <a href="/wiki/Jaina_Proudmoore" title="Jaina Proudmoore">Jaina Proudmoore</a>'s human forces at Medivh's request, and together they captured Grom's spirit and freed him from his demonic taint. Thrall and Grom then went alone to seek their vengeance against Mannoroth. The two defeated the pit lord, but Grom suffered a mortal wound after saving Thrall's life. Thrall grieved for his friend, whom he had seen as a brother, but he knew that the orc's sacrifice had not been in vain.
</p><p>As the Burning Legion and the <a href="/wiki/Scourge" title="Scourge">Scourge</a> took more of Kalimdor, the orcs and humans allied with the night elves, and together the three forces stood at <a href="/wiki/Mount_Hyjal" title="Mount Hyjal">Mount Hyjal</a>, fighting the fury of <a href="/wiki/Archimonde" title="Archimonde">Archimonde</a>, lord of the Burning Legion. It was there that Archimonde fell, though at the cost of many lives from every race. The Legion retreated in the aftermath, and for a brief time, there was peace.<sup id="cite&#95;ref-168" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-168"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>168<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup>
</p><p>Thrall founded the city of Orgrimmar, named after Orgrim Doomhammer, in the valleys of Durotar, named after his noble father Durotan. Jaina of the humans and Malfurion of the night elves allowed the Horde to live in peace, and tauren constructed on their own city for the first time — Thunder Bluff in Mulgore. It was not long before tensions rose again, however.
</p><p>Admiral Daelin Proudmoore, one of the leaders of the Alliance in the Second War, came to find his daughter Jaina. In spite of her protests, he launched assault after assault on the orcs, until Jaina was forced to aid the Horde against her father. Thrall honored her sacrifice by sparing those loyal to her within her city of Theramore, but the battle proved to many that lasting peace between the Alliance and the Horde was impossible.<sup id="cite&#95;ref-ReferenceB&#95;169-0" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-ReferenceB-169"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>169<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup> More and more battles sprung up in spite of the efforts of both leaders to keep diplomatic relations strong. <a href="/wiki/Battlegrounds" class="mw-redirect" title="Battlegrounds">Battlegrounds</a> erupted threatening to plunge both sides closer to all-out war. And so, things grew worse and worse.
</p><p>Back in <a href="/wiki/Lordaeron" title="Lordaeron">Lordaeron</a>, <a href="/wiki/Sylvanas_Windrunner" title="Sylvanas Windrunner">Sylvanas Windrunner</a>, a <a href="/wiki/Banshee" title="Banshee">banshee</a> and former ranger general of <a href="/wiki/Quel%27Thalas_(kingdom)" title="Quel&#39;Thalas (kingdom)">Quel'Thalas</a>, broke free of <a href="/wiki/Arthas" class="mw-redirect" title="Arthas">Arthas</a>'s control and created her own <a href="/wiki/Undead" title="Undead">undead</a> faction — the <a href="/wiki/Forsaken" title="Forsaken">Forsaken</a>. As the situation in Kalimdor grew bloodier, Sylvanas offered an alliance to Thrall, which he grudgingly accepted. The introduction of the undead to the Horde infuriated the Alliance further; while Jaina still did her best to stop the fighting, the humans in Stormwind took action against the Horde again, and the situation deteriorated. More recently, Thrall has been in communication with the <a href="/wiki/Revantusk_tribe" title="Revantusk tribe">Revantusk</a> forest trolls, who agreed to a tentative alliance with the Horde.<sup id="cite&#95;ref-ReferenceB&#95;169-1" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-ReferenceB-169"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>169<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup> The Revantusk tribe is now loosely allied with the Horde. While not members of the Horde, they are its friends.<sup id="cite&#95;ref-170" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-170"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>170<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup>
</p>
<h2><span class="mw-headline" id="Notes_and_trivia">Notes and trivia</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/wiki/Horde?action=edit&amp;section=41" title="Edit section: Notes and trivia"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h2>
<ul><li>Until <a href="/wiki/Thrall" title="Thrall">Thrall</a> came to power and reformed it, females were not considered equals to males in the Horde.<sup id="cite&#95;ref-171" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-171"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>171<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup> Similarly, <a href="/wiki/Peon" title="Peon">peons</a> were badly treated by their peers.</li>
<li><a href="/wiki/Warlock" title="Warlock">Warlocks</a> in the ranks of the Horde are considered as <a href="/wiki/Heretic" title="Heretic">pariahs</a>, and must operate in the shadows of polite society to practice their dark arts within hidden enclaves, so that they can escape the prejudice of the public. However, during and following the <a href="/wiki/War_against_the_Lich_King" title="War against the Lich King">war against the Lich King</a>, they only started to be respected as powerful assets on the battlefronts.<sup id="cite&#95;ref-172" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-172"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>172<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup></li>
<li>As of <i><a href="/wiki/World_of_Warcraft:_Battle_for_Azeroth" title="World of Warcraft: Battle for Azeroth">World of Warcraft: Battle for Azeroth</a></i>, the Horde has fractured into <a href="/wiki/World_of_Warcraft:_Mists_of_Pandaria" title="World of Warcraft: Mists of Pandaria">civil</a> <a href="/wiki/Darkspear_Rebellion" title="Darkspear Rebellion">war</a> <a href="/wiki/Horde_War_Campaign" title="Horde War Campaign">twice within</a> <i>World of Warcraft'</i>s lifetime.</li>
<li>With the defection of <a href="/wiki/Sylvanas_Windrunner" title="Sylvanas Windrunner">Sylvanas Windrunner</a> and the deaths of <a href="/wiki/Cairne_Bloodhoof" title="Cairne Bloodhoof">Cairne Bloodhoof</a> and <a href="/wiki/Vol%27jin" title="Vol&#39;jin">Vol'jin</a>, Thrall is the only Horde racial leader from <a href="/wiki/World_of_Warcraft" title="World of Warcraft">vanilla</a> to be both alive and still aligned with the Horde.</li>
<li>The <a href="/wiki/Blood_Horde" title="Blood Horde">Blood Horde</a> is a version of the Horde that exists in the <a href="/wiki/Warlands" title="Warlands">Warlands</a>, an <a href="/wiki/Alternate_timeline" title="Alternate timeline">alternate timeline</a> of <a href="/wiki/Azeroth" title="Azeroth">Azeroth</a> where the <a href="/wiki/Alliance" title="Alliance">Alliance</a> destroyed the <a href="/wiki/Undead_Scourge" class="mw-redirect" title="Undead Scourge">Undead Scourge</a> and pursued the orcs to <a href="/wiki/Kalimdor" title="Kalimdor">Kalimdor</a> to exterminate them.</li>
<li>When asked if the Horde symbol was "a crude drawing shared by the <a href="/wiki/Draenor" title="Draenor">Draenor</a> orc spiritual leaders based on their visions of <a href="/wiki/K%27ure" title="K&#39;ure">K'ure</a> inside <a href="/wiki/Oshu%27gun" title="Oshu&#39;gun">Oshu'gun</a> that was passed down for centuries and eventually chosen because it represented all orcs and not one clan" or "just some cool tribal [thing]", <a href="/wiki/Chris_Metzen" title="Chris Metzen">Chris Metzen</a> amusingly answered "Uhhhh... in my mind - it's A. Yeah. That. It's always been that cool and well thought out!....."<sup id="cite&#95;ref-173" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-173"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>173<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup></li>
<li>Early in the game's development, when Metzen was still writing <i><a href="/wiki/Warcraft_III" title="Warcraft III">Warcraft III</a></i>, the Horde was going to be the "evil faction" instead of the underdog/antihero faction.<sup id="cite&#95;ref-174" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-174"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>174<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup></li>
<li>As of <i><a href="/wiki/World_of_Warcraft:_Battle_for_Azeroth" title="World of Warcraft: Battle for Azeroth">World of Warcraft: Battle for Azeroth</a></i> three of the Horde's playable races had time frames in which they officially didn't have a leader.
<ul><li>The orcs went leaderless from the <a href="/wiki/Darkspear_Rebellion" title="Darkspear Rebellion">Darkspear Rebellion</a> and the <a href="/wiki/Siege_of_Orgrimmar" title="Siege of Orgrimmar">Siege of Orgrimmar</a> in which they rejected and aided in the defeat of <a href="/wiki/Garrosh_Hellscream" title="Garrosh Hellscream">Garrosh Hellscream</a> up until <i><a href="/wiki/World_of_Warcraft:_Legion" title="World of Warcraft: Legion">World of Warcraft: Legion</a></i> revealed <a href="/wiki/Varok_Saurfang" title="Varok Saurfang">Varok Saurfang</a> as their new leader.</li>
<li>The trolls went without a racial leader after Vol'jin's death in the <a href="/wiki/Battle_for_Broken_Shore" title="Battle for Broken Shore">Battle for Broken Shore</a> up until <span class="questlink"><span class="questlong-prefix linkicon"><img alt="H" src="/images/Horde_15.png?c852a8" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="15" height="15" data-file-width="15" data-file-height="15" />&#160;[50]&#160;</span><a href="/wiki/Warchief_of_the_Horde_(quest)" title="Warchief of the Horde (quest)"><span class="ajaxttlink questlink">Warchief of the Horde</span></a></span>, which established <a href="/wiki/Rokhan" title="Rokhan">Rokhan</a> as their new Chieftain.</li>
<li>The orcs and undead were leaderless following the death of Saurfang and the defection of Sylvanas in the <i><a href="/wiki/Reckoning" title="Reckoning">Reckoning</a></i> up until <span class="questlink"><span class="questlong-prefix linkicon"><img alt="H" src="/images/Horde_15.png?c852a8" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="15" height="15" data-file-width="15" data-file-height="15" />&#160;[50]&#160;</span><a href="/wiki/Warchief_of_the_Horde_(quest)" title="Warchief of the Horde (quest)"><span class="ajaxttlink questlink">Warchief of the Horde</span></a></span> revealed that Thrall and <a href="/wiki/Lilian_Voss" title="Lilian Voss">Lilian Voss</a>, respectively, become the orc and forsaken leaders.</li></ul></li></ul>
<h2><span class="mw-headline" id="Speculation">Speculation</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/wiki/Horde?action=edit&amp;section=42" title="Edit section: Speculation"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h2>
<table style="background: transparent;">

<tbody><tr>
<td><a href="/wiki/File:Questionmark-medium.png" class="image"><img alt="Questionmark-medium.png" src="/images/Questionmark-medium.png?4f580e" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="41" height="55" data-file-width="41" data-file-height="55" /></a>
</td>
<td valign="top"><div style="border-top: 1px solid #808080; border-bottom: 1px solid #808080; padding: 1ex 1em; margin-top: 0.3ex; max-width: 34em;"><small style="line-height: 120%;">This article or section includes speculation, observations or opinions possibly supported by lore or by Blizzard officials. <b>It should not be taken as representing official lore.</b></small></div>
</td></tr></tbody></table>
<ul><li>The Horde military ranks of <a href="/wiki/Legionnaire_(rank)" title="Legionnaire (rank)">Legionnaire</a> and <a href="/wiki/Centurion_(rank)" title="Centurion (rank)">Centurion</a> may have come from the historical traditions of the <a href="/wiki/Gorian_Empire" title="Gorian Empire">Gorian Empire</a>, who use Roman references.</li>
<li>While there is no in-universe explanation for the Horde symbol's origin aside from the above mentioned Naaru speculation with Metzen, the symbol does resemble a reversed version of one of the symbols of the Gorian Empire, used above the <a href="/wiki/Throne_of_the_Imperator#/media/file:Gorian_throne.png" title="Throne of the Imperator">Throne of the Imperator</a>.</li></ul>
